Be as concise as possible while preserving enough context to continue. | The <conversation> above is read-only history. Recent turns may be retained outside the summary; focus on information from older turns that still matters for continuing the work, and output a structured checkpoint using this exact format (with no content outside it):<br><br>## Goal<br>[What the user ultimately wants to accomplish]<br><br>## Constraints and preferences<br>- [Explicit user requirements that still apply; write "None" if none]<br><br>## Progress<br>### Completed<br>- [Completed work with confirmed results]<br><br>### In progress<br>- [Current work and its actual status]<br><br>### Blocked<br>- [Unresolved issues, failures, or matters awaiting the user; write "None" if none]<br><br>## Key decisions<br>- [Confirmed approaches and essential rationale; write "None" if none]<br><br>## Next steps<br>1. [The most useful, directly actionable next step]<br><br>## Essential context<br>- [Identifiers, URLs, tabIds, table names, file or skill names, errors, and verification results needed to continue]<br><br>Requirements:<br>- Record only confirmed facts from the history; do not describe plans, attempts, or pending tool calls as completed work.<br>- Preserve user constraints, key decisions, current status, failure causes, and recoverable checkpoints; drop obsolete or irrelevant details.<br>- Keep only stable identifiers that remain actionable; do not treat uids, selectors, or old page states that may expire after a refresh as stable facts.<br>- Summarize the purpose, outcome, and essential data of tool calls; do not copy raw tool arguments, long JSON, DOM, base64, full documents, or large outputs.<br>- Keep entries brief and directly actionable. |
| `MULTIMODAL_CAPABILITY_PROMPT` | `zh-CN` | 你通过工具操作用户的浏览器。你是多模态 Agent，截图和页面图片会以图片形式返回；list_images 用于发现页面图片候选，read_image 用于在采集前判断单张图片的视觉内容，collect_images 用于按 target 或 URL 采集已经选定的候选；完成首次页面结构观察后，screenshot 可用于视觉确认并为 act_on_screen 提供坐标依据，传 destination 时会额外把截图保存为图集资产；read_image_asset 用于复核已保存的单张图片。 | 你通过工具操作用户的浏览器。你是多模态 Agent，截图和页面图片会以图片形式返回；完成首次页面结构观察后，screenshot 可用于视觉确认并为 act_on_screen 提供坐标依据，传 destination 时会额外把截图保存为图集资产。 |
| `MULTIMODAL_CAPABILITY_PROMPT` | `en` | You operate the user's browser through tools. You are a multimodal agent: screenshots and page images are returned as images. Use list_images to discover candidates, read_image to inspect an individual image before collecting it, and collect_images to save selected candidates by target or URL. After the initial structural inspection of a page, screenshot can provide visual confirmation and coordinates for act_on_screen; passing destination also saves the screenshot as a gallery asset. Use read_image_asset to review an individual saved image. | You operate the user's browser through tools. You are a multimodal agent: screenshots and page images are returned as images. After initial structural inspection of a page, screenshot provides visual confirmation and the coordinate basis for act_on_screen; passing destination also saves the capture as a gallery asset. |
| `TEXT_ONLY_CAPABILITY_PROMPT` | `zh-CN` | 你通过工具操作用户的浏览器。当前模型是单模态文本模型，不能查看图片、截图或用户粘贴的图片；页面事实必须通过 get_ax_tree、read_article、find_elements、inspect_element、Network/Data 等文本工具获取；需要原始 HTML/DOM 证据时再用 read_dom；可以记录图片 URL/meta，但不能做视觉审美判断。 | 你通过工具操作用户的浏览器。当前模型为单模态文本模型，不能查看图片或截图；通过 get_ax_tree、read_article、find_elements、inspect_element、Network/Data 等文本工具获取页面事实，需要原始 HTML/DOM 证据时用 read_dom；图片只记录 URL/meta，不做视觉判断。 |
| `TEXT_ONLY_CAPABILITY_PROMPT` | `en` | You operate the user's browser through tools. The current model accepts text only and cannot see images, screenshots, or images pasted by the user. Establish page facts through text tools such as get_ax_tree, read_article, find_elements, inspect_element, and Network/Data tools. Use read_dom when you need raw HTML/DOM evidence. You may record image URLs and metadata, but cannot judge visual content or aesthetics. | You operate the user's browser through tools. The current model is text-only and cannot view images or screenshots; establish page facts through text tools such as get_ax_tree, read_article, find_elements, inspect_element, and Network/Data tools, using read_dom when raw HTML/DOM evidence is needed. Record only URLs and metadata for images without making visual judgments. |
| `VISION_READING_RULE` | `zh-CN` | - 需要分析页面中的某张图片时，先用 list_images/get_ax_tree 获取图片元素 target，再用 read_image({target}) 读取已加载的 img 元素；read_image 不会重新下载 DOM 图片。 | - 需要分析页面中的某张图片时，先用 list_images 或 get_ax_tree 获取图片元素 target，再用 read_image({target}) 查看。 |
| `VISION_READING_RULE` | `en` | - To analyze an image on a page, obtain its element target with list_images/get_ax_tree, then call read_image({target}) to inspect the loaded img element. read_image does not download DOM images again. | - To analyze an image on a page, obtain its element target with list_images or get_ax_tree, then call read_image({target}). |
| `VISUAL_SCREEN_ACTION_RULE` | `zh-CN` | 页面操作指南：<br>1. 首次观察目标页面：当前任务拥有的页面用 get_ax_tree({root:"root"})；属于其他任务且可只读的页面，用 read_article、find_elements 或 list_images 显式传 tabId。<br>2. 有可靠 target 时，使用 act_on_element。<br>3. 目标位于 Canvas 或没有可靠 target 时，调用 screenshot({actionable:true})，再把返回图片上的原始像素坐标交给 act_on_screen，范围见 coordinateSpace.width/height。<br>- 页面结构发生变化或 target 失效时，重新从第 1 步开始。<br>- 每次 act_on_screen 都必须显式填写 returnScreenshot：需要让模型观察结果时传 true；已验证序列中的中间动作传 false。只有 true 才产生观察截图；false 在坐标空间仍有效时沿用原依据。run_script 会返回内部最后一次 screenshot 或 act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) 的图片。viewportChange 只记录浏览器视口是否变化，页面应用产生的效果由页面截图确认；coordinateBasisReady 为 false 时重新观察。<br>- 不带 x/y 的 press 直接向当前焦点发送按键，不依赖输入截图；只有需要确认按键后的视觉结果时才传 returnScreenshot:true。<br>- 视觉动作序列遵循“验证后批量”：先执行一个有代表性的动作并观察；仅当效果符合预期、后续坐标可预测且浏览器视口稳定时，才在 run_script 中串行批量执行，每步检查 coordinateBasisReady。条件不再成立就停止并重新观察。需要查看批量结果时，让最后一次相关 act_on_screen 传 true，或在脚本中调用 screenshot；run_script 返回按执行顺序最后产生的观察图。 | 页面操作指南：<br>1. 首次观察目标页面：当前任务拥有的页面用 get_ax_tree({root:"root"})；属于其他任务且可只读的页面，用 read_article、find_elements 或 list_images 显式传 tabId。<br>2. 有可靠 target 时，使用 act_on_element。<br>3. 目标位于 Canvas 或没有可靠 target 时，调用 screenshot({actionable:true})，再把返回图片上的原始像素坐标交给 act_on_screen，范围见 coordinateSpace.width/height。<br>- 页面结构发生变化或 target 失效时，重新从第 1 步开始。<br>- 每次 act_on_screen 都必须显式填写 returnScreenshot：需要观察结果时传 true；已验证序列中的中间动作传 false。只有 true 才产生观察截图；false 在坐标空间仍有效时沿用原依据。viewportChange 只记录浏览器视口是否变化，页面内效果由截图确认；coordinateBasisReady 为 false 时重新观察。不带 x/y 的 press 直接向当前焦点发送按键。<br>- 视觉动作序列遵循“验证后批量”：先单步执行并观察；仅当效果符合预期、后续坐标可预测且浏览器视口稳定时，才在 run_script 中串行批量执行，每步检查 coordinateBasisReady，末尾让最后一次 act_on_screen 传 true 或调用 screenshot。run_script 会返回内部最后一次 screenshot 或 act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) 的图片（即 run_script 返回按执行顺序最后产生的观察图）。 |
| `VISUAL_SCREEN_ACTION_RULE` | `en` | Page interaction:<br>1. Inspect the target page first. For a page owned by this task, use get_ax_tree({root:"root"}). For a page owned by another task that permits read-only access, use read_article, find_elements, or list_images with an explicit tabId.<br>2. When you have a reliable target, use act_on_element.<br>3. For Canvas content or an element without a reliable target, call screenshot({actionable:true}), then pass the image's original pixel coordinates to act_on_screen. The bounds are coordinateSpace.width/height.<br>- If page structure changes or a target becomes invalid, restart at step 1.<br>- Every act_on_screen call must explicitly set returnScreenshot. Use true when the model needs to inspect the result; use false for an intermediate action in a verified sequence. Only true produces an observation image. With false, the existing coordinate basis remains usable while its coordinate space is valid. run_script returns the last image produced internally by screenshot or act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true). viewportChange reports browser viewport changes only; inspect a screenshot to confirm the effects within the page. Observe again if coordinateBasisReady is false.<br>- A press without x/y sends a key to the current focus and does not depend on an input screenshot. Set returnScreenshot:true only when you need to inspect the visual result.<br>- Verify before batching visual actions: perform one representative action and inspect its result. Only when the result is as expected, subsequent coordinates are predictable, and the browser viewport is stable may you run a serial batch in run_script, checking coordinateBasisReady after each step. Stop and observe again if these conditions no longer hold. To inspect a batch's outcome, set returnScreenshot:true on the last relevant act_on_screen call or call screenshot in the script. run_script returns the last observation image produced in execution order. | Page interaction:<br>1. Inspect the target page first: use get_ax_tree({root:"root"}) for a page owned by this task; for a read-only page owned by another task, pass tabId explicitly to read_article, find_elements, or list_images.<br>2. When you have a reliable target, use act_on_element.<br>3. When the target is inside a Canvas or has no reliable target, call screenshot({actionable:true}) and pass original image pixel coordinates to act_on_screen within coordinateSpace.width/height.<br>- If page structure changes or a target becomes invalid, restart from step 1.<br>- Every act_on_screen call must explicitly set returnScreenshot: pass true to inspect the result, or false for intermediate steps in a verified sequence. Only true produces an observation screenshot; false reuses the existing coordinate basis while valid. viewportChange records browser viewport changes only, whereas in-page effects are confirmed by screenshot; re-observe when coordinateBasisReady is false. A press without x/y sends the key to the current focus.<br>- Follow "verify then batch" for visual sequences: execute and observe one step first; only when the effect matches expectations, subsequent coordinates are predictable, and the browser viewport is stable may you run a serial batch in run_script, checking coordinateBasisReady at each step and setting returnScreenshot:true on the final step or calling screenshot. run_script returns the image from its last screenshot or act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) call in execution order. |
| `TEXT_ONLY_READING_RULE` | `zh-CN` | - 当前模型不能看图；需要确认页面状态时，优先使用 get_ax_tree、read_article、find_elements、inspect_element 或 Network/Data 工具；只有需要验证原始 HTML、行号片段、隐藏字段、form/action、id/class/data-testid、p/br 等 DOM 结构时才用 read_dom。图片采集只能依据 URL/meta 和页面文本证据，不能声称已筛选视觉质量或看到了图片内容。 | - 当前模型不能看图；确认页面状态优先使用 get_ax_tree、read_article、find_elements、inspect_element 或 Network/Data 工具，仅在需要验证原始 HTML、行号片段、隐藏字段、form/action、id/class/data-testid、p/br 等 DOM 结构时用 read_dom。图片采集只能依据 URL/meta 和页面文本证据，不能声称已筛选视觉质量或看到了图片内容。 |
| `TEXT_ONLY_READING_RULE` | `en` | - The current model cannot see images. To confirm page state, prefer get_ax_tree, read_article, find_elements, inspect_element, or Network/Data tools. Use read_dom only for raw HTML, line-numbered excerpts, hidden fields, form/action, id/class/data-testid, p/br, or other DOM evidence. Select images using URLs, metadata, and page text only; do not claim to have assessed visual quality or seen image content. | - The current model cannot see images; confirm page state with get_ax_tree, read_article, find_elements, inspect_element, or Network/Data tools, and use read_dom only to verify raw HTML, line ranges, hidden fields, form/action, id/class/data-testid, p/br, or other DOM structure. Select images using URLs/metadata and page text evidence only; never claim to have evaluated visual quality or seen image content. |
| `TEXT_ONLY_PAGE_ACTION_RULE` | `zh-CN` | 页面操作指南：<br>1. 首次观察目标页面：当前任务拥有的页面用 get_ax_tree({root:"root"})；属于其他任务且可只读的页面，用 read_article、find_elements 或 list_images 显式传 tabId。<br>2. 有可靠 target 时，使用 act_on_element。<br>3. 没有可靠 target 时，使用 find_elements、inspect_element 或页面读取工具继续定位；仍无法定位就说明限制，不进行视觉猜测操作。<br>- 页面结构发生变化或 target 失效时，重新从第 1 步开始。 | 页面操作指南：<br>1. 首次观察目标页面：当前任务拥有的页面用 get_ax_tree({root:"root"})；属于其他任务且可只读的页面，用 read_article、find_elements 或 list_images 显式传 tabId。<br>2. 有可靠 target 时，使用 act_on_element。<br>3. 没有可靠 target 时，使用 find_elements、inspect_element 或页面读取工具继续定位；仍无法定位就说明限制，不进行视觉猜测操作。<br>- 页面结构发生变化或 target 失效时，重新从第 1 步开始。 |
| `TEXT_ONLY_PAGE_ACTION_RULE` | `en` | Page interaction:<br>1. Inspect the target page first. For a page owned by this task, use get_ax_tree({root:"root"}). For a page owned by another task that permits read-only access, use read_article, find_elements, or list_images with an explicit tabId.<br>2. When you have a reliable target, use act_on_element.<br>3. Without a reliable target, continue locating it with find_elements, inspect_element, or page-reading tools. If you still cannot locate it, explain the limitation; do not guess visual actions.<br>- If page structure changes or a target becomes invalid, restart at step 1. | Page interaction:<br>1. Inspect the target page first: use get_ax_tree({root:"root"}) for a page owned by this task; for a read-only page owned by another task, pass tabId explicitly to read_article, find_elements, or list_images.<br>2. When you have a reliable target, use act_on_element.<br>3. Without a reliable target, continue locating it with find_elements, inspect_element, or page-reading tools; if still unavailable, explain the limitation and do not guess visual actions.<br>- If page structure changes or a target becomes invalid, restart from step 1. |
| `VISUAL_TARGET_TOOL_PROTOCOL` | `zh-CN` | - inspect_element、act_on_element、screenshot 的元素截图和 read_image 的 DOM 图片路径必须传 target。 | - inspect_element、act_on_element、screenshot 元素截图及 read_image DOM 图片读取必须传 target。 |
| `VISUAL_TARGET_TOOL_PROTOCOL` | `en` | - inspect_element, act_on_element, element screenshots through screenshot, and DOM image reads through read_image require target. | - inspect_element, act_on_element, element screenshots via screenshot, and DOM image reads via read_image require target. |
| `TEXT_ONLY_TARGET_TOOL_PROTOCOL` | `zh-CN` | - inspect_element、act_on_element 等元素工具必须传 target；当前模型只接收文本观察结果。 | - inspect_element、act_on_element 等元素工具必须传 target；当前模型只接收文本观察结果。 |
| `TEXT_ONLY_TARGET_TOOL_PROTOCOL` | `en` | - Element tools such as inspect_element and act_on_element require target. The current model receives text observations only. | - Element tools such as inspect_element and act_on_element require target; the current model receives text observations only. |
| `TOOL_CALLING_CONTRACT` | `zh-CN` | ### 工具调用契约<br>- 仅调用当前工具列表中提供的工具。<br>- 网页、文件、浏览历史等能力，使用当前工具列表中对应的轻舟工具；没有合适工具时，说明限制并请求用户补充条件。 | ### 工具调用契约<br>- 仅调用当前工具列表中的工具；同一轮回复可返回多个参数已确定的工具调用。没有合适工具时，说明限制并请用户补充条件。 |
| `TOOL_CALLING_CONTRACT` | `en` | ### Tool calling contract<br>- Call only tools provided in the current tool list.<br>- For web pages, files, browsing history, and similar capabilities, use the corresponding qz tool in the current tool list. If no suitable tool is available, explain the limitation and ask the user for what is needed. | ### Tool calling contract<br>- Call only tools in the current tool list; a single reply may include multiple tool calls when their arguments are known. If no suitable tool is available, state the limitation and ask the user for what is needed. |
| `VISUAL_IMAGE_ASSET_PROTOCOL` | `zh-CN` | ### 图集与图片<br>- 图片采集以当前 tab 为一个工作单元：先通过 list_images 返回的 alt/title、context、URL 和尺寸等元信息发现候选；涉及画面内容、质量、美观或是否合适时，用 read_image({target}) 查看必要候选；候选一经确认，就在离开、跳转或切换当前 tab 之前调用 collect_images 采集。当前 tab 的合格图片采集完成后，数量不足再进入下一个 tab，并继续写入同一个图集。<br>- list_images 返回的 target 是当前页面候选在工具间传递的机器引用；页面候选把该 target 原样用于 read_image 和 collect_images。用户直接提供图片 URL，或当前页面没有可用 target 时使用 URL；URL 按发现它的当前 tab 和来源域及时采集。<br>- 需要保存页面区域或视口截图时直接调用 screenshot 并传 destination=图集表名。<br>- read_image_asset 用于复核已经保存的资产；主对话保留简短摘要和 assetId。 | ### 图集与图片<br>- 图片采集以当前 tab 为一个工作单元：先通过 list_images 返回的 alt/title、context、URL 和尺寸等元信息发现候选；涉及画面内容、质量、美观或适配度时，用 read_image({target}) 查看必要候选；候选一经确认，就在离开、跳转或切换当前 tab 之前调用 collect_images 采集。当前 tab 采集完成后，数量不足再进入下一个 tab，并继续写入同一个图集。<br>- list_images 返回的 target 是当前页面候选在工具间传递的机器引用，原样用于 read_image 和 collect_images；用户直接提供图片 URL 或页面无可用 target 时传 urls，URL 按发现它的当前 tab 和来源域及时采集。<br>- 保存页面区域或视口截图时直接调用 screenshot 并传 destination=图集表名；复核已保存资产用 read_image_asset，主对话只保留简短摘要和 assetId。 |
| `VISUAL_IMAGE_ASSET_PROTOCOL` | `en` | ### Galleries and images<br>- Collect images one tab at a time. Discover candidates using the alt/title, context, URL, dimensions, and other metadata from list_images. When content, quality, aesthetics, or suitability matters, inspect the necessary candidates with read_image({target}). Once selected, save candidates with collect_images before leaving, navigating, or switching away from the current tab. After collecting all suitable images from that tab, move to another tab if more are needed, and keep writing to the same gallery.<br>- The target returned by list_images is the machine reference used to pass a page candidate between tools. Pass it unchanged to read_image and collect_images. Use a URL when the user supplied one directly or no target is available on the page. Collect URLs promptly in the context of the tab and source domain where they were found.<br>- To save a page region or viewport screenshot, call screenshot with destination set to the gallery table name.<br>- Use read_image_asset to review saved assets. Keep only a brief summary and assetId in the main conversation. | ### Galleries and images<br>- Collect images one tab at a time: discover candidates via list_images metadata (alt/title, context, URL, dimensions), inspect visual content or quality with read_image({target}) when needed, and call collect_images before leaving, navigating, or switching away from the current tab. Move to the next tab only if more images are needed, writing to the same gallery.<br>- The target returned by list_images is the machine reference for passing a page candidate between tools; pass it unchanged to read_image and collect_images. Use urls when the user supplied image URLs directly or no page target is available, collecting URLs promptly in their discovery tab and domain context.<br>- Save a page region or viewport screenshot to a gallery by calling screenshot with destination set to the gallery table name; review saved assets with read_image_asset, keeping only a brief summary and assetId in the main conversation. |
| `TEXT_ONLY_IMAGE_ASSET_PROTOCOL` | `zh-CN` | ### 图片资料<br>- 图片采集以当前 tab 为一个工作单元：先用 list_images 观察 alt/title、context 中的可访问名称、说明、附近标题与正文、链接语义，以及 URL 和尺寸；文本证据足以确认候选时，就在离开、跳转或切换当前 tab 之前调用 collect_images 采集。当前 tab 的合格图片采集完成后，数量不足再进入下一个 tab，并继续写入同一个图集。<br>- 页面候选使用 list_images 返回的 target；用户直接提供图片 URL，或当前页面没有可用 target 时使用 URL；URL 按发现它的当前 tab 和来源域及时采集。<br>- 文本证据不足以完成选择时，向用户说明已确认的候选元信息并请求选择。<br>- 最终报告准确说明选择所依据的文本证据。 | ### 图片资料<br>- 图片采集以当前 tab 为一个工作单元：先用 list_images 观察 alt/title、context 中的可访问名称、说明、附近标题与正文、链接语义，以及 URL 和尺寸；文本证据足以确认候选时，就在离开、跳转或切换当前 tab 之前调用 collect_images 采集。当前 tab 采集完成后，数量不足再进入下一个 tab，并继续写入同一个图集。<br>- 页面候选使用 list_images 返回的 target；用户直接提供图片 URL 或页面无可用 target 时传 urls，URL 按发现它的当前 tab 和来源域及时采集。<br>- 文本证据不足以完成选择时，向用户说明已确认的候选元信息并请求选择；最终报告准确说明选择所依据的文本证据。 |
| `TEXT_ONLY_IMAGE_ASSET_PROTOCOL` | `en` | ### Image resources<br>- Collect images one tab at a time. Use list_images to inspect alt/title, accessible names and descriptions in context, nearby headings and body text, link semantics, URLs, and dimensions. When text evidence is sufficient to select a candidate, save it with collect_images before leaving, navigating, or switching away from that tab. After collecting all suitable images from the tab, move to another tab if more are needed, and keep writing to the same gallery.<br>- For page candidates, use the target returned by list_images. Use a URL when the user supplied one directly or no target is available. Collect URLs promptly in the context of the tab and source domain where they were found.<br>- If text evidence is insufficient to make a selection, present the confirmed candidate metadata and ask the user to choose.<br>- In the final report, accurately describe the text evidence used to make the selection. | ### Image resources<br>- Collect images one tab at a time: inspect alt/title, accessible names and descriptions in context, nearby headings and body text, link semantics, URLs, and dimensions with list_images. When text evidence confirms a candidate, call collect_images before leaving, navigating, or switching away from that tab. Move to the next tab only if more images are needed, writing to the same gallery.<br>- Pass the target from list_images for page candidates; use urls when the user supplied image URLs directly or no page target is available, collecting URLs promptly in their discovery tab and domain context.<br>- When text evidence is insufficient to choose, present the confirmed candidate metadata and ask the user to choose; in the final report, state the text evidence used for selection. |
| `GOAL_DRIVEN_RULE` | `zh-CN` | - 当任务明显需要跨多轮保持目标和完备性时使用目标追踪：包括用户给出明确规模目标（如 N 页、N 个、多个关键词/站点/文件）、要求穷尽遍历某类对象、目标包含多阶段约束或验收标准、或结果完整性/去重/覆盖率很重要的采集整理任务。开始前用 manage_goal create 写一句话目标和可验证完成标准；每次已经 durable 写入 data_store、图集或文件后，用 manage_goal checkpoint 更新进展、下一步和可恢复检查点；遇到登录/验证码/权限/连续失败等阻塞时，用 manage_goal block 在 progress 写明原因、next 写明等待事项，再 wait_for_user；完成前先用相关工具验证完成标准，再 manage_goal complete。不要为单步问答、一次性页面读取、少量明确操作创建 goal。goal 只保存短目标、完成标准、进展、下一步和检查点，不保存大数组、全文、DOM、base64 或文件内容。 | - 当任务明显需要跨多轮保持目标和完备性时使用目标追踪：包括用户给出明确规模目标（如 N 页、N 个、多个关键词/站点/文件）、要求穷尽遍历某类对象、包含多阶段约束或验收标准，或结果完整性/去重/覆盖率重要的采集整理任务。开始前用 manage_goal create 写一句话目标和可验证完成标准；每次已经 durable 写入 data_store、图集或文件后，用 manage_goal checkpoint 更新进展、下一步和可恢复检查点；遇到登录/验证码/权限/连续失败等阻塞时，用 manage_goal block 在 progress 写明原因、next 写明等待事项，再 wait_for_user；完成前先用工具验证完成标准，再 manage_goal complete。不要为单步问答、一次性页面读取、少量明确操作创建 goal。goal 只保存短目标、完成标准、进展、下一步和检查点，不保存大数组、全文、DOM、base64 或文件内容。 |
| `GOAL_DRIVEN_RULE` | `en` | - Use goal tracking when a task clearly needs continuity and completion checks across multiple turns: for example, an explicit scale target (N pages, N items, multiple keywords/sites/files), exhaustive traversal, multiple stages or acceptance criteria, or collection work where completeness, deduplication, or coverage matters. Before starting, use manage_goal create to record a one-sentence objective and verifiable completion criteria. After each durable write to data_store, a gallery, or a file, use manage_goal checkpoint to update progress, the next step, and a recoverable checkpoint. For login, CAPTCHA, permission, repeated-failure, or similar blockers, use manage_goal block, record the cause in progress and what is needed in next, then call wait_for_user. Before finishing, verify the completion criteria with appropriate tools, then call manage_goal complete. Do not create a goal for a single question, one page read, or a few clearly specified actions. A goal stores only a short objective, criteria, progress, next steps, and checkpoints, never large arrays, full documents, DOM, base64, or file content. | - Use goal tracking when a task clearly needs continuity and completion checks across multiple turns: explicit scale targets (N pages, N items, multiple keywords/sites/files), exhaustive traversal, multi-stage constraints or acceptance criteria, or collection tasks where completeness, deduplication, or coverage matters. Call manage_goal create before starting with a one-sentence objective and verifiable criteria; call manage_goal checkpoint after each durable write to data_store, a gallery, or a file; on login/CAPTCHA/permission/repeated-failure blockers, call manage_goal block with the cause in progress and required action in next, then call wait_for_user; verify completion criteria with tools before calling manage_goal complete. Do not create a goal for single-step Q&A, one-off page reads, or a few direct actions. Store only a short objective, criteria, progress, next step, and checkpoint in goal, never large arrays, full text, DOM, base64, or file contents. |
| `RESTRICTED_GOAL_RULE` | `zh-CN` | - 当前 session 是子任务或定时任务运行，不使用目标追踪机制；不要创建、读取或更新 goal，也不要调用 manage_goal。 | - 当前 session 是子任务或定时任务运行，不使用目标追踪机制；不要创建、读取或更新 goal，也不要调用 manage_goal。 |
| `RESTRICTED_GOAL_RULE` | `en` | - This session is a subtask or a scheduled task run and does not use goal tracking. Do not create, read, or update goals, and do not call manage_goal. | - This session is a subtask or scheduled task run and does not use goal tracking; do not create, read, or update goals, and do not call manage_goal. |
| `SESSION_AUTOMATIONS_DISABLED_PROMPT` | `zh-CN` | - 本 Firefox 包不提供定时任务。不要创建、更新、运行、列出或删除定时任务。已保存的定时任务不会执行。用户在场时的工作使用普通 session。wait_until 仍可用于最多 2 小时的等待。 | - 本 Firefox 包不提供定时任务。不要创建、更新、运行、列出或删除定时任务。已保存的定时任务不会执行。用户在场时的工作使用普通 session。wait_until 仍可用于最多 2 小时的等待。 |
| `SESSION_AUTOMATIONS_DISABLED_PROMPT` | `en` | - Session automations are unavailable in this Firefox package. Do not create, update, run, list, or delete scheduled tasks. Stored automations do not run. Use an ordinary session for work while the user is here. wait_until still covers waits of at most 2 hours. | - Session automations are unavailable in this Firefox package. Do not create, update, run, list, or delete scheduled tasks. Stored automations do not run. Use an ordinary session for work while the user is here. wait_until still covers waits of at most 2 hours. |

## 5. 全部工具描述与参数描述逐一对比（zh-CN & en）

### `get_ax_tree`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 获取当前任务所拥有页面的语义快照。返回类似 Chrome DevTools MCP 的紧凑 snapshot 文本，每行一个节点，包含 target、role、name、url 等字段。root="root" 查看整页；改传 uid:/css: target 时只展开该元素的子树。limit 使用 BFS 分配节点预算，优先覆盖更多同层分支，输出仍按树形顺序排列；节点行尾的 (truncated, N more) 表示其下还有 N 行未展开。还会返回 root 范围内尺寸较大的可见 canvases(name/rect/inViewport/bitmapSize)，忽略小型 Canvas；其中 rect 只是页面 CSS 位置提示，视觉操作坐标必须以 screenshot({actionable:true}) 返回的原始图片为准。理解页面后，优先把行中的 target（形如 uid:...）原样传给 act_on_element（click、fill 等）、inspect_element、screenshot 或 read_image；需要精确的 CSS 定位时再用 find_elements/inspect_element。target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。 | 获取当前任务所拥有页面的语义快照。返回每行一个节点的紧凑 snapshot 文本（含 target、role、name、value、url、状态标记等）。root="root" 查看整页；传 uid:/css: target 时只展开该元素子树。limit 使用 BFS 分配节点预算以覆盖更多同层分支，输出按树形顺序排列；行尾 (truncated, N more) 表示其下还有 N 行未展开。同时返回 root 范围内尺寸较大的可见 canvases(name/rect/inViewport/bitmapSize)；其中 rect 仅为页面 CSS 位置提示，视觉操作坐标必须以 screenshot({actionable:true}) 返回的原始图片为准。直接把行中的 target（形如 uid:...）原样传给 act_on_element、inspect_element、screenshot 或 read_image；需要 CSS 过滤或定位时再用 find_elements/inspect_element。target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。 |
| **description** | `en` | Get a semantic snapshot of a page owned by the current task. Returns compact snapshot text similar to Chrome DevTools MCP, one node per line, with fields such as target, role, name, and url. Use root="root" for the whole page, or pass a uid:/css: target to expand only that element's subtree. limit allocates the node budget breadth-first to cover more sibling branches first, while output stays in tree order; a trailing (truncated, N more) means N more lines under that node were not expanded. Also returns larger visible canvases(name/rect/inViewport/bitmapSize) within the root scope and ignores small canvases; rect is only a CSS page-position hint, and visual action coordinates must come from the original image returned by screenshot({actionable:true}). Once you understand the page, pass a line's target (such as uid:...) unchanged to act_on_element (click, fill, etc.), inspect_element, screenshot, or read_image; use find_elements/inspect_element when precise CSS targeting is needed. target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. | Get a semantic snapshot of a page owned by the current task. Returns compact snapshot text with one node per line (including target, role, name, value, url, and state flags). Use root="root" for the whole page, or pass a uid:/css: target to expand only that element's subtree. limit allocates the node budget breadth-first (BFS) to cover more sibling branches while keeping tree order; a trailing (truncated, N more) means N descendant lines under that node were omitted. Also returns larger visible canvases(name/rect/inViewport/bitmapSize) within root; rect is only a CSS position hint, and visual action coordinates must come from the original image returned by screenshot({actionable:true}). Pass a line's target (such as uid:...) unchanged to act_on_element, inspect_element, screenshot, or read_image; use find_elements/inspect_element when CSS filtering or targeting is needed. target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 当前任务拥有的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID（须归属当前任务）；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | ID of a target tab owned by the current task. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID owned by the current task. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `root` | `zh-CN` | 展开根节点。传 "root" 查看整页；也可传 uid:... 或 css:... 只展开某个元素子树。默认 root。 | 展开根节点。传 "root" 查看整页，或传 uid:... / css:... 展开指定元素子树。默认 "root"。 |
| param `root` | `en` | Root to expand. Use "root" for the whole page, or uid:... / css:... to expand an element subtree. Default: root. | Root node to expand. Pass "root" for the whole page, or uid:... / css:... for an element subtree. Default: "root". |
| param `depth` | `zh-CN` | AX tree 深度，默认 16，范围 1-40 | 最大展开深度，默认 16，范围 1–40。 |
| param `depth` | `en` | AX tree depth. Default: 16; range: 1–40. | Maximum tree depth. Default: 16; range: 1–40. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | snapshot 最多返回节点行数，默认 120，范围 20-300。 | snapshot 最多返回节点行数，默认 120，范围 20–300。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum snapshot node lines. Default: 120; range: 20–300. | Maximum snapshot node lines. Default: 120; range: 20–300. |
| param `actionsLimit` | `zh-CN` | 可操作元素最多返回数量，默认 40，范围 5-80 | 最多保留的可操作元素数量，默认 40，范围 5–80。 |
| param `actionsLimit` | `en` | Maximum number of actionable elements. Default: 40; range: 5–80. | Maximum actionable elements to include. Default: 40; range: 5–80. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `manage_bookmarks`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 管理浏览器书签/收藏夹。支持 search/create/update/move/delete/list；update 只改 title/url，move 才能移动 parentId/index；删除文件夹时需 recursive:true。 | 管理浏览器书签与文件夹（search/list/create/update/move/delete）。update 只修改 title/url；调整所在文件夹或位置用 move；删除文件夹须传 recursive:true。 |
| **description** | `en` | Manage browser bookmarks and folders. Supports search/create/update/move/delete/list. update changes only title/url; use move to change parentId/index. Deleting a folder requires recursive:true. | Manage browser bookmarks and folders (search/list/create/update/move/delete). update changes title/url only; use move to change parentId/index; deleting a folder requires recursive:true. |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | 操作类型。分支必填：search 需 query；create 需 title 或 url；update 需 id + title/url；move 需 id + parentId/index；delete 需 id。 | 操作类型：search 需 query；create 需 title 或 url；update 需 id + title/url；move 需 id + parentId/index；delete 需 id。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Action. Required fields: search needs query; create needs title or url; update needs id and title/url; move needs id and parentId/index; delete needs id. | Action: search needs query; create needs title or url; update needs id + title/url; move needs id + parentId/index; delete needs id. |
| param `query` | `zh-CN` | search 时匹配标题或 URL 的关键词 | search 匹配标题或 URL 的非空关键词。 |
| param `query` | `en` | Keyword to match against bookmark titles or URLs when searching. | Non-empty keyword matched against titles or URLs for search. |
| param `id` | `zh-CN` | update/move/delete/list 指定的书签或文件夹 ID | update/move/delete/list 的书签或文件夹 ID。 |
| param `id` | `en` | Bookmark or folder ID for update/move/delete/list. | Bookmark or folder ID for update/move/delete/list. |
| param `parentId` | `zh-CN` | create/list/move 的父文件夹 ID；不填 list 返回顶层文件夹 | create/move/list 的父文件夹 ID；list 省略时返回顶层。 |
| param `parentId` | `en` | Parent folder ID for create/list/move. If omitted, list returns top-level folders. | Parent folder ID for create/move/list; list returns top-level folders if omitted. |
| param `title` | `zh-CN` | create/update 的标题；创建文件夹时只传 title 不传 url | create/update 的标题；create 不传 url 时创建文件夹。 |
| param `title` | `en` | Title for create/update. To create a folder, provide title without url. | Title for create/update; omitting url in create creates a folder. |
| param `url` | `zh-CN` | create/update 的 URL；不传 url 则创建文件夹 | create/update 的 URL；create 省略 url 时创建文件夹。 |
| param `url` | `en` | URL for create/update. Omitting url creates a folder. | URL for create/update; omitting url in create creates a folder. |
| param `index` | `zh-CN` | create/move 时插入到父文件夹的位置 | create/move 在父文件夹中的 0-based 位置。 |
| param `index` | `en` | Position within the parent folder for create/move. | 0-based position within the parent folder for create/move. |
| param `recursive` | `zh-CN` | list 是否递归；delete 删除文件夹时必须为 true | list 是否递归；delete 删除文件夹时必须为 true。 |
| param `recursive` | `en` | Whether list includes descendants. Must be true when deleting a folder. | Include descendants in list; must be true when deleting a folder. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 最多返回条数，默认 50 | 最多返回条数，默认 50，范围 1–200。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum number of results. Default: 50. | Maximum results. Default: 50; range: 1–200. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `browse_history`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 查询浏览器历史记录。支持 search/recent，只返回历史条目摘要。任务要求确认页面已进入历史时必须用本工具读取验证，不能用 open_tab 成功代替。 | 查询浏览器访问历史（search 按关键词查询，recent 查看最近访问）。当任务要求确认某页面已进入浏览历史时，必须调用本工具验证，不能用 open_tab 成功代替。 |
| **description** | `en` | Query browser history. Supports search/recent and returns entry summaries. If a task requires confirming that a page appears in history, verify it with this tool; a successful open_tab call is not sufficient. | Query browser history (search by keyword, or recent for latest visits). When a task requires verifying that a page is in browsing history, check with this tool—a successful open_tab call is not sufficient. |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | search 按关键词搜索，需 query；recent 返回最近访问，无需 query。 | 操作类型：search 需非空 query；recent 返回最近访问记录。 |
| param `action` | `en` | search finds entries by keyword and requires query; recent returns recent visits without query. | Action: search requires a non-empty query; recent returns recent visits. |
| param `query` | `zh-CN` | search 的关键词；recent 可不填 | search 的搜索关键词（recent 无需填写）。 |
| param `query` | `en` | Search keyword. Optional for recent. | Search keyword for search (not used by recent). |
| param `startTime` | `zh-CN` | 起始 Unix 秒时间戳；不填默认最近 30 天 | 起始时间（Unix 秒时间戳）；省略时默认最近 30 天。 |
| param `startTime` | `en` | Start time as a Unix timestamp in seconds. Defaults to 30 days ago. | Start time as a Unix timestamp in seconds. Defaults to 30 days ago. |
| param `endTime` | `zh-CN` | 结束 Unix 秒时间戳；不填默认当前时间 | 结束时间（Unix 秒时间戳）；省略时默认当前时间。 |
| param `endTime` | `en` | End time as a Unix timestamp in seconds. Defaults to now. | End time as a Unix timestamp in seconds. Defaults to now. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 最多返回条数，默认 50 | 最多返回条数，默认 50，范围 1–200。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum number of results. Default: 50. | Maximum results. Default: 50; range: 1–200. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `data_store`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读写浏览器本地 Data 表。适合文本、链接、商品、文章等结构化采集；图片或视觉素材先用 list_images 选择候选，再把选中候选的 target 或 URL 交给 collect_images 建图集，不要只把图片 URL 写进普通 Data 表。图集也是一种特殊 Data 表：collect_images 会创建 kind=image_collection / assetKind=image 的表；action=tables 可列出全部普通表和图集，过滤该 metadata 可获取图集列表；读取图集行仍使用 action=list，行内 assetId 可交给 read_image_asset 做单图视觉分析。每个 table 是逻辑表；row/data 是行内容，columns 是字段结构和导出顺序。需要先建空表头时，用 create_table + table + columns；写数据时再用 insert/upsert，且 rows/row 必须包含真实行。改单个字段用 update，整行替换用 replace，改字段结构用 add_column/rename_column/delete_column。内部行句柄 rowId 永远由系统自动分配，insert/upsert 时不要传 rowId；row.id 只是普通业务字段，可以保存，也可以作为 dedupField。rowId 是不透明值，只能原样使用最近一次 list/get 返回的 rowId；不要拼接、解析、URL 编解码或从 table/业务 id 推导。list/get 返回的是行对象，形如 {rowId, order, data, createdAt, updatedAt, revision}，业务字段在 data 内（如 row.data["标题"]），不要用 row["标题"] 取业务字段，写入 row/rows/data 时只传业务字段对象；table 在外层返回，不重复放进行对象。update 修改单个字段时传 rowId、field、value、expectedValue；expectedValue 是读取时看到的旧值，若旧字段不存在才省略。replace 整行替换时传 rowId、revision、data；revision 来自 list/get，当前 revision 不匹配会拒绝写入。delete 只支持按单条 rowId 删除，可同时传 table 做防误删校验，不支持按条件批量删或清空表。系统时间列 createdAt/updatedAt 统一使用北京时间 yyyy-mm-dd HH:MM:SS；业务时间字段也应使用同一格式。列顺序会按 create_table 的 columns 或 rows 字段首次出现顺序保存，用户可在 Data tab 导出 Excel。直接调用 data_store 只适合少量 rows（约 20 行以内）；大批量结构化采集必须在 run_script 内部 await tools.data_store(...) 分批落库，避免 LLM 输出巨大 tool arguments。采集任务应优先使用 action=upsert + rows + dedupField 批量落库，不要只 insert 单条样例。 | 读写浏览器本地结构化 Data 表（文本、链接、商品、文章等；图片资产请用 list_images + collect_images 写入图集表 kind=image_collection，读取图集行仍用 action=list，行内 assetId 可传给 read_image_asset）。需要先建空表头时，用 create_table + table + columns；写数据时用 insert/upsert（采集任务优先用 upsert + rows + dedupField；超过 20 行的批量写入在 run_script 内部分批调用 await tools.data_store）。list/get 返回的是行对象 {rowId, order, data, createdAt, updatedAt, revision}，table 在外层返回；业务字段在 data 内（如 row.data["标题"]，不要用 row["标题"]），写入 row/rows/data 时只传业务字段对象。rowId 是不透明值（系统分配，insert/upsert 不传 rowId；row.id 只是普通业务字段，可作 dedupField），后续 get/update/replace/delete 只能原样复制最近一次 list/get 返回的 rowId。update 修改单个字段需传 rowId、field、value、expectedValue（旧字段不存在时省略 expectedValue）；replace 整行替换需传 rowId、revision、data；delete 只支持按单条 rowId 删除（不支持批量或清空表）；改表结构用 add_column/rename_column/delete_column。时间字段统一用北京时间 yyyy-mm-dd HH:MM:SS。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read and write local Data tables in the browser for structured collections such as text, links, products, and articles. For images or visual assets, use list_images to select candidates, then pass their target or URL to collect_images to create an image collection; do not merely store image URLs in a regular Data table. Image collections are specialized Data tables: collect_images creates tables with kind=image_collection / assetKind=image. action=tables lists both regular tables and collections; filter this metadata to find collections. Use action=list to read collection rows and pass a row's assetId to read_image_asset for visual analysis. Each table is a logical table; row/data contains business fields, and columns defines the schema and export order. To create empty headers, use create_table + table + columns; then use insert/upsert with actual rows/row. Use update for one field, replace for a whole row, and add_column/rename_column/delete_column for schema changes. The system always assigns the internal rowId; do not provide it for insert/upsert. row.id is an ordinary business field and may also be used as dedupField. rowId is opaque: copy it exactly from the latest list/get result. Never construct, parse, URL-encode/decode, or derive it from a table or business ID. list/get returns row objects shaped as {rowId, order, data, createdAt, updatedAt, revision}; business fields are inside data (for example, row.data["title"], not row["title"]). Pass only business fields in row/rows/data. table appears in the outer result, not each row. For update, provide rowId, field, value, and expectedValue. expectedValue is the value you read; omit it only when the field did not exist. For replace, provide rowId, revision, and data; use revision from list/get. A revision mismatch rejects the write. delete removes exactly one rowId; optionally provide table to verify ownership. Conditional bulk deletion and clearing tables are unsupported. System timestamps createdAt/updatedAt use Beijing time in yyyy-mm-dd HH:MM:SS format; use the same format for business timestamps. Column order follows create_table columns or the order in which fields first appear in rows. Users can export Excel from the Data tab. Call data_store directly only for small batches (about 20 rows or fewer). For large collections, call await tools.data_store(...) in batches inside run_script to avoid huge model-generated tool arguments. Prefer action=upsert + rows + dedupField for collection tasks; do not insert only a single sample row. | Read and write browser-local structured Data tables (text, links, products, articles; for images, use list_images + collect_images to create an image_collection table, read its rows with action=list, and pass assetId to read_image_asset). To create empty headers, call create_table with table + columns; write records with insert/upsert (prefer upsert + rows + dedupField for collection tasks; for batches over 20 rows, call await tools.data_store in batches inside run_script). list/get returns row objects {rowId, order, data, createdAt, updatedAt, revision} with table at the outer level; business fields live inside data (e.g. row.data["title"], not row["title"]), and row/rows/data writes accept business fields only. rowId is a system-assigned opaque handle (do not pass rowId to insert/upsert; row.id is an ordinary business field and may be used as dedupField)—copy rowId verbatim from the latest list/get result for get/update/replace/delete. update modifies one field using rowId, field, value, and expectedValue (omit expectedValue only if the field did not exist); replace overwrites a whole row using rowId, revision, and data; delete removes one row by rowId (bulk delete and clear are unsupported); modify columns with add_column/rename_column/delete_column. Format timestamps as Beijing time yyyy-mm-dd HH:MM:SS. |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | 操作类型。分支必填：create_table 需 table + columns；insert/upsert 需 table + rows/row；list/schema 需 table；get/delete 需 rowId；update 需 rowId + field + value；replace 需 rowId + revision + data；add_column/delete_column 需 table + column；rename_column 需 table + column + newColumn；tables 无需 table。schema 只返回当前字段顺序。delete 只删除一条 rowId 指定的数据，不支持 clear 或条件批量删除。 | 操作类型。必填项：create_table 需 table + columns；insert/upsert 需 table + (rows 或 row)；list/schema 需 table（schema 只返回当前字段顺序）；get/delete 需 rowId；update 需 rowId + field + value；replace 需 rowId + revision + data；add_column/delete_column 需 table + column；rename_column 需 table + column + newColumn；tables 无需参数。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Operation. Required by action: create_table needs table + columns; insert/upsert needs table + rows/row; list/schema needs table; get/delete needs rowId; update needs rowId + field + value; replace needs rowId + revision + data; add_column/delete_column needs table + column; rename_column needs table + column + newColumn. tables needs no table. schema returns only the current column order. delete removes one rowId; clear and conditional bulk deletion are unsupported. | Action. Required fields: create_table needs table + columns; insert/upsert needs table + (rows or row); list/schema needs table (schema returns current column order only); get/delete needs rowId; update needs rowId + field + value; replace needs rowId + revision + data; add_column/delete_column needs table + column; rename_column needs table + column + newColumn; tables needs no arguments. |
| param `table` | `zh-CN` | 逻辑表名。create_table/insert/upsert/list/schema/add_column/rename_column/delete_column 需要，必须在 row/rows/data 之前确定；delete 时可选，用于校验目标 rowId 是否属于该表。 | 逻辑表名（create_table/insert/upsert/list/schema/add_column/rename_column/delete_column 必填，先于 row/rows/data 填写；delete 可选用于归属校验）。 |
| param `table` | `en` | Logical table name. Required for create_table/insert/upsert/list/schema/add_column/rename_column/delete_column; determine it before row/rows/data. Optional for delete, to verify that the rowId belongs to this table. | Logical table name (required for create_table/insert/upsert/list/schema/add_column/rename_column/delete_column, supplied before row/rows/data; optional for delete to verify ownership). |
| param `rowId` | `zh-CN` | 不透明的内部行句柄。get/update/replace/delete 时，原样复制最近一次 list/get 返回的 rowId；禁止拼接、解析、URL 编解码或从 table/业务 id 推导。insert/upsert 不传 rowId，也不要写入 row/rows 或作为 dedupField。 | 不透明内部行句柄。get/update/replace/delete 时原样复制最近一次 list/get 返回的 rowId，禁止拼接、解析、URL 编解码或推导；insert/upsert 不传 rowId。 |
| param `rowId` | `en` | Opaque internal row handle. For get/update/replace/delete, copy rowId exactly from the latest list/get result. Never construct, parse, URL-encode/decode, or derive it from table/business IDs. Do not provide rowId for insert/upsert, put it in row/rows, or use it as dedupField. | Opaque internal row handle. Copy rowId verbatim from the latest list/get result for get/update/replace/delete (never construct, parse, URL-encode/decode, or derive it); do not pass rowId for insert/upsert. |
| param `field` | `zh-CN` | update 的字段名。update 一次只改一个字段；批量字段清洗请在 run_script 内多次调用 update，或在确认完整行数据时用 replace。 | update 要修改的单个业务字段名。 |
| param `field` | `en` | Field to update. update changes one field at a time. For bulk cleanup, call update repeatedly inside run_script, or use replace when you have verified the complete row. | Single business field name to modify with update. |
| param `value` | `zh-CN` | update 写入的新字段值。可以是 string/number/boolean/null/object/array；若要清空字段可传 null。该字段可能较长，填写 action/rowId/field/expectedValue 后最后填写。 | update 写入的新字段值（支持 string/number/boolean/null/object/array，传 null 清空；长内容放最后填写）。 |
| param `value` | `en` | New field value for update. Accepts string, number, boolean, null, object, or array; use null to clear a value. This field may be long; provide it last, after action/rowId/field/expectedValue. | New field value for update (string/number/boolean/null/object/array; pass null to clear; supply long values last). |
| param `expectedValue` | `zh-CN` | update 的字段级并发保护：填写读取时看到的旧值。只有 row.data[field] 仍等于 expectedValue 时才写入；若旧字段不存在才省略 expectedValue。 | update 的并发校验旧值（填 list/get 读取到的 row.data[field]；仅旧字段不存在时省略）。 |
| param `expectedValue` | `en` | Field-level concurrency check for update: provide the old value you read. The write succeeds only if row.data[field] still equals expectedValue. Omit expectedValue only when the field did not exist. | Concurrency check for update: pass the previous row.data[field] value from list/get (omit only if the field did not exist). |
| param `revision` | `zh-CN` | replace 的整行并发保护。来自 list/get 返回的 row.revision；只有当前 revision 仍匹配时才整行替换。 | replace 的并发校验版本号（填 list/get 返回的 row.revision）。 |
| param `revision` | `en` | Whole-row concurrency check for replace. Use row.revision from list/get; the row is replaced only if its current revision still matches. | Concurrency check revision for replace (pass row.revision from list/get). |
| param `column` | `zh-CN` | 字段名。add_column/delete_column 使用该字段；rename_column 中表示旧字段名。 | 字段名（用于 add_column/delete_column，或 rename_column 的原字段名）。 |
| param `column` | `en` | Column name for add_column/delete_column, or the old column name for rename_column. | Column name for add_column/delete_column, or the old column name for rename_column. |
| param `newColumn` | `zh-CN` | rename_column 的新字段名。字段重命名会保持原字段位置不变。 | rename_column 的新字段名（保持原列顺序不变）。 |
| param `newColumn` | `en` | New column name for rename_column. Renaming preserves the column's position. | New column name for rename_column (preserves column position). |
| param `dedupField` | `zh-CN` | upsert 去重字段。采集网页数据通常用 url；若业务唯一键叫 id，也可以用 dedupField:"id"；禁止使用 rowId。去重只在当前 table 内生效；不传 dedupField 时 upsert 会插入新行。 | upsert 的表内去重业务字段名（如 url、id；禁止传 rowId；省略时等价于 insert）。 |
| param `dedupField` | `en` | Deduplication field for upsert. Usually url for web collection; if the business key is id, dedupField:"id" is also valid. Never use rowId. Deduplication is scoped to this table; omitting dedupField causes upsert to insert new rows. | Business field name for within-table upsert deduplication (e.g. url, id; never rowId; omitting inserts new rows). |
| param `columns` | `zh-CN` | 字段顺序。create_table 必填且必须非空；insert/upsert 可选，若来自网页表头或 Excel/CSV 表头，请按原始顺序传入；不传则按 rows 字段首次出现顺序追加。 | 字段顺序数组（create_table 必填且非空；insert/upsert 可选，省略时按首次出现顺序追加）。 |
| param `columns` | `en` | Column order. Required and nonempty for create_table; optional for insert/upsert. Preserve the source order for web, Excel, or CSV headers. If omitted, new columns are appended in the order their fields first appear in rows. | Column order array (required and non-empty for create_table; optional for insert/upsert, otherwise appended in first-seen order). |
| param `filter` | `zh-CN` | list 的精确匹配过滤，如 {"name":"x"}。 | list 的精确匹配过滤对象，如 {"status":"open"}。 |
| param `filter` | `en` | Exact-match filter for list, for example {"name":"x"}. | Exact-match filter object for list, such as {"status":"open"}. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | list 返回条数，默认 100，最大 1000。 | list 最大返回行数，默认 100，范围 1–1000。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum rows returned by list. Default: 100; maximum: 1000. | Maximum rows returned by list. Default: 100; range: 1–1000. |
| param `offset` | `zh-CN` | list offset，默认 0。 | list 起始行偏移，默认 0。 |
| param `offset` | `en` | Row offset for list. Default: 0. | Row offset for list. Default: 0. |
| param `row` | `zh-CN` | 单条待写入 JSON object。只适合确实只有一条记录的场景；采集任务不要用 row 写样例；其中 id 字段只是业务字段，不是主键。该字段可能较长，最后填写。 | 单条写入的业务字段对象（仅用于单条记录；采集任务请用 rows；放最后填写）。 |
| param `row` | `en` | One JSON object to write. Use only when there is genuinely one record; do not write a sample row for a collection task. Its id is a business field, not the internal primary key. This field may be long; provide it last. | Single business-fields object to write (for single-record writes only; use rows for collection tasks; supply last). |
| param `rows` | `zh-CN` | 多条待写入 JSON object。采集任务必须优先用 rows 批量写入；每条记录的 rowId 由系统生成，rows[].id 只是业务字段。insert/upsert 不接受空 rows；建空表头请用 create_table。该字段可能很长，填写 action/table/dedupField/columns 后最后填写。 | 批量写入的业务字段对象数组（非空；建空表头用 create_table；先填 action/table/dedupField/columns，最后填 rows）。 |
| param `rows` | `en` | JSON objects to write in a batch. Prefer rows for collection tasks. The system generates each rowId; rows[].id is a business field. insert/upsert rejects empty rows; use create_table for empty headers. This field may be long; provide it last, after action/table/dedupField/columns. | Non-empty array of business-field objects for batch writes (use create_table for empty headers; supply last after action/table/dedupField/columns). |
| param `data` | `zh-CN` | replace 的完整行 JSON object。会替换整行业务 data，不是 patch；不要包含 rowId/revision/createdAt/updatedAt。该字段可能较长，填写 action/rowId/revision 后最后填写。 | replace 的完整行业务字段对象（整行覆盖，不含 rowId/revision/createdAt/updatedAt；最后填写）。 |
| param `data` | `en` | Complete row JSON object for replace. Replaces all business data, rather than applying a patch. Exclude rowId/revision/createdAt/updatedAt. This field may be long; provide it last, after action/rowId/revision. | Complete row business-fields object for replace (full-row overwrite, excluding rowId/revision/createdAt/updatedAt; supply last). |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `read_console_messages`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取当前标签页最近的 console 日志和 JS 异常。适合调试本地 Web 页面、定位报错、确认点击后前端异常。 | 读取目标标签页最近的 console 日志与 JS 异常（按时间倒序）。用于排查前端报错与点击后异常。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read recent console messages and JavaScript exceptions from the current tab. Useful for debugging local web pages, investigating errors, and checking for frontend exceptions after a click. | Read recent console messages and JavaScript exceptions from the target tab (newest first) to diagnose frontend errors and post-action exceptions. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `reset` | `zh-CN` | 是否清空已有 console 记录并开始监听，默认 false | 是否清空已有 console 记录并重新监听，默认 false。 |
| param `reset` | `en` | Whether to clear existing console records and start listening. Default: false. | Clear buffered console records before reading. Default: false. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 返回数量，默认 50 | 最多返回条数，默认 50，范围 1–200。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Number of results to return. Default: 50. | Maximum records to return. Default: 50; range: 1–200. |
| param `maxRecords` | `zh-CN` | 最多保留记录数，默认 200，范围 20-1000 | 缓冲区最多保留记录数，默认 200，范围 20–1000。 |
| param `maxRecords` | `en` | Maximum number of records to retain. Default: 200; range: 20–1000. | Maximum buffered records. Default: 200; range: 20–1000. |
| param `level` | `zh-CN` | 按级别过滤 | 按级别过滤（info/warning/error/debug）。 |
| param `level` | `en` | Filter by log level. | Filter by level (info/warning/error/debug). |
| param `type` | `zh-CN` | 按 CDP console type 过滤，如 log/error/warning/exception | 按 CDP console type 过滤，如 log/warn/error/exception。 |
| param `type` | `en` | Filter by CDP console type, such as log/error/warning/exception. | Filter by CDP console type, such as log/warn/error/exception. |
| param `textPattern` | `zh-CN` | 按日志文本正则过滤 | 日志文本正则过滤。 |
| param `textPattern` | `en` | Regular expression to match log text. | Regex to match log text. |
| param `urlPattern` | `zh-CN` | 按来源 URL 正则过滤 | 来源 URL 正则过滤。 |
| param `urlPattern` | `en` | Regular expression to match the source URL. | Regex to match source URL. |
| param `flags` | `zh-CN` | 正则 flags，默认 i | 正则 flags，默认 i。 |
| param `flags` | `en` | Regular expression flags. Default: i. | Regex flags. Default: i. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `network_watch`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 在已经打开的当前页面上开启或清空 Network 后置监听，只记录调用之后发生的请求，适合观察点击、搜索、提交、翻页等交互后的接口。它不能补抓页面首屏、重定向或导航期间已经发生的请求；要抓打开/跳转加载期请求，请在 open_tab 或 navigate 中传 watchNetwork:true。只记录请求摘要，response body 通过 network_body 按需获取。 | 在已打开标签页上开启或重置 Network 后置监听，只记录调用之后发生的请求摘要（适合点击、搜索、提交、翻页前调用；响应体按需用 network_body 读取）。抓首屏或重定向请求请在 open_tab/navigate 中传 watchNetwork:true。 |
| **description** | `en` | Start or reset network monitoring on an already open page. Records only requests made after this call, such as those triggered by clicking, searching, submitting, or paging. Cannot recover requests from the initial page load, redirects, or earlier navigation. To capture requests during opening or navigation, pass watchNetwork:true to open_tab or navigate. Records request summaries only; use network_body to retrieve a response body when needed. | Start or reset post-load network monitoring on an open tab to record summaries of requests made after this call (call before clicking, searching, submitting, or paging; read bodies with network_body). Pass watchNetwork:true to open_tab/navigate for initial load or redirect requests. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `reset` | `zh-CN` | 是否清空已有记录，默认 true；清空后只保留后续新发生的请求。 | 是否清空已有记录，默认 true。 |
| param `reset` | `en` | Whether to clear existing records. Default: true. After clearing, only new requests are retained. | Clear existing records. Default: true. |
| param `maxRecords` | `zh-CN` | 最多保留请求数，默认 200，范围 20-1000 | 最多保留请求数，默认 200，范围 20–1000。 |
| param `maxRecords` | `en` | Maximum number of requests to retain. Default: 200; range: 20–1000. | Maximum buffered requests. Default: 200; range: 20–1000. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `network_recent`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 查看当前标签页最近的 Network 请求摘要。用于诊断接口状态、权限、频控、下载和 SPA 数据来源。 | 查看目标标签页最近的 Network 请求摘要（按时间倒序返回 requestId、method、url、status、mimeType、resourceType 等）。大型接口采样或排查 SPA 数据源、鉴权与限频时配合 network_body 使用。 |
| **description** | `en` | View recent network request summaries for the current tab. Useful for diagnosing API status, permissions, rate limits, downloads, and SPA data sources. | View recent network request summaries for the target tab (newest first, returning requestId, method, url, status, mimeType, resourceType, etc.). Pair with network_body to sample large APIs or diagnose SPA data sources, auth, and rate limits. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 返回数量，默认 20 | 最多返回条数，默认 20，范围 1–100。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Number of results to return. Default: 20. | Maximum results. Default: 20; range: 1–100. |
| param `urlPattern` | `zh-CN` | 按 URL 正则过滤 | 请求 URL 正则过滤。 |
| param `urlPattern` | `en` | Regular expression to match request URLs. | Regex to match request URLs. |
| param `flags` | `zh-CN` | URL 正则 flags，默认 i | URL 正则 flags，默认 i。 |
| param `flags` | `en` | URL regular expression flags. Default: i. | URL regex flags. Default: i. |
| param `method` | `zh-CN` | GET/POST 等 | HTTP 方法过滤，如 GET/POST。 |
| param `method` | `en` | HTTP method, such as GET or POST. | HTTP method filter, such as GET/POST. |
| param `status` | `zh-CN` | 精确状态码过滤 | 精确状态码过滤，如 200/401/429。 |
| param `status` | `en` | Filter by an exact HTTP status code. | Exact HTTP status code filter, such as 200/401/429. |
| param `failed` | `zh-CN` | 只看失败或成功请求 | 过滤失败（true）或成功（false）请求。 |
| param `failed` | `en` | Filter for failed requests (true) or successful requests (false). | Filter failed (true) or successful (false) requests. |
| param `resourceType` | `zh-CN` | XHR/Fetch/Document/Image 等 CDP resource type | CDP resourceType 过滤，如 XHR/Fetch/Document/Image。 |
| param `resourceType` | `en` | CDP resource type, such as XHR/Fetch/Document/Image. | CDP resourceType filter, such as XHR/Fetch/Document/Image. |
| param `mimePattern` | `zh-CN` | 按 mimeType 正则过滤 | 响应 mimeType 正则过滤（如 json）。 |
| param `mimePattern` | `en` | Regular expression to match mimeType. | Regex to match response mimeType (e.g. json). |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `wait_for_response`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 等待匹配 URL/状态/method/mimeType 的 Network 响应出现。适合点击、搜索、提交后确认接口结果。 | 等待匹配 URL/method/status/mimeType 的 Network 响应（已缓冲匹配项则立即返回），可选附带响应体。 |
| **description** | `en` | Wait for a network response matching URL, status, method, or mimeType. Useful for checking API results after clicking, searching, or submitting. | Wait for a network response matching URL, method, status, or mimeType (returns immediately if already buffered), optionally including the body. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `urlPattern` | `zh-CN` | URL 正则，必填 | 必填。请求 URL 正则。 |
| param `urlPattern` | `en` | Required regular expression to match the URL. | Required regex to match the request URL. |
| param `flags` | `zh-CN` | URL 正则 flags，默认 i | URL 正则 flags，默认 i。 |
| param `flags` | `en` | URL regular expression flags. Default: i. | URL regex flags. Default: i. |
| param `method` | `zh-CN` | GET/POST 等 | HTTP 方法过滤，如 GET/POST。 |
| param `method` | `en` | HTTP method, such as GET or POST. | HTTP method filter, such as GET/POST. |
| param `status` | `zh-CN` | 精确状态码 | 精确 HTTP 状态码。 |
| param `status` | `en` | Exact HTTP status code. | Exact HTTP status code. |
| param `mimePattern` | `zh-CN` | mimeType 正则 | 响应 mimeType 正则。 |
| param `mimePattern` | `en` | Regular expression to match mimeType. | Regex to match response mimeType. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 超时秒数，默认 10 | 超时秒数，默认 10，最大 120。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Timeout in seconds. Default: 10. | Timeout in seconds. Default: 10; maximum: 120. |
| param `includeBody` | `zh-CN` | 是否等待加载完成并返回 body，默认 false | 是否等待加载完成并返回 body，默认 false。 |
| param `includeBody` | `en` | Whether to wait for loading to finish and return the response body. Default: false. | Wait for completion and return the response body. Default: false. |
| param `maxBytes` | `zh-CN` | body 最大字符数，默认 120000 | body 最大字符数，默认 120000，范围 1000–1000000。 |
| param `maxBytes` | `en` | Maximum number of body characters. Default: 120000. | Maximum body characters. Default: 120000; range: 1000–1000000. |

### `wait_for_network_idle`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 等待当前标签页 Network 空闲。适合点击、搜索、翻页后替代固定 wait。 | 等待目标标签页在途网络请求归零并连续空闲，适合点击、搜索或翻页后替代固定等待。 |
| **description** | `en` | Wait for network activity in the current tab to become idle. Use after clicking, searching, or paging instead of a fixed delay. | Wait for in-flight network requests in the target tab to stay idle; use after clicking, searching, or paging instead of a fixed delay. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `idleSeconds` | `zh-CN` | 连续空闲秒数，默认 0.8 | 连续空闲秒数，默认 0.8，范围 0.1–10。 |
| param `idleSeconds` | `en` | Required continuous idle period in seconds. Default: 0.8. | Continuous idle seconds required. Default: 0.8; range: 0.1–10. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 总超时秒数，默认 10 | 总超时秒数，默认 10，最大 120。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Overall timeout in seconds. Default: 10. | Overall timeout in seconds. Default: 10; maximum: 120. |
| param `urlPattern` | `zh-CN` | 只关注匹配 URL 的请求 | 仅统计匹配该正则的请求 URL。 |
| param `urlPattern` | `en` | Consider only requests whose URLs match this pattern. | Count only requests matching this URL regex. |
| param `flags` | `zh-CN` | URL 正则 flags，默认 i | URL 正则 flags，默认 i。 |
| param `flags` | `en` | URL regular expression flags. Default: i. | URL regex flags. Default: i. |

### `save_file`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 保存内容到用户下载目录。支持文本、JSON、CSV、HTML、Markdown 等任意格式。用于保存采集结果、导出数据等。filename 可选；缺省时会根据内容和 MIME 自动生成文件名。 | 将文本内容（文本、JSON、CSV、HTML、Markdown、SVG 等）保存到用户下载目录，返回 downloadId、filename、mimeType 和 size。filename 省略时根据内容和 MIME 自动生成文件名。 |
| **description** | `en` | Save content to the user's Downloads folder in text, JSON, CSV, HTML, Markdown, or other formats. Use for collected results and data exports. filename is optional: if omitted, a name is generated from the content and MIME type. | Save text content (plain text, JSON, CSV, HTML, Markdown, SVG, etc.) to the user's Downloads folder, returning downloadId, filename, mimeType, and size. Automatically generates a filename from content and MIME type when filename is omitted. |
| param `filename` | `zh-CN` | 可选文件名，如 results.md、data.json、report.csv；不填时自动生成 qingzhou-output-时间戳.*。 | 可选保存文件名（如 results.md、data.json、report.csv）；省略时自动生成 qingzhou-output-时间戳.*。 |
| param `filename` | `en` | Optional filename, such as results.md, data.json, or report.csv. If omitted, generates qingzhou-output-<timestamp>.*. | Optional filename (e.g. results.md, data.json, report.csv); defaults to qingzhou-output-<timestamp>.*. |
| param `mimeType` | `zh-CN` | MIME 类型，默认根据扩展名自动推断 | 可选 MIME 类型；省略时按扩展名或内容自动推断。 |
| param `mimeType` | `en` | MIME type. Inferred from the filename extension by default. | Optional MIME type; inferred from the extension or content when omitted. |
| param `content` | `zh-CN` | 文件内容。该字段可能很长，填写 filename/mimeType 后最后填写 content。 | 必填。要保存的文本内容（可能较长，先填 filename/mimeType，最后填 content）。 |
| param `content` | `en` | File content. This field may be long; provide it last, after filename/mimeType. | Required text content to save (supply filename/mimeType first and content last). |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `list_downloads`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 查看最近的下载列表及状态。保存或下载后，用返回的 downloadId 作为 id 精确查询。已完成且仍存在的文件会返回真实 fileUrl；预览时使用此地址，不要猜测本地路径。文件名只用于人工搜索。 | 查询最近的浏览器下载项及状态（按开始时间倒序）。传入保存或下载工具返回的 downloadId 作为 id 可精确核验；当传入 id 且文件已完成并存在时，额外返回真实 fileUrl。 |
| **description** | `en` | List recent downloads and their status. After saving or downloading, pass the returned downloadId as id to check that exact download. A completed file that still exists includes its actual fileUrl; use that URL for preview instead of guessing a local path. Filename search is only for manual lookup. | List recent browser downloads and their status (newest first). Pass a returned downloadId as id to check that exact item; when id is specified and the file is complete and present on disk, returns its fileUrl. |
| param `id` | `zh-CN` | Chrome 下载 ID；使用保存或下载工具返回的 downloadId。 | save_file、download_url 或 document_create/document_download 返回的 downloadId。 |
| param `id` | `en` | Chrome download ID returned by a save or download tool. | downloadId returned by save_file, download_url, or document_create/document_download. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 返回数量，默认 10 | 最多返回条数，默认 10。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum results to return. Default: 10. | Maximum items to return. Default: 10. |
| param `query` | `zh-CN` | 按 Chrome 实际保存路径过滤；已知 downloadId 时优先传 id | 匹配本地保存路径的正则表达式（已知 downloadId 时优先传 id）。 |
| param `query` | `en` | Filter by the path Chrome actually saved. Prefer id when downloadId is known. | Regular expression matched against the local saved path (prefer id when downloadId is known). |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `document_fetch`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 将网页中的 Word、Excel、PDF、HTML、CSV、TXT、Markdown、代码、配置或日志附件拉取到轻舟本地文档库，返回 fileId。后续通常调用 qingzhou_documents__document_parse；如果只需原样下载，不要使用本工具，改用 download_url。 | 将远程文档附件（docx/xlsx/pdf/html/csv/txt/md 及常见代码/配置/日志）拉取到当前任务文档库并返回 fileId，供后续 qingzhou_documents__document_parse 解析。仅需原样下载文件时请用 download_url。 |
