Usually followed by qingzhou_documents__document_parse. For downloading the original file unchanged, use download_url instead. | Fetch a remote document attachment (docx/xlsx/pdf/html/csv/txt/md or code/config/log file) into the current task's document library and return fileId for qingzhou_documents__document_parse. Use download_url instead to download a file unchanged. |
| param `url` | `zh-CN` | 附件 URL，支持 docx/xlsx/pdf/html/csv/txt/md 以及常见代码、配置和日志文件。 | 必填。远程文档 HTTP(S) URL。 |
| param `url` | `en` | Attachment URL. Supports docx/xlsx/pdf/html/csv/txt/md and common code, configuration, and log files. | Required HTTP(S) URL of the remote document. |
| param `filename` | `zh-CN` | 本地文档库文件名；不填时从响应头或 URL 推断。 | 可选文档库文件名；省略时从响应头或 URL 推断。 |
| param `filename` | `en` | Filename in the local document library. If omitted, inferred from response headers or the URL. | Optional filename in the document library; inferred from response headers or URL if omitted. |
| param `mimeType` | `zh-CN` | MIME 类型；不填时从响应头推断。 | 可选 MIME 类型；省略时从响应头或文件名推断。 |
| param `mimeType` | `en` | MIME type. If omitted, inferred from response headers. | Optional MIME type; inferred from response headers or filename if omitted. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `document_parse`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 将已拉取的本地文件解析为结构化 docModel，支持 Office、PDF、HTML、CSV、文本、Markdown、代码、配置和日志。不要把整份长文档塞进上下文；拿到 docModelId 后按需调用 qingzhou_documents__document_read_chunk，或直接用 qingzhou_documents__document_create 转换格式。 | 将 fileId 对应的本地文件解析为结构化 docModel，返回 docModelId、目录、分块数与表格摘要。随后用 qingzhou_documents__document_read_chunk 按需分块读取，或用 qingzhou_documents__document_create 转换格式。 |
| **description** | `en` | Parse a fetched local file into a structured docModel. Supports Office, PDF, HTML, CSV, text, Markdown, code, configuration, and logs. Do not put an entire long document in context. After obtaining docModelId, read selected chunks with qingzhou_documents__document_read_chunk or convert formats directly with qingzhou_documents__document_create. | Parse a fetched local file (fileId) into a structured docModel, returning docModelId, outline, chunk count, and table summaries. Read chunks with qingzhou_documents__document_read_chunk or convert formats with qingzhou_documents__document_create. |
| param `fileId` | `zh-CN` | qingzhou_documents__document_fetch 返回的 fileId。 | 必填。qingzhou_documents__document_fetch 返回的 fileId。 |
| param `fileId` | `en` | fileId returned by qingzhou_documents__document_fetch. | Required fileId returned by qingzhou_documents__document_fetch. |
| param `chunkSize` | `zh-CN` | 每个文本块的目标字符数，默认 6000。 | 每个文本块的目标字符数，默认 6000。 |
| param `chunkSize` | `en` | Target characters per text chunk. Default: 6000. | Target characters per text chunk. Default: 6000. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `document_read_chunk`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 小块读取 docModel，避免长文档挤占上下文。正文按 chunkIndex 返回 Markdown；表格按返回的 tableId（Excel 也兼容 sheetName）配合 offset、limit 分页读取结构化行。继续读取时保留 docModelId，并从结果中的 next 继续。 | 按块或按表分页读取 docModel：不传 tableId/sheetName 时按 chunkIndex 返回正文 Markdown；传 tableId（或 Excel/CSV 的 sheetName）配合 offset/limit 分页读取表格行。沿用返回结果中的 next 继续读取。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read small portions of a docModel to preserve context space. Text is returned as Markdown by chunkIndex. Read structured table rows using the returned tableId (sheetName is also supported for Excel), offset, and limit. Keep docModelId and continue from next in the result. | Read a docModel by text chunk or table page: omit tableId/sheetName to read Markdown text by chunkIndex; pass tableId (or sheetName for Excel/CSV) with offset/limit to page through table rows. Follow next in the result to continue. |
| param `docModelId` | `zh-CN` | qingzhou_documents__document_parse 返回的 docModelId。 | 必填。qingzhou_documents__document_parse 或 load_skill_resource 返回的 docModelId。 |
| param `docModelId` | `en` | docModelId returned by qingzhou_documents__document_parse. | Required docModelId returned by qingzhou_documents__document_parse or load_skill_resource. |
| param `chunkIndex` | `zh-CN` | 文本块序号，从 0 开始；默认 0。 | 正文块序号（0-based），默认 0。 |
| param `chunkIndex` | `en` | Zero-based text chunk index. Default: 0. | 0-based text chunk index. Default: 0. |
| param `tableId` | `zh-CN` | 正文读取结果中 tables 返回的稳定表格标识；传入后按表格行读取。 | 要读取的稳定表格标识（来自解析结果或正文块中的 tables）。 |
| param `tableId` | `en` | Stable table ID from tables in a text-reading result. When provided, reads table rows. | Stable table ID to read (from parse results or tables in a text chunk). |
| param `sheetName` | `zh-CN` | Excel/CSV sheet 名称；为兼容入口，优先使用返回的 tableId。 | Excel/CSV 工作表名称（优先使用 tableId）。 |
| param `sheetName` | `en` | Excel/CSV sheet name, supported for compatibility. Prefer the returned tableId. | Excel/CSV sheet name (prefer tableId when available). |
| param `offset` | `zh-CN` | 表格起始行，默认 0。 | 表格读取起始行偏移，默认 0。 |
| param `offset` | `en` | Starting table row. Default: 0. | Starting row offset for table reads. Default: 0. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 表格读取行数，默认 50，最多 200。 | 表格单次读取行数，默认 50，范围 1–200。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Table rows to read. Default: 50; maximum: 200. | Number of table rows to read. Default: 50; range: 1–200. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `document_create`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 根据 docModelId 或少量 blocks/sheets 生成本地产物。format 必填且只能是 "docx"、"xlsx"、"pdf"、"html"、"txt"、"csv"；用户明确指定目标格式时必须传对应值并直接生成，不要创建其他中间格式。每次重试仍须显式传 format；直接传 blocks 时每个 block 都必须显式传 type。大文档必须传 docModelId，不要让模型重新输出整份 blocks；download 默认 true，会直接加入 Chrome 下载列表。 | 根据 docModelId 或少量 blocks/sheets 生成文档产物（默认 download:true 直接加入下载列表）。format 必填且只能是 "docx"、"xlsx"、"pdf"、"html"、"txt"、"csv"；用户明确指定目标格式时必须传对应值并直接生成，不要建中间格式，每次调用与重试均须显式传 format。大文档必须传 docModelId；直接传 blocks 时每项须显式指定 type。 |
| **description** | `en` | Generate a local artifact from docModelId or a small blocks/sheets array. format is required and must be one of "docx", "xlsx", "pdf", "html", "txt", "csv". If the user specifies a target format, generate that format directly without intermediates. Explicitly supply format on every retry. Every directly supplied block must have type. Large documents must use docModelId; do not regenerate the entire blocks array. download defaults to true and adds the artifact to Chrome downloads. | Generate a document artifact from docModelId or a small blocks/sheets array (download defaults to true and adds to browser downloads). format is required and must be one of "docx", "xlsx", "pdf", "html", "txt", "csv" (supply explicitly on every call and retry, generating the requested format directly without intermediates). Large documents must use docModelId; every direct block must specify type. |
| param `format` | `zh-CN` | 必填，只能是 "docx"、"xlsx"、"pdf"、"html"、"txt"、"csv"。必须与用户要求的目标格式一致；重试时也必须再次传入。 | 必填，只能是 "docx"、"xlsx"、"pdf"、"html"、"txt"、"csv"；每次调用及重试均须显式传入。 |
| param `format` | `en` | Required. Must be one of "docx", "xlsx", "pdf", "html", "txt", "csv" and match the format requested by the user. Supply it again on every retry. | Required. Must be one of "docx", "xlsx", "pdf", "html", "txt", "csv"; supply explicitly on every call and retry. |
| param `filename` | `zh-CN` | 输出文件名，例如 result.pdf。 | 输出文件名，如 result.pdf。 |
| param `filename` | `en` | Output filename, for example result.pdf. | Output filename, such as result.pdf. |
| param `title` | `zh-CN` | 文档元数据标题，也用于未填写 filename 时生成默认文件名；不会自动写入正文。需要可见标题时，在 blocks 中传 {type:'heading', level:1, text:'标题'}。 | 文档元数据标题，未填 filename 时也作默认文件名，不会自动写入正文；需要可见标题时在 blocks 中传 {type:'heading', level:1, text:'标题'}。 |
| param `title` | `en` | Document metadata title, also used to generate a default filename when filename is omitted. It is not inserted into the document body. For a visible title, add {type:'heading', level:1, text:'Title'} to blocks. | Document metadata title, also used as the default filename when filename is omitted and not inserted into body text; add {type:'heading', level:1, text:'Title'} to blocks for a visible heading. |
| param `docModelId` | `zh-CN` | qingzhou_documents__document_parse 返回的 docModelId；大文档优先使用。 | 来源 docModelId（不可与 blocks/sheets 同时传入；长文档转换优先使用）。 |
| param `docModelId` | `en` | docModelId returned by qingzhou_documents__document_parse. Prefer this for large documents. | Source docModelId (cannot be combined with blocks/sheets; preferred for large documents). |
| param `download` | `zh-CN` | 创建后是否立即加入下载列表，默认 true。 | 生成后是否立即加入浏览器下载列表，默认 true。 |
| param `download` | `en` | Add the file to Chrome downloads immediately after creation. Default: true. | Add the generated file to browser downloads immediately. Default: true. |
| param `saveAs` | `zh-CN` | 下载时是否弹出另存为，默认 false。 | 下载时是否弹出另存为对话框，默认 false。 |
| param `saveAs` | `en` | Show a Save As dialog when downloading. Default: false. | Show a Save As dialog when downloading. Default: false. |
| param `blocks` | `zh-CN` | 少量文档块。每项必须使用 {type:'heading', text:'标题', level:1}、{type:'paragraph', text:'正文'}、{type:'list', items:['事项']} 或 {type:'table', columns:['列名'], rows:[['值']]} 等规范结构；不接受 {heading:...}/{table:true}/{list:[...]} 简写。不要传超大数组；先填写 format/filename/docModelId/download/saveAs，最后填写本字段。 | 直接传入的少量内容块数组（JSON 序列化不超过 200,000 字符；不可与 docModelId 同用）。每项必须含显式 type（如 {type:'heading', level:1, text:'...'}、{type:'paragraph', text:'...'}、{type:'list', items:['...']}、{type:'table', columns:['...'], rows:[['...']]}，禁止 {heading:...} 等简写；xlsx/csv 仅允许 table 块）。先填 format/filename/title/download/saveAs，最后填本字段。 |
| param `blocks` | `en` | A small array of document blocks. Each item must use a supported shape, such as {type:'heading', text:'Title', level:1}, {type:'paragraph', text:'Body'}, {type:'list', items:['Item']}, or {type:'table', columns:['Column'], rows:[['Value']]}. Shorthand such as {heading:...}, {table:true}, or {list:[...]} is not accepted. Avoid large arrays. Provide this field last, after format/filename/docModelId/download/saveAs. | Small array of content blocks (at most 200,000 JSON chars; cannot be combined with docModelId). Each block must specify type (e.g. {type:'heading', level:1, text:'...'}, {type:'paragraph', text:'...'}, {type:'list', items:['...']}, {type:'table', columns:['...'], rows:[['...']]}; shorthand like {heading:...} is rejected; xlsx/csv accepts table blocks only). Supply this field last. |
| param `blocks[].type` | `zh-CN` | 内容块类型；每个 block 都必须显式填写。 | 内容块类型；每个 block 都必须显式填写。 |
| param `blocks[].type` | `en` | Block type. Specify this for every block. | Block type. Specify this for every block. |
| param `blocks[].text` | `zh-CN` | paragraph、heading、code 的正文；link 的显示文本。 | paragraph、heading、code 的正文；link 的显示文本。 |
| param `blocks[].text` | `en` | Body text for paragraph, heading, or code; display text for link. | Body text for paragraph, heading, or code; display text for link. |
| param `blocks[].level` | `zh-CN` | heading 级别，默认 1。 | heading 级别，默认 1。 |
| param `blocks[].level` | `en` | Heading level. Defaults to 1. | Heading level. Defaults to 1. |
| param `blocks[].ordered` | `zh-CN` | list 是否为有序列表，默认 false。 | list 是否为有序列表，默认 false。 |
| param `blocks[].ordered` | `en` | Whether the list is ordered. Defaults to false. | Whether the list is ordered. Defaults to false. |
| param `blocks[].items` | `zh-CN` | list 的非空列表项。 | list 的非空列表项。 |
| param `blocks[].items` | `en` | A nonempty array of list items. | A nonempty array of list items. |
| param `blocks[].items[]` | `zh-CN` | 列表项使用非空字符串，或 {text, children}；children 使用相同结构。 | 列表项使用非空字符串，或 {text, children}；children 使用相同结构。 |
| param `blocks[].items[]` | `en` | Use a nonempty string or {text, children} for each list item. Items in children use the same structure. | Use a nonempty string or {text, children} for each list item. Items in children use the same structure. |
| param `blocks[].name` | `zh-CN` | table 名称；导出为工作簿时会作为 sheet 名称。不能包含 : \ / ? * [ ]，也不能以单引号开头或结尾。 | table 名称；导出为工作簿时会作为 sheet 名称。不能包含 : \ / ? * [ ]，也不能以单引号开头或结尾。 |
| param `blocks[].name` | `en` | Table name, also used as the sheet name when exported to a workbook. Must not contain : \ / ? * [ ] or start or end with a single quote. | Table name, also used as the sheet name when exported to a workbook. Must not contain : \ / ? * [ ] or start or end with a single quote. |
| param `blocks[].columns` | `zh-CN` | table 的稳定非空列名。 | table 的稳定非空列名。 |
| param `blocks[].columns` | `en` | Stable, nonempty column names for the table. | Stable, nonempty column names for the table. |
| param `blocks[].rows` | `zh-CN` | 每个数组元素表示一行：对象行写成 rows:[{"列名":"值"},{"列名":"值"}]，数组行写成 rows:[["值1","值2"],["值3","值4"]]；不要把多行再整体包进一个数组。同一张表不可混用两种行结构。 | 每个数组元素表示一行：对象行写成 rows:[{"列名":"值"},{"列名":"值"}]，数组行写成 rows:[["值1","值2"],["值3","值4"]]；不要把多行再整体包进一个数组。同一张表不可混用两种行结构。 |
| param `blocks[].rows` | `en` | Each array element is one row: use rows:[{"Column":"Value"},{"Column":"Value"}] for object rows, or rows:[["Value1","Value2"],["Value3","Value4"]] for array rows. Do not wrap all rows in another array. Do not mix the two row formats in one table. | Each array element is one row: use rows:[{"Column":"Value"},{"Column":"Value"}] for object rows, or rows:[["Value1","Value2"],["Value3","Value4"]] for array rows. Do not wrap all rows in another array. Do not mix the two row formats in one table. |
| param `blocks[].rows[]` | `zh-CN` | 每个数组元素表示一行：对象行写成 rows:[{"列名":"值"},{"列名":"值"}]，数组行写成 rows:[["值1","值2"],["值3","值4"]]；不要把多行再整体包进一个数组。同一张表不可混用两种行结构。 | 每个数组元素表示一行：对象行写成 rows:[{"列名":"值"},{"列名":"值"}]，数组行写成 rows:[["值1","值2"],["值3","值4"]]；不要把多行再整体包进一个数组。同一张表不可混用两种行结构。 |
| param `blocks[].rows[]` | `en` | Each array element is one row: use rows:[{"Column":"Value"},{"Column":"Value"}] for object rows, or rows:[["Value1","Value2"],["Value3","Value4"]] for array rows. Do not wrap all rows in another array. Do not mix the two row formats in one table. | Each array element is one row: use rows:[{"Column":"Value"},{"Column":"Value"}] for object rows, or rows:[["Value1","Value2"],["Value3","Value4"]] for array rows. Do not wrap all rows in another array. Do not mix the two row formats in one table. |
| param `blocks[].header` | `zh-CN` | 二维数组第一行是否为表头；默认 true。 | 二维数组第一行是否为表头；默认 true。 |
| param `blocks[].header` | `en` | Whether the first array row is a header. Defaults to true. | Whether the first array row is a header. Defaults to true. |
| param `blocks[].url` | `zh-CN` | link 使用 http(s)、mailto、tel 或页内 #anchor；image 使用 http(s) 或 base64 编码的 PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP。 | link 使用 http(s)、mailto、tel 或页内 #anchor；image 使用 http(s) 或 base64 编码的 PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP。 |
| param `blocks[].url` | `en` | For link: http(s), mailto, tel, or an in-page #anchor. For image: http(s) or a base64-encoded PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP data URL. | For link: http(s), mailto, tel, or an in-page #anchor. For image: http(s) or a base64-encoded PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP data URL. |
| param `blocks[].alt` | `zh-CN` | image 的替代文本。 | image 的替代文本。 |
| param `blocks[].alt` | `en` | Alternative text for the image. | Alternative text for the image. |
| param `blocks[].caption` | `zh-CN` | image 的说明文字。 | image 的说明文字。 |
| param `blocks[].caption` | `en` | Image caption. | Image caption. |
| param `blocks[].language` | `zh-CN` | code 的语言标识。 | code 的语言标识。 |
| param `blocks[].language` | `en` | Language identifier for the code block. | Language identifier for the code block. |
| param `blocks[].startLine` | `zh-CN` | code 的可选起始行号。 | code 的可选起始行号。 |
| param `blocks[].startLine` | `en` | Optional starting line number for the code block. | Optional starting line number for the code block. |
| param `blocks[].endLine` | `zh-CN` | code 的可选结束行号。 | code 的可选结束行号。 |
| param `blocks[].endLine` | `en` | Optional ending line number for the code block. | Optional ending line number for the code block. |
| param `sheets` | `zh-CN` | 少量 Excel sheets：{name, columns, rows}。rows 的每个元素就是一行：对象行使用 rows:[{"公司":"A"},{"公司":"B"}]，数组行使用 rows:[["A"],["B"]]，不要写成 rows:[[{"公司":"A"},{"公司":"B"}]]。单张表内不可混用行结构。大表格先写入 data_store 或使用 docModelId；先填写定位参数，最后填写本字段。 | 直接传入的少量工作表数组 {name, columns, rows, header}（不可与 docModelId 或 table blocks 同用；csv 仅支持单张表）。rows 的每个元素就是一行：对象行 rows:[{"公司":"A"},{"公司":"B"}] 或数组行 rows:[["A"],["B"]]，不要写成 rows:[[{"公司":"A"},{"公司":"B"}]]，单表内不可混用。先填控制参数，最后填本字段。 |
| param `sheets` | `en` | A small array of Excel sheets: {name, columns, rows}. Each rows element is one row. Use rows:[{"Company":"A"},{"Company":"B"}] for object rows or rows:[["A"],["B"]] for array rows, never rows:[[{"Company":"A"},{"Company":"B"}]]. Do not mix row shapes within a sheet. Store large tables with data_store or use docModelId. Provide identifying parameters first and this field last. | Small array of worksheets {name, columns, rows, header} (cannot be combined with docModelId or table blocks; csv supports one sheet only). Each rows element is one row: object rows rows:[{"Company":"A"},{"Company":"B"}] or array rows rows:[["A"],["B"]], never rows:[[{"Company":"A"},{"Company":"B"}]], without mixing in a sheet. Supply control arguments first and this field last. |
| param `sheets[].name` | `zh-CN` | 工作表名称；必须唯一，不能包含 : \ / ? * [ ]，也不能以单引号开头或结尾。 | 工作表名称；必须唯一，不能包含 : \ / ? * [ ]，也不能以单引号开头或结尾。 |
| param `sheets[].name` | `en` | Unique worksheet name. Must not contain : \ / ? * [ ] or start or end with a single quote. | Unique worksheet name. Must not contain : \ / ? * [ ] or start or end with a single quote. |
| param `sheets[].columns` | `zh-CN` | 稳定非空列名。 | 稳定非空列名。 |
| param `sheets[].columns` | `en` | Stable, nonempty column names. | Stable, nonempty column names. |
| param `sheets[].rows` | `zh-CN` | 每个数组元素表示一行：对象行写成 rows:[{"列名":"值"},{"列名":"值"}]，数组行写成 rows:[["值1","值2"],["值3","值4"]]；不要把多行再整体包进一个数组。同一张表不可混用两种行结构。 | 每个数组元素表示一行：对象行写成 rows:[{"列名":"值"},{"列名":"值"}]，数组行写成 rows:[["值1","值2"],["值3","值4"]]；不要把多行再整体包进一个数组。同一张表不可混用两种行结构。 |

Do not wrap all rows in another array. Do not wrap all rows in another array. Do not mix the two row formats in one table. |
| param `sheets[].rows[]` | `zh-CN` | 每个数组元素表示一行：对象行写成 rows:[{"列名":"值"},{"列名":"值"}]，数组行写成 rows:[["值1","值2"],["值3","值4"]]；不要把多行再整体包进一个数组。同一张表不可混用两种行结构。 | 每个数组元素表示一行：对象行写成 rows:[{"列名":"值"},{"列名":"值"}]，数组行写成 rows:[["值1","值2"],["值3","值4"]]；不要把多行再整体包进一个数组。同一张表不可混用两种行结构。 |

Do not wrap all rows in another array. Do not mix the two row formats in one table. | Each array element is one row: use rows:[{"Column":"Value"},{"Column":"Value"}] for object rows, or rows:[["Value1","Value2"],["Value3","Value4"]] for array rows. Do not wrap all rows in another array. Do not mix the two row formats in one table. |

### `document_download`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 将 qingzhou_documents__document_create 已生成但尚未下载的 artifact，或任务中已有的文档 artifact，加入 Chrome 下载列表。 | 将当前任务已生成的文档产物（artifactId）加入浏览器下载列表。 |
| **description** | `en` | Add an artifact created by qingzhou_documents__document_create but not yet downloaded, or an existing document artifact in this task, to Chrome downloads. | Add a generated document artifact (artifactId) from the current task to browser downloads. |
| param `artifactId` | `zh-CN` | qingzhou_documents__document_create 或 qingzhou_documents__document_list_artifacts 返回的 artifactId。 | 必填。qingzhou_documents__document_create 或 document_list_artifacts 返回的 artifactId。 |
| param `artifactId` | `en` | artifactId returned by qingzhou_documents__document_create or qingzhou_documents__document_list_artifacts. | Required artifactId from qingzhou_documents__document_create or document_list_artifacts. |
| param `filename` | `zh-CN` | 下载文件名，可不填。 | 可选下载文件名。 |
| param `filename` | `en` | Optional download filename. | Optional download filename. |
| param `saveAs` | `zh-CN` | 是否弹出另存为，默认 false。 | 是否弹出另存为对话框，默认 false。 |
| param `saveAs` | `en` | Show a Save As dialog. Default: false. | Show a Save As dialog. Default: false. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `document_list_artifacts`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 列出当前任务最近生成的文档产物，便于查找 artifactId 或重新下载。 | 列出当前任务已生成的文档产物摘要及 artifactId。 |
| **description** | `en` | List document artifacts recently generated in this task to find artifactId values or download files again. | List document artifacts generated in the current task with their artifactIds. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 返回数量，默认 20，最多 100。 | 最多返回数量，默认 20，范围 1–100。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum results to return. Default: 20; maximum: 100. | Maximum artifacts to return. Default: 20; range: 1–100. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `mcp_inspect_server`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 探测尚未注册到 skill 的远程 HTTP MCP server，读取 serverInfo、capabilities 和 tools/list 等 metadata。默认使用本地 metadata cache；cache miss 或 refresh=true 时才请求远端。不会注册 server，不支持本地 stdio。 | 探测尚未注册到 Skill 的远程 HTTP MCP server，读取 serverInfo、capabilities 和 tools/list（不支持本地 stdio，也不会自动注册 server）。 |
| **description** | `en` | Inspect an unregistered remote HTTP MCP server for metadata such as serverInfo, capabilities, and tools/list. Uses the local metadata cache unless it is missing or refresh=true. Does not register the server. Local stdio is unsupported. | Inspect an unregistered remote HTTP MCP server for serverInfo, capabilities, and tools/list (local stdio is unsupported; does not register the server). |
| param `url` | `zh-CN` | 远程 HTTP MCP endpoint URL，例如 https://example.com/mcp。只支持 http(s)，不支持本地 stdio。 | 必填。远程 HTTP(S) MCP endpoint URL（如 https://example.com/mcp）。 |
| param `url` | `en` | Remote HTTP MCP endpoint URL, for example https://example.com/mcp. Only http(s) is supported; local stdio is unsupported. | Required remote HTTP(S) MCP endpoint URL (e.g. https://example.com/mcp). |
| param `name` | `zh-CN` | 可选临时名称，用于结果展示和缓存 key；不填时使用 adhoc。 | 可选临时标识名，默认 adhoc。 |
| param `name` | `en` | Optional temporary name for display and the cache key. Default: adhoc. | Optional temporary server name. Default: adhoc. |
| param `headers` | `zh-CN` | 可选请求头，例如 Authorization 或 API key。 | 可选 HTTP 请求头对象（如 Authorization）。 |
| param `headers` | `en` | Optional request headers, such as Authorization or an API key. | Optional HTTP request headers object (e.g. Authorization). |
| param `refresh` | `zh-CN` | 是否绕过 metadata cache，强制重新 initialize 并执行 tools/list。 | 是否强制重新请求远端元数据，默认 false。 |
| param `refresh` | `en` | Bypass the metadata cache, initialize again, and run tools/list. | Force a fresh metadata request to the remote server. Default: false. |
| param `includeResources` | `zh-CN` | 是否尝试读取 resources/list 和 resource templates；仅在 server capabilities 声明支持时执行。 | 当 server 支持时是否一并读取 resources/list 与模板，默认 false。 |
| param `includeResources` | `en` | Attempt resources/list and resource templates only if the server declares support in its capabilities. | Read resources/list and resource templates when supported by the server. Default: false. |
| param `includePrompts` | `zh-CN` | 是否尝试读取 prompts/list；仅在 server capabilities 声明支持时执行。 | 当 server 支持时是否一并读取 prompts/list，默认 false。 |
| param `includePrompts` | `en` | Attempt prompts/list only if the server declares support in its capabilities. | Read prompts/list when supported by the server. Default: false. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | tools/resources/prompts 最多返回多少项，默认 100。 | tools/resources/prompts 各自最多返回项数，默认 100。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum tools/resources/prompts to return. Default: 100. | Maximum items returned per list (tools/resources/prompts). Default: 100. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `mcp_call_tool`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 直接按 URL 调用尚未注册到 skill 的远程 HTTP MCP tool，用于开发期一次性验证或轻量请求。已注册 MCP 必须先 load_skill(skillName)，再调用 registry 返回的 Skill 工具名 pluginTool.name；不要用本工具绕过 Skill 工具层。建议先探测 inputSchema；默认不重复执行 tools/list。不会注册 server，不支持本地 stdio。 | 按 URL 调用尚未注册到 Skill 的远程 HTTP MCP 工具（仅用于一次性验证；已注册到 Skill 的 MCP 必须先 load_skill(skillName) 再调用对应的 Skill 工具名 pluginTool.name）。调用前先用 mcp_inspect_server 确认工具名与 inputSchema。 |
| **description** | `en` | Call an unregistered remote HTTP MCP tool directly by URL for a one-off request or development check. For registered MCP tools, first load_skill(skillName), then invoke pluginTool.name from the registry. Do not bypass the Skill tool layer with this tool. Inspect inputSchema first. Does not repeat tools/list by default or register the server. Local stdio is unsupported. | Directly invoke an unregistered remote HTTP MCP tool by URL for one-off testing (for registered MCP tools, call load_skill(skillName) and invoke the Skill tool name pluginTool.name instead). Inspect the server with mcp_inspect_server first to confirm tool name and inputSchema. |
| param `url` | `zh-CN` | 远程 HTTP MCP endpoint URL，例如 https://example.com/mcp。只支持 http(s)，不支持本地 stdio。 | 必填。远程 HTTP(S) MCP endpoint URL（如 https://example.com/mcp）。 |
| param `url` | `en` | Remote HTTP MCP endpoint URL, for example https://example.com/mcp. Only http(s) is supported; local stdio is unsupported. | Required remote HTTP(S) MCP endpoint URL (e.g. https://example.com/mcp). |
| param `name` | `zh-CN` | 可选临时名称，用于结果展示和缓存 key；不填时使用 adhoc。 | 可选临时标识名，默认 adhoc。 |
| param `name` | `en` | Optional temporary name for display and the cache key. Default: adhoc. | Optional temporary server name. Default: adhoc. |
| param `headers` | `zh-CN` | 可选请求头，例如 Authorization 或 API key。 | 可选 HTTP 请求头对象（如 Authorization）。 |
| param `headers` | `en` | Optional request headers, such as Authorization or an API key. | Optional HTTP request headers object (e.g. Authorization). |
| param `tool` | `zh-CN` | 远程 MCP tool 原始名称。建议来自 qingzhou_mcp__mcp_inspect_server 返回的 tools[].name。 | 必填。远程 MCP 工具原始名称（取自 mcp_inspect_server 返回的 tools[].name）。 |
| param `tool` | `en` | Original remote MCP tool name. Prefer tools[].name returned by qingzhou_mcp__mcp_inspect_server. | Required remote MCP tool name (from tools[].name in mcp_inspect_server). |
| param `args` | `zh-CN` | 传给远程 MCP tool 的 JSON 参数对象。 | 传给远程 MCP 工具的 JSON 参数对象。 |
| param `args` | `en` | JSON argument object to pass to the remote MCP tool. | JSON arguments object passed to the remote MCP tool. |
| param `validate` | `zh-CN` | 调用前是否用 tools/list 校验 tool 存在。默认 false；传 true 时使用 metadata cache，refresh=true 时强制刷新。 | 调用前是否先通过 tools/list 校验工具存在，默认 false。 |
| param `validate` | `en` | Check that the tool exists using tools/list before calling it. Default: false. When true, uses the metadata cache unless refresh=true. | Verify that the tool exists in tools/list before calling. Default: false. |
| param `refresh` | `zh-CN` | validate=true 时是否绕过 metadata cache，强制重新执行 tools/list。 | validate=true 时是否强制刷新 tools/list，默认 false。 |
| param `refresh` | `en` | When validate=true, bypass the metadata cache and request tools/list again. | Force a fresh tools/list check when validate=true. Default: false. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `mcp_list_tools`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 列出所有 skill/plugin.json 汇总出的虚拟全局 MCP registry。结果里的 pluginTool.name 是已注册 MCP 的正式调用入口；应先 load_skill(skillName)，再调用该 Skill 工具名。若只看当前页面或本会话已加载 skill 激活的 MCP，传 activeOnly=true。 | 列出所有 Skill plugin.json 声明的虚拟全局 MCP 注册表。结果中的 pluginTool.name 是已注册 MCP 的正式调用入口：先调用 load_skill(skillName) 激活所属 Skill，再调用 pluginTool.name。 |
| **description** | `en` | List the virtual global MCP registry assembled from all skill/plugin.json manifests. pluginTool.name in each result is the registered invocation entry point. Call load_skill(skillName) first, then use that Skill tool name. Set activeOnly=true to include only MCP tools activated by the current page or Skills loaded in this session. | List the virtual global MCP registry declared across all Skill plugin.json manifests. pluginTool.name in each entry is the formal invocation entry point: call load_skill(skillName) first, then invoke pluginTool.name. |
| param `server` | `zh-CN` | 可选 MCP server 引用，如 pluginId:serverId 或 serverId；不填时列出虚拟 registry 中所有 MCP server。 | 可选 MCP server 过滤引用（如 pluginId:serverId 或 serverId）；省略时列出全部。 |
| param `server` | `en` | Optional MCP server reference, such as pluginId:serverId or serverId. If omitted, lists every server in the virtual registry. | Optional MCP server filter reference (e.g. pluginId:serverId or serverId); lists all servers if omitted. |
| param `refresh` | `zh-CN` | 是否绕过本地 MCP 工具缓存，重新向远程 server 执行 tools/list。 | 是否强制重新请求远端 tools/list，默认 false。 |
| param `refresh` | `en` | Bypass the local MCP tool cache and request tools/list from the remote server again. | Force a fresh tools/list request to the remote server. Default: false. |
| param `activeOnly` | `zh-CN` | 是否只包含当前页面或已 load_skill 激活的 Skill MCP。默认 false，即全局列出所有 skill 声明的 MCP。 | 是否仅列出当前页面或本会话已 load_skill 激活的 MCP 工具，默认 false。 |
| param `activeOnly` | `en` | Include only Skill MCP tools activated by the current page or load_skill. Default: false, which lists MCP tools declared by all Skills. | Include only MCP tools activated by the current page or load_skill in this session. Default: false. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 最多返回多少个工具，默认 50。 | 最多返回工具数，默认 50。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum tools to return. Default: 50. | Maximum tools to return. Default: 50. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `mcp_search_tools`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 搜索所有 skill/plugin.json 汇总出的虚拟全局 MCP registry。结果里的 pluginTool.name 是已注册 MCP 的正式调用入口；应先 load_skill(skillName)，再调用该 Skill 工具名。若只搜索当前页面或本会话已加载 skill 激活的 MCP，传 activeOnly=true。 | 按关键词搜索所有 Skill plugin.json 声明的虚拟全局 MCP 注册表。先调用 load_skill(skillName) 激活所属 Skill，再调用返回的 pluginTool.name。 |

Call load_skill(skillName) first, then use that Skill tool name. Set activeOnly=true to search only MCP tools activated by the current page or Skills loaded in this session. | Search the virtual global MCP registry declared across all Skill plugin.json manifests by keyword. Call load_skill(skillName) first, then invoke the returned pluginTool.name. |
| param `query` | `zh-CN` | 搜索关键词，可以是工具名、能力描述、serverId、pluginId 或 skill 名。 | 必填。搜索关键词（匹配工具名、描述、serverId、pluginId 或 skillName）。 |
| param `query` | `en` | Search terms: a tool name, capability description, serverId, pluginId, or Skill name. | Required search query (matches tool name, description, serverId, pluginId, or skillName). |
| param `server` | `zh-CN` | 可选 MCP server 引用，如 pluginId:serverId 或 serverId。 | 可选 MCP server 过滤引用（如 pluginId:serverId 或 serverId）。 |
| param `server` | `en` | Optional MCP server reference, such as pluginId:serverId or serverId. | Optional MCP server filter reference (e.g. pluginId:serverId or serverId). |
| param `refresh` | `zh-CN` | 是否重新向远程 server 执行 tools/list。 | 是否强制重新请求远端 tools/list，默认 false。 |
| param `refresh` | `en` | Request tools/list from the remote server again. | Force a fresh tools/list request to the remote server. Default: false. |
| param `activeOnly` | `zh-CN` | 是否只包含当前页面或已 load_skill 激活的 Skill MCP。默认 false，即全局搜索所有 skill 声明的 MCP。 | 是否仅搜索当前页面或本会话已 load_skill 激活的 MCP 工具，默认 false。 |
| param `activeOnly` | `en` | Search only Skill MCP tools activated by the current page or load_skill. Default: false, which searches MCP tools declared by all Skills. | Search only MCP tools activated by the current page or load_skill in this session. Default: false. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 最多返回多少个工具，默认 50。 | 最多返回工具数，默认 50。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum tools to return. Default: 50. | Maximum tools to return. Default: 50. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `screenshot`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 截图当前视口或指定元素的已渲染区域，并始终以图片形式返回给 LLM。需要随后按页面截图坐标操作时，传 actionable=true；返回的 coordinateSpace 会给出图片原始像素宽高，可直接把图上坐标交给 act_on_screen。传 destination 时会额外把截图保存为该图集的 image asset；不传 destination 则只返回图片不保存。元素级截图适合 read_image 因跨域 canvas 失败后的视觉兜底。target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。 | 截取当前视口或指定元素的已渲染区域，并始终以图片形式返回给 LLM。需随后用 act_on_screen 按坐标操作时传 actionable=true（不可与 target 同用），返回的 coordinateSpace 给出原始像素宽高，可直接将图上坐标传给 act_on_screen。传 destination 时额外把截图保存为该图集表的 image asset；传 target 时截取该元素区域。target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。 |
| **description** | `en` | Capture the current viewport or the rendered region of a specified element, and always return the capture to the model as an image. Pass actionable=true if you will use screenshot coordinates for subsequent actions. The returned coordinateSpace gives the original image width and height in pixels, so coordinates from the image can be passed directly to act_on_screen. Providing destination also saves the capture as an image asset in that collection; otherwise the image is returned without being saved. Element screenshots provide a visual fallback when read_image fails because of cross-origin canvas restrictions. target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. | Capture the current viewport or the rendered region of a target element, and always return the image to the model. Pass actionable=true (cannot be combined with target) when you will act by coordinates with act_on_screen; the returned coordinateSpace gives the original pixel width and height. Pass destination to also save the capture as an image asset in that gallery table; pass target to capture a specific element. target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页，target 为 uid:... 时可省略。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页（target 为 uid:... 时也可省略）。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab; may be omitted for a uid:... target. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab (optional when target is uid:...). |
| param `target` | `zh-CN` | 元素级截图目标。元素机器引用，格式：target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。element_uid 必须来自 get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images 返回的 uid；css_selector 必须能被当前页面 querySelector/querySelectorAll 匹配。示例：uid:t5493.000001.12；uid:t5493.000001.i3；css:button[aria-label="下一页"]；css:a[href*="page=2"]；css:input[name="q"]；css:textarea[placeholder*="评论"]；css:[data-testid="submit"]。不要传按钮文案、页面标题、URL 或自然语言。 | 元素截图目标（不可与 actionable=true 同用）。元素引用（target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>）。element_uid 取自 get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images 的 uid；css_selector 须能被 querySelector/querySelectorAll 匹配（例：uid:t5493.000001.12；uid:t5493.000001.i3；css:button[aria-label="下一页"]；css:a[href*="page=2"]；css:input[name="q"]；css:textarea[placeholder*="评论"]；css:[data-testid="submit"]）。禁止传按钮文案、标题、URL 或自然语言。 |
| param `target` | `en` | Target for an element screenshot. Machine-readable element reference: target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. element_uid must be a uid returned by get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match the current page via querySelector/querySelectorAll. Examples: uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]. Do not pass button text, page titles, URLs, or natural-language descriptions. | Target for an element screenshot (cannot be combined with actionable=true). Element reference (target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>). element_uid comes from get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match via querySelector/querySelectorAll (e.g. uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]). Never pass button text, titles, URLs, or natural language. |
| param `index` | `zh-CN` | target 为 css:... 且匹配多个时的 index，默认 0 | target 为 css:... 且匹配多个元素时的 0-based 序号，默认 0。 |
| param `index` | `en` | Match index when a css:... target matches multiple elements. Default: 0. | 0-based match index when a css:... target matches multiple elements. Default: 0. |
| param `destination` | `zh-CN` | 可选图集表名。传入时把截图额外保存为该图集的 image asset；不传则不保存。 | 可选图集表名；传入时将截图保存为该图集的 image asset。 |
| param `destination` | `en` | Optional image collection table name. If provided, also saves the screenshot as an image asset in that collection; otherwise it is not saved. | Optional gallery table name; when provided, saves the screenshot as an image asset in that gallery. |
| param `format` | `zh-CN` | 默认 jpeg | 图片格式，默认 jpeg。 |
| param `format` | `en` | Default: jpeg. | Image format. Default: jpeg. |
| param `quality` | `zh-CN` | JPEG 质量 0-100，默认 70 | JPEG 质量（0–100），默认 70。 |
| param `quality` | `en` | JPEG quality from 0 to 100. Default: 70. | JPEG quality (0–100). Default: 70. |
| param `padding` | `zh-CN` | 元素截图外扩像素，默认 0，最大 200 | 元素截图四周外扩像素，默认 0，范围 0–200。 |
| param `padding` | `en` | Padding around an element screenshot in pixels. Default: 0; maximum: 200. | Padding around an element screenshot in pixels. Default: 0; range: 0–200. |
| param `maxWidth` | `zh-CN` | 返回图片最大宽度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB | 返回图片最大宽度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB |
| param `maxWidth` | `en` | Maximum returned image width. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. | Maximum returned image width. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. |
| param `maxHeight` | `zh-CN` | 返回图片最大高度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB | 返回图片最大高度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB |
| param `maxHeight` | `en` | Maximum returned image height. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. | Maximum returned image height. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. |
| param `actionable` | `zh-CN` | 是否把当前视口截图作为下一次 act_on_screen 的坐标依据，默认 false；不能与 target 同用 | 是否将该视口截图作为后续 act_on_screen 的坐标依据，默认 false（不可与 target 同用）。 |
| param `actionable` | `en` | Whether to use the current viewport screenshot as the coordinate basis for the next act_on_screen call. Default: false. Cannot be combined with target. | Use this viewport screenshot as the coordinate basis for subsequent act_on_screen calls. Default: false (cannot be combined with target). |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `act_on_screen`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 依据最近一次 screenshot(actionable=true) 或 act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) 建立的坐标状态，直接操作当前任务页面，不查询 DOM。x/y/endX/endY 是返回页面截图上以左上角为原点的原始像素坐标，范围见 coordinateSpace.width/height。不带 x/y 时无需预先截图：press/type 操作当前焦点，scroll 在当前视口中心滚动；传 x/y 时按截图坐标定位，type 先点击再输入；replace=true 且 text 为空表示清空。调用必须串行并显式填写 returnScreenshot：需要观察本次结果时传 true；已验证且无需逐步观察的连续操作传 false。只有 true 才产生观察截图。结果中的 viewportChange 客观记录浏览器视口变化，coordinateBasisReady 表示当前坐标空间能否继续使用。run_script 返回内部最后一次 screenshot 或 act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) 的图片。long_press 会在指定位置持续按住后释放。移动模式自动使用触摸：click/double_click 为轻点/双击，long_press 为长按，drag 为手指滑动；scroll 仍表示滚动内容。移动模式不支持 hover 或鼠标右键/中键。 | 依据最近一次 screenshot(actionable=true) 或 act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) 建立的坐标状态，直接按屏幕坐标或当前焦点操作页面。x/y/endX/endY 使用返回截图左上角为原点的原始像素坐标，范围见 coordinateSpace.width/height。不带 x/y 时无需预先截图：press/type 操作当前焦点，scroll 在当前视口中心滚动；传 x/y 时按截图坐标定位，type 先点击再输入；replace=true 且 text 为空表示清空。调用必须串行并显式填写 returnScreenshot（true 返回操作后观察截图并刷新坐标空间；false 不截图并在页面未跳变时沿用原坐标空间）。viewportChange 客观记录浏览器视口变化，coordinateBasisReady 表示当前坐标空间能否继续使用。移动模式自动转为触摸（click/double_click/long_press/drag），不支持 hover 或右键/中键。 |
| **description** | `en` | Interact directly with the current task’s page using the coordinate state established by the latest screenshot(actionable=true) or act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true), without querying the DOM. x/y/endX/endY are original image-pixel coordinates measured from the top-left corner of the returned page screenshot; see coordinateSpace.width/height for bounds. Without x/y, no prior screenshot is needed: press/type act on the current focus and scroll uses the current viewport center. Supplied x/y use screenshot coordinates; type clicks there before typing. replace=true with empty text clears the field. Calls must be sequential and explicitly include returnScreenshot: use true to observe the result, or false for verified sequences that do not need observation after every step. Only true produces an observation screenshot. viewportChange records browser viewport changes; coordinateBasisReady indicates whether the current coordinate space remains usable. run_script returns the image from its last screenshot or act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) call. long_press holds at the specified position and then releases. Mobile mode uses touch input automatically: click/double_click tap once/twice, long_press holds a finger down, and drag swipes between the endpoints. scroll still scrolls content. Hover and right/middle mouse buttons are unavailable in mobile mode. | Interact directly with the current task's page using the coordinate state established by the latest screenshot(actionable=true) or act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true). x/y/endX/endY are original pixel coordinates measured from the top-left corner of the returned screenshot within coordinateSpace.width/height. Without x/y, no prior screenshot is needed: press/type act on the current focus and scroll scrolls at the viewport center; with x/y, actions target those screenshot coordinates and type clicks before typing; replace=true with empty text clears the field. Calls must be sequential and explicitly set returnScreenshot (true captures a post-action observation screenshot and refreshes the coordinate space; false skips capture and reuses the coordinate space while valid). viewportChange objectively records browser viewport changes; coordinateBasisReady indicates whether the current coordinate space remains usable. Mobile mode uses touch automatically (click/double_click/long_press/drag) and does not support hover or right/middle mouse buttons. |
| param `returnScreenshot` | `zh-CN` | 必须显式填写。需要观察本次操作结果时传 true；已验证且无需逐步观察的连续操作传 false | 必填。需要观察本次操作结果时传 true；已验证且无需逐步观察的连续操作传 false。 |
| param `returnScreenshot` | `en` | Required. Use true to observe the result of this action, or false for verified sequences that do not need observation after every step. | Required. Pass true to observe the result of this action, or false for verified sequences that do not need observation after every step. |
| param `x` | `zh-CN` | 返回图片上的起点横坐标（像素）；scroll 不填时使用当前视口中心，无需预先截图，press/type 可不填 | 截图起点横坐标（像素，与 y 成对；click/double_click/long_press/hover/drag 必填，scroll 缺省取视口中心，press/type 可省略）。 |
| param `x` | `en` | Starting x-coordinate in pixels on the returned image. scroll defaults to the current viewport center without a prior screenshot; optional for press/type. | Starting x-coordinate in image pixels (paired with y; required for click/double_click/long_press/hover/drag, defaults to viewport center for scroll, optional for press/type). |
| param `y` | `zh-CN` | 返回图片上的起点纵坐标（像素）；scroll 不填时使用当前视口中心，无需预先截图，press/type 可不填 | 截图起点纵坐标（像素，与 x 成对；click/double_click/long_press/hover/drag 必填，scroll 缺省取视口中心，press/type 可省略）。 |
| param `y` | `en` | Starting y-coordinate in pixels on the returned image. scroll defaults to the current viewport center without a prior screenshot; optional for press/type. | Starting y-coordinate in image pixels (paired with x; required for click/double_click/long_press/hover/drag, defaults to viewport center for scroll, optional for press/type). |
| param `endX` | `zh-CN` | drag 在返回图片上的终点横坐标（像素） | drag 终点横坐标（像素）。 |
| param `endX` | `en` | Ending x-coordinate for drag, in pixels on the returned image. | Ending x-coordinate in image pixels for drag. |
| param `endY` | `zh-CN` | drag 在返回图片上的终点纵坐标（像素） | drag 终点纵坐标（像素）。 |
| param `endY` | `en` | Ending y-coordinate for drag, in pixels on the returned image. | Ending y-coordinate in image pixels for drag. |
| param `text` | `zh-CN` | type 要输入的文本；replace=true 时可传空字符串表示只清空 | type 输入文本（replace=true 时传 "" 清空）。 |
| param `text` | `en` | Text to enter with type. With replace=true, an empty string clears the field. | Text for type (pass "" with replace=true to clear). |
| param `replace` | `zh-CN` | type 前是否全选并替换当前内容，默认 false；与空 text 合用表示清空 | type 前是否全选并替换现有内容，默认 false。 |
| param `replace` | `en` | Whether type selects all and replaces existing content. Default: false. With empty text, clears the field. | Select all and replace existing content before typing. Default: false. |
| param `key` | `zh-CN` | press 的按键，如 Enter、Escape、Tab、Meta+A、Control+L；不带 x/y 时无需预先截图 | press 按键名，如 Enter、Escape、Tab、Meta+A、Control+L。 |
| param `key` | `en` | Key for press, such as Enter, Escape, Tab, Meta+A, or Control+L. No prior screenshot is needed when x/y are omitted. | Key for press, such as Enter, Escape, Tab, Meta+A, or Control+L. |
| param `button` | `zh-CN` | click/double_click/long_press/drag 使用的鼠标键，默认 left | 鼠标按键（left/right/middle），默认 left。 |
| param `button` | `en` | Mouse button for click/double_click/long_press/drag. Default: left. | Mouse button (left/right/middle). Default: left. |
| param `durationMs` | `zh-CN` | long_press 按住的毫秒数，默认 800，范围 300–5000 | long_press 毫秒数，默认 800，范围 300–5000。 |
| param `durationMs` | `en` | Duration of long_press in milliseconds. Default: 800; range: 300–5000. | Duration of long_press in ms. Default: 800; range: 300–5000. |
| param `deltaX` | `zh-CN` | scroll 水平滚动像素，向右为正 | scroll 水平滚动像素（向右为正，范围 -10000–10000）。 |
| param `deltaX` | `en` | Horizontal scroll distance in pixels. Positive values scroll right. | Horizontal scroll pixels (positive right, range -10000–10000). |
| param `deltaY` | `zh-CN` | scroll 垂直滚动像素，向下为正 | scroll 垂直滚动像素（向下为正，范围 -10000–10000）。 |
| param `deltaY` | `en` | Vertical scroll distance in pixels. Positive values scroll down. | Vertical scroll pixels (positive down, range -10000–10000). |
| param `repeat` | `zh-CN` | scroll 在同一坐标连续发送的滚轮事件数，默认 1，最大 20 | scroll 连续滚轮次数，默认 1，范围 1–20。 |
| param `repeat` | `en` | Number of consecutive wheel events at the same coordinates. Default: 1; maximum: 20. | Consecutive wheel events for scroll. Default: 1; range: 1–20. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `list_images`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 发现目标页面中的图片候选，只返回简短的 alt、title、可访问名称、说明文字、附近标题与正文、链接语义、尺寸、来源摘要和位置等元信息。每个候选都会返回绑定当前 session、标签页、页面和图片地址的 target；在该页面上下文中完成判断后，把合格候选的 target 原样交给 read_image 或 collect_images。index/rank 仅用于阅读候选列表。 | 扫描目标页面中的图片候选，返回 alt、title、context（可访问名称/说明、图注、附近标题与正文、链接文本与 URL）、尺寸、位置、来源摘要，以及绑定当前 session、标签页、页面和图片地址的 target。在当前页面完成筛选后，将选中候选的 target 原样传给 read_image 或 collect_images；index/rank 仅用于阅读列表。 |
| **description** | `en` | Discover image candidates on the target page. Returns only brief metadata: alt text, title, accessible name, captions, nearby headings and text, link context, dimensions, source summary, and position. Each candidate includes a target bound to the current session, tab, page, and image URL. Evaluate candidates in that page context, then pass selected targets unchanged to read_image or collect_images. index/rank are for reading the candidate list only. | Discover image candidates on the target page, returning alt, title, context (accessible name/description, caption, nearby heading and text, link text and URL), dimensions, position, source summary, and a target bound to the current session, tab, page, and image URL. Evaluate candidates in the current page context and pass selected targets unchanged to read_image or collect_images; index/rank are for reading the list only. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `selector` | `zh-CN` | 可选容器或 img CSS selector；也可传 target 格式 css:... / uid:...；不填扫描整个页面 | 可选容器或 img 选择器；可传原始 CSS，也可传 target 格式 css:... / uid:...；省略时扫描整页。 |
| param `selector` | `en` | Optional container or img CSS selector, or a css:... / uid:... target. If omitted, scans the whole page. | Optional container or img selector. Accepts raw CSS or a target in css:... / uid:... format; scans the whole page if omitted. |
| param `minWidth` | `zh-CN` | 最小图片宽度，按显示宽度或 naturalWidth 判断，默认 1 | 最小宽度（按显示宽度与 naturalWidth 较大值判断），默认 1。 |
| param `minWidth` | `en` | Minimum image width, checked against displayed width or naturalWidth. Default: 1. | Minimum width (checked against the larger of displayed width and naturalWidth). Default: 1. |
| param `minHeight` | `zh-CN` | 最小图片高度，按显示高度或 naturalHeight 判断，默认 1 | 最小高度（按显示高度与 naturalHeight 较大值判断），默认 1。 |
| param `minHeight` | `en` | Minimum image height, checked against displayed height or naturalHeight. Default: 1. | Minimum height (checked against the larger of displayed height and naturalHeight). Default: 1. |
| param `visibleOnly` | `zh-CN` | 是否只列出可见图片，默认 true | 是否只列出可见图片，默认 true。 |
| param `visibleOnly` | `en` | Whether to list visible images only. Default: true. | List visible images only. Default: true. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 最多返回多少张，默认 100，最大 1000 | 最多返回图片数，默认 100，范围 1–1000。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum number of images to return. Default: 100; maximum: 1000. | Maximum images to return. Default: 100; range: 1–1000. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `collect_images`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 采集已经选定的图片：保存为本地 image assets，并把元信息写入图集 Data 表。页面候选通过 targets 逐张指定，并在发现它们的当前页面上下文中及时采集；直接图片资源通过 urls 逐张指定，并按当前来源上下文采集。同一任务可多次调用并持续写入同一个 table。工具会在写入前解析全部 target，并核对候选页面和图片地址；返回摘要和 assetId。 | 将已选定的页面图片（targets）或图片 URL（urls）保存为本地 image assets 并写入图集 Data 表（targets 与 urls 至少提供一项，单次合计最多 200 项）。页面候选须在离开或切换发现它的页面前及时采集；同一任务可多次调用并追加写入同一个 table。返回采集统计和 assetIds。 |
| **description** | `en` | Collect selected images: save them as local image assets and write metadata to an image collection Data table. Specify page candidates individually with targets and collect them while still in the page context where they were discovered. Specify direct image resources individually with urls and collect them in the current source context. The same task can call this tool repeatedly to append to the same table. Before writing, the tool resolves all targets and verifies the candidate page and image URL. Returns a summary and assetId values. | Save selected page images (targets) or direct image URLs (urls) as local image assets and write metadata to a gallery Data table (provide at least one of targets or urls, up to 200 items total per call). Collect page candidates before leaving or switching away from the page where they were found; call repeatedly across tabs to append to the same table. Returns collection counts and assetIds. |
| param `table` | `zh-CN` | 图集表名，必填。用于稳定写入和后续 data_store list/read_image_asset 验证；极端缺失时 runtime 会自动生成随机图集名兜底。 | 必填。目标图集表名，用于持续写入及后续 data_store list / read_image_asset 复核。 |
| param `table` | `en` | Required image collection table name. Use a consistent name for writes and subsequent verification with data_store list/read_image_asset. The runtime generates a random fallback name if this value is missing. | Required gallery table name, used for appending and subsequent verification via data_store list or read_image_asset. |
| param `targets` | `zh-CN` | 选中页面图片的 target 列表。每项原样使用 list_images 返回的 target。元素机器引用，格式：target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。element_uid 必须来自 get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images 返回的 uid；css_selector 必须能被当前页面 querySelector/querySelectorAll 匹配。示例：uid:t5493.000001.12；uid:t5493.000001.i3；css:button[aria-label="下一页"]；css:a[href*="page=2"]；css:input[name="q"]；css:textarea[placeholder*="评论"]；css:[data-testid="submit"]。不要传按钮文案、页面标题、URL 或自然语言。 | 选中的页面图片 target 数组，原样传入 list_images 返回的 target。元素引用（target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>）。element_uid 取自 get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images 的 uid；css_selector 须能被 querySelector/querySelectorAll 匹配（例：uid:t5493.000001.12；uid:t5493.000001.i3；css:button[aria-label="下一页"]；css:a[href*="page=2"]；css:input[name="q"]；css:textarea[placeholder*="评论"]；css:[data-testid="submit"]）。禁止传按钮文案、标题、URL 或自然语言。 |
| param `targets` | `en` | Targets of selected page images. Pass each target from list_images unchanged. Machine-readable element reference: target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. element_uid must be a uid returned by get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match the current page via querySelector/querySelectorAll. Examples: uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]. Do not pass button text, page titles, URLs, or natural-language descriptions. | Selected page image targets array; pass each target from list_images unchanged. Element reference (target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>). element_uid comes from get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match via querySelector/querySelectorAll (e.g. uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]). Never pass button text, titles, URLs, or natural language. |
| param `urls` | `zh-CN` | 选中图片的直接 URL 列表，支持 http/https/data:image。 | 选中的直接图片 URL 数组（支持 http/https/data:image）。 |
| param `urls` | `en` | Direct URLs of selected images. Supports http/https/data:image. | Selected direct image URLs array (supports http/https/data:image). |
| param `dedupField` | `zh-CN` | 图集表 upsert 去重字段，默认 sourceKey。 | 图集表 upsert 去重字段，默认 sourceKey。 |
| param `dedupField` | `en` | Deduplication field for upsert into the collection table. Default: sourceKey. | Deduplication field for gallery table upserts. Default: sourceKey. |
| param `maxWidth` | `zh-CN` | 读取/存储图片最大宽度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB。 | 读取/存储图片最大宽度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB。 |
| param `maxWidth` | `en` | Maximum image width for reading/storage. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. | Maximum image width for reading/storage. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. |
| param `maxHeight` | `zh-CN` | 读取/存储图片最大高度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB。 | 读取/存储图片最大高度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB。 |
| param `maxHeight` | `en` | Maximum image height for reading/storage. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. | Maximum image height for reading/storage. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. |
| param `maxBytes` | `zh-CN` | 单张 URL 图片最大读取字节数，默认 20 MiB，最大 32 MiB；编码后图片不超过 2 MiB。 | 单张 URL 图片最大读取字节数，默认 20 MiB，最大 32 MiB；编码后图片不超过 2 MiB。 |
| param `maxBytes` | `en` | Maximum bytes to read per URL image. Default: 20 MiB; maximum: 32 MiB. Encoded output is at most 2 MiB. | Maximum bytes to read per URL image. Default: 20 MiB; maximum: 32 MiB. Encoded output is at most 2 MiB. |
| param `maxImages` | `zh-CN` | 本地 asset store 最大图片张数，默认 2000。 | 本地 asset store 最大图片张数，默认 2000。 |
| param `maxImages` | `en` | Maximum number of images in the local asset store. Default: 2000. | Maximum images in the local asset store. Default: 2000. |
| param `maxStorageBytes` | `zh-CN` | 本地 asset store 最大总字节数，默认 1.5 GiB。 | 本地 asset store 最大总字节数，默认 1.5 GiB。 |
| param `maxStorageBytes` | `en` | Maximum total bytes in the local asset store. Default: 1.5 GiB. | Maximum total bytes in the local asset store. Default: 1.5 GiB. |
| param `format` | `zh-CN` | 页面侧转码格式，默认 jpeg。 | 转码格式，默认 jpeg。 |
| param `format` | `en` | Image format for conversion in the page. Default: jpeg. | Output image format. Default: jpeg. |
| param `quality` | `zh-CN` | jpeg/webp 质量，0-1 或 1-100，默认 0.88。 | jpeg/webp 质量（0–1 或 1–100），默认 0.88。 |
| param `quality` | `en` | JPEG/WebP quality, from 0–1 or 1–100. Default: 0.88. | JPEG/WebP quality (0–1 or 1–100). Default: 0.88. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `read_image_asset`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取已保存的本地图片 asset，并作为图片消息返回给 LLM。适合从 collect_images 采集后的图集中挑选少量图片做视觉分析。 | 按 assetId 读取已保存在本地图集中的单张图片并作为图片消息返回给 LLM，用于抽检或复核已采集图片的视觉内容。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read a saved local image asset and return it to the model as an image message. Useful for visually analyzing a small selection from a collection created by collect_images. | Read a saved local image asset by assetId and return it to the model as an image message to inspect or verify collected images. |
| param `assetId` | `zh-CN` | collect_images 返回或图集表中的 assetId。 | collect_images、screenshot(destination) 或图集表行中的 assetId。 |
| param `assetId` | `en` | assetId returned by collect_images or stored in the collection table. | assetId returned by collect_images, screenshot(destination), or a gallery table row. |
| param `detail` | `zh-CN` | 传给视觉模型的图片细节等级，默认 auto。 | 视觉细节等级（low/auto/high），默认 auto。 |
| param `detail` | `en` | Image detail level sent to the vision model. Default: auto. | Vision detail level (low/auto/high). Default: auto. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `read_image`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取图片并作为图片消息返回给 LLM，url 或 target 二选一。传 url 读取图片资源；传页面 img 或图片容器的 target 读取已经加载的 DOM 图片。页面候选满足采集条件时，在切换页面上下文前把同一个 target 交给 collect_images。用于分析单张图片的视觉内容。target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。 | 读取单张图片并作为图片消息返回给 LLM，url 或 target 二选一：传 target 读取页面已加载的 img/图片容器（满足采集条件时，在切换页面前将同一 target 传给 collect_images）；传 url 读取 http/https/data:image 图片地址。target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read an image and return it to the model as an image message. Provide either url or target. Use url to read an image resource, or the target of a page img/image container to read an already loaded DOM image. If a page candidate meets the collection criteria, pass the same target to collect_images before switching page context. Use to analyze the visual content of an individual image. target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. | Read an individual image and return it to the model as an image message. Provide either url or target: pass target to inspect a loaded page img/container (if selected for collection, pass the same target to collect_images before switching pages), or pass url to read an http/https/data:image resource. target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. |
| param `url` | `zh-CN` | 图片 URL。支持 http/https/data:image。传 url 时不需要 tabId/target。 | 图片 URL（支持 http/https/data:image）；传 url 时无需 tabId/target。 |
| param `url` | `en` | Image URL. Supports http/https/data:image. tabId/target are not needed when url is provided. | Image URL (supports http/https/data:image); tabId and target are not needed when url is provided. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页，target 为 uid:... 时可省略。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页（target 为 uid:... 时也可省略）。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab; may be omitted for a uid:... target. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab (optional when target is uid:...). |
| param `target` | `zh-CN` | 页面 img 或图片容器目标。元素机器引用，格式：target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。element_uid 必须来自 get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images 返回的 uid；css_selector 必须能被当前页面 querySelector/querySelectorAll 匹配。示例：uid:t5493.000001.12；uid:t5493.000001.i3；css:button[aria-label="下一页"]；css:a[href*="page=2"]；css:input[name="q"]；css:textarea[placeholder*="评论"]；css:[data-testid="submit"]。不要传按钮文案、页面标题、URL 或自然语言。 | 页面 img 或图片容器的 target。元素引用（target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>）。element_uid 取自 get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images 的 uid；css_selector 须能被 querySelector/querySelectorAll 匹配（例：uid:t5493.000001.12；uid:t5493.000001.i3；css:button[aria-label="下一页"]；css:a[href*="page=2"]；css:input[name="q"]；css:textarea[placeholder*="评论"]；css:[data-testid="submit"]）。禁止传按钮文案、标题、URL 或自然语言。 |
| param `target` | `en` | Target of a page img or image container. Machine-readable element reference: target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. element_uid must be a uid returned by get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match the current page via querySelector/querySelectorAll. Examples: uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]. Do not pass button text, page titles, URLs, or natural-language descriptions. | Target of a page img or image container. Element reference (target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>). element_uid comes from get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match via querySelector/querySelectorAll (e.g. uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]). Never pass button text, titles, URLs, or natural language. |
| param `index` | `zh-CN` | target 为 css:... 且匹配多个时的 index，默认 0 | target 为 css:... 且匹配多个元素时的 0-based 序号，默认 0。 |
| param `index` | `en` | Match index when a css:... target matches multiple elements. Default: 0. | 0-based match index when a css:... target matches multiple elements. Default: 0. |
| param `detail` | `zh-CN` | 传给视觉模型的图片细节等级，默认 auto | 视觉细节等级（low/auto/high），默认 auto。 |
| param `detail` | `en` | Image detail level sent to the vision model. Default: auto. | Vision detail level (low/auto/high). Default: auto. |
| param `maxWidth` | `zh-CN` | 返回图片最大宽度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB | 返回图片最大宽度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB |
| param `maxWidth` | `en` | Maximum returned image width. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. | Maximum returned image width. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. |
| param `maxHeight` | `zh-CN` | 返回图片最大高度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB | 返回图片最大高度，默认及上限 2048；编码后不超过 2 MiB |
| param `maxHeight` | `en` | Maximum returned image height. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. | Maximum returned image height. Default and ceiling: 2048; encoded output is at most 2 MiB. |
| param `maxBytes` | `zh-CN` | URL 图片最大读取字节数，默认 20 MiB，最大 32 MiB；编码后图片不超过 2 MiB | URL 图片最大读取字节数，默认 20 MiB，最大 32 MiB；编码后图片不超过 2 MiB |
| param `maxBytes` | `en` | Maximum bytes to read from a URL image. Default: 20 MiB; maximum: 32 MiB. Encoded output is at most 2 MiB. | Maximum bytes to read from a URL image. Default: 20 MiB; maximum: 32 MiB. Encoded output is at most 2 MiB. |
| param `format` | `zh-CN` | 页面侧转码格式，默认 png。png 可保留透明通道，避免透明图片转 jpeg 后变黑。 | 转码格式，默认 png（png 可保留透明通道，避免透明背景转 jpeg 后变黑）。 |
| param `format` | `en` | Image format for conversion in the page. Default: png. PNG preserves transparency, avoiding black backgrounds when transparent images are converted to JPEG. | Output image format. Default: png (png preserves transparency and avoids black backgrounds on transparent images). |
| param `quality` | `zh-CN` | jpeg/webp 质量，0-1 或 1-100，默认 0.88 | jpeg/webp 质量（0–1 或 1–100），默认 0.88。 |
| param `quality` | `en` | JPEG/WebP quality, from 0–1 or 1–100. Default: 0.88. | JPEG/WebP quality (0–1 or 1–100). Default: 0.88. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `read_article`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取目标页面经过整理的正文 Markdown。正文抽取会组合规则清噪、Readability 对照与失败恢复，并保留标题、列表、链接、代码和表格结构；适合文章、文档、搜索结果摘要、长文本页面。每次只返回 offset/limit 指定的正文片段，并返回 totalChars/hasMore/nextOffset/sourceSelector/linkCount；outline 是可定位目录，条目带 offset/endOffset/charCount，需要读取某章节时用对应 offset 和 limit 继续调用 read_article；需要 DOM 结构证据时，用 sourceSelector 继续调用 read_dom。limit 必填，建议 4000-8000，最大 20000。读正文优先使用本工具。 | 读取目标页面整理后的正文 Markdown（保留标题、列表、链接、代码和表格，也支持纯文本或原始 JSON 页面）。读正文优先使用本工具，不要用于寻找可操作控件。返回 offset/limit 指定的正文片段及 totalChars/hasMore/nextOffset/sourceSelector/linkCount；长文用 nextOffset 续读。outline 为带 offset/endOffset/charCount 的目录，可按 offset 和 limit 读指定章节；需原始 DOM 证据时把 sourceSelector 传给 read_dom。limit 必填，建议 4000–8000，范围 1000–20000。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read cleaned main content from the target page as Markdown. Extraction combines rule-based cleanup, Readability comparison, and failure recovery while preserving headings, lists, links, code, and tables. Suitable for articles, documents, search-result summaries, and long text pages. Each call returns only the segment specified by offset/limit, along with totalChars/hasMore/nextOffset/sourceSelector/linkCount. outline is a navigable table of contents whose entries include offset/endOffset/charCount; use the corresponding offset and limit in another read_article call to read a section. For DOM evidence, pass sourceSelector to read_dom. limit is required; 4000–8000 is recommended, with a maximum of 20000. Prefer this tool for reading text. | Read cleaned main content from the target page as Markdown (preserving headings, lists, links, code, and tables; also works on plain-text or raw JSON pages). Prefer this tool for reading body text, not for finding interactive controls. Returns the offset/limit text segment plus totalChars/hasMore/nextOffset/sourceSelector/linkCount; pass nextOffset to continue reading. outline lists sections with offset/endOffset/charCount; pass sourceSelector to read_dom when raw DOM evidence is needed. limit is required (recommended 4000–8000, range 1000–20000). |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `source` | `zh-CN` | 读取来源，默认 auto：选中文本优先，其次自动整理正文，最后 main/body | 读取来源，默认 auto（依次尝试 selection、article、selector、body）。 |
| param `source` | `en` | Content source. Default: auto, which prefers selected text, then extracted main content, then main/body. | Content source. Default: auto (tries selection, article, selector, then body). |
| param `selector` | `zh-CN` | source=selector 时读取的范围；可传原始 CSS，也可传 target 格式 css:... / uid:... | source=selector 必填（auto 可选）的范围；支持原始 CSS 或 target 格式 css:... / uid:...。 |
| param `selector` | `en` | Scope to read when source=selector. Accepts a raw CSS selector or a css:... / uid:... target. | Scope for source=selector (optional for auto). Accepts raw CSS or a target in css:... / uid:... format. |
| param `offset` | `zh-CN` | 正文字符起始 offset，默认 0 | 起始字符 offset，默认 0；续读传 nextOffset，读章节传 outline 的 offset。 |
| param `offset` | `en` | Starting character offset in the main text. Default: 0. | Starting character offset. Default: 0. Pass nextOffset to continue or an outline offset for a section. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 必填。返回字符数，建议 4000-8000，最大 20000；继续读下一段时传上次返回的 nextOffset | 必填。返回字符数，建议 4000–8000，范围 1000–20000。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Required number of characters to return. Recommended: 4000–8000; maximum: 20000. To continue reading, pass nextOffset from the previous result. | Required character count to return. Recommended: 4000–8000; range: 1000–20000. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `read_dom`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 分页读取目标页面的实时 DOM HTML 原始片段。返回原始 HTML 和行号，支持指定起始行和行数进行分段读取，总是返回 totalLines 方便后续继续读取。用于读取 find_elements 返回的 line/lineRange，或验证隐藏字段、form/action、id/class/data-testid、p/br 等 DOM 结构。 | 按行分页读取目标页面的实时 DOM HTML（剔除 script/style/noscript/svg），返回带 1-indexed 行号的 content、totalLines 和 hasMore。用于读取 find_elements 的 lineRange、read_article 的 sourceSelector，或验证隐藏字段、form/action、id/class/data-testid、p/br 等 DOM 结构。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read paginated raw HTML from the target page’s live DOM. Returns raw HTML with line numbers, accepts a starting line and line count, and always returns totalLines for continued reading. Use to read line/lineRange returned by find_elements, or to verify DOM structure such as hidden fields, form/action, id/class/data-testid, or p/br. | Read paginated live DOM HTML by line (script/style/noscript/svg removed), returning 1-indexed numbered content, totalLines, and hasMore. Use to inspect lineRange from find_elements, sourceSelector from read_article, or DOM structure such as hidden fields, form/action, id/class/data-testid, and p/br. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `offset` | `zh-CN` | 起始行号（1-indexed），默认 1 | 起始行号（1-indexed），默认 1。 |
| param `offset` | `en` | Starting line number, 1-indexed. Default: 1. | Starting line number (1-indexed). Default: 1. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 读取行数，默认 200 | 读取行数，默认 200。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Number of lines to read. Default: 200. | Number of lines to read. Default: 200. |
| param `selector` | `zh-CN` | 限定读取范围，默认 body；可传原始 CSS，也可传 target 格式 css:... / uid:... | 读取范围，默认 body；支持原始 CSS 或 target 格式 css:... / uid:...。 |
| param `selector` | `en` | Scope to read. Default: body. Accepts a raw CSS selector or a css:... / uid:... target. | Scope to read. Default: body. Accepts raw CSS or a target in css:... / uid:... format. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `find_elements`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 按范围、语义和可选文本正则查找页面元素候选，并优先排序可操作、可见、匹配更明确的元素。默认用于定位按钮、链接、输入框、列表项，也可查找普通文本/容器；pattern 是可选过滤条件：不传 pattern 时会列出 selector/tags/roles/types 命中的候选。返回可直接用于 inspect_element/act_on_element 的 target，以及 stable selector、role/name/text/rect/actionable/inViewport/lineRange 等摘要。需要原始 DOM 时用 read_dom 读取对应行号。 | 按范围、语义属性和可选正则查找页面元素候选（优先排序可操作、视口内、匹配更明确的元素）。pattern 是可选过滤条件：不传 pattern 时会列出 selector/tags/roles/types 命中的候选。返回可直接用于 inspect_element/act_on_element 的 target，以及 selector、role/name/text/rect/actionable/inViewport/lineRange 等摘要；需原始 HTML 时将 lineRange 传给 read_dom。 |
| **description** | `en` | Find candidate page elements by scope, semantics, and an optional text regular expression. Prioritizes actionable, visible, and more specific matches. Commonly used for buttons, links, inputs, and list items; also supports plain text and containers. pattern is an optional filter: without it, lists candidates matching selector/tags/roles/types. Returns a target usable directly with inspect_element/act_on_element, plus summaries including a stable selector, role/name/text/rect/actionable/inViewport/lineRange. For raw DOM, pass the corresponding line numbers to read_dom. | Find candidate page elements by scope, semantic filters, and an optional regex (prioritizing actionable, in-viewport, and specific matches). pattern is an optional filter: when omitted, lists candidates matching selector/tags/roles/types. Returns a target usable directly with inspect_element/act_on_element, plus selector, role/name/text/rect/actionable/inViewport/lineRange summaries; pass lineRange to read_dom for raw HTML. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `pattern` | `zh-CN` | 可选 JavaScript RegExp pattern，用于匹配文本、aria、placeholder、class、id、href 等；不传则列出范围内候选元素。 | 可选正则，匹配 name、aria-label、placeholder、value、id、name/title/data-testid、href、text、role、tag、class。 |
| param `pattern` | `en` | Optional JavaScript RegExp pattern to match text, aria, placeholder, class, id, href, and similar fields. If omitted, lists candidates within the scope. | Optional regex matched against name, aria-label, placeholder, value, id, name/title/data-testid, href, text, role, tag, and class. |
| param `flags` | `zh-CN` | RegExp flags，默认 i | RegExp flags，默认 i。 |
| param `flags` | `en` | RegExp flags. Default: i. | RegExp flags. Default: i. |
| param `selector` | `zh-CN` | 限定查找范围，默认 body；可传原始 CSS，也可传 target 格式 css:... / uid:... | 查找范围（穿透 open Shadow DOM），默认 body；支持原始 CSS 或 target 格式 css:... / uid:...。 |
| param `selector` | `en` | Search scope. Default: body. Accepts a raw CSS selector or a css:... / uid:... target. | Search scope (pierces open Shadow DOM). Default: body. Accepts raw CSS or a target in css:... / uid:... format. |
| param `roles` | `zh-CN` | 可选 role 过滤，如 button/link/textbox/checkbox | 可选 ARIA role 过滤（含显式与隐式 role），如 button/link/textbox/checkbox。 |
| param `roles` | `en` | Optional role filter, such as button/link/textbox/checkbox. | Optional ARIA role filter (explicit and implicit), such as button/link/textbox/checkbox. |
| param `tags` | `zh-CN` | 可选 tag 过滤，如 button/a/input/div | 可选 tag 过滤，如 button/a/input/div。 |
| param `tags` | `en` | Optional tag filter, such as button/a/input/div. | Optional HTML tag filter, such as button/a/input/div. |
| param `types` | `zh-CN` | 可选 input type 过滤，如 text/search/submit | 可选 type 属性过滤，如 text/search/submit/file。 |
| param `types` | `en` | Optional input type filter, such as text/search/submit. | Optional type attribute filter, such as text/search/submit/file. |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | 最多返回数量，默认 20 | 最多返回数量，默认 20，范围 1–100。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum number of results. Default: 20. | Maximum results. Default: 20; range: 1–100. |
| param `includeHidden` | `zh-CN` | 是否包含不可见元素，默认 false | 是否包含不可见元素，默认 false。 |
| param `includeHidden` | `en` | Whether to include hidden elements. Default: false. | Include hidden elements. Default: false. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |
