| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `act_on_element`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 对元素执行高层动作。常规操作只用这个工具：click=真实点击按钮/链接/自定义选项（移动模式自动使用触摸）；fill=填写输入框/textarea/contenteditable，contenteditable 多行文本会尝试按富文本段落插入并返回 p/br/block 结构；press=按键如 Enter；select=选择原生 <select> 的 option（优先精确匹配 option.value，否则匹配可见 label，label 忽略首尾空白）；check/uncheck=勾选；scroll=滚到元素。必须先从 get_ax_tree/find_elements 获取 target。本地文件选择通过 wait_for_user 交给用户完成。target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。失败会返回元素诊断和可选候选。 | 对页面元素执行高层动作：click=点击按钮/链接/控件（移动模式自动用触摸）；fill=填写 input/textarea/contenteditable（空字符串清空；contenteditable 多行文本按段落插入并返回 p/br/block 结构）；press=按键（如 Enter）；select=选择原生 <select> 选项（优先精确匹配 option.value，否则匹配忽略首尾空白的可见 label）；check/uncheck=切换原生 checkbox/radio（radio 不可 uncheck）；scroll=滚到该元素。先从 get_ax_tree 或 find_elements 获取 target。本地文件选择通过 wait_for_user 交给用户完成。target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。 |
| **description** | `en` | Perform a high-level action on an element. Use this tool for routine interactions: click sends a real click to a button/link/custom option, using touch in mobile mode; fill enters text into an input/textarea/contenteditable, attempting rich-text paragraphs for multiline contenteditable text and returning p/br/block structure; press sends a key such as Enter; select chooses an option in a native <select> by exact option.value first, or by visible label (surrounding whitespace ignored) if no value matches; check/uncheck toggles a checkbox; scroll scrolls to an element. Obtain the target from get_ax_tree/find_elements first. Use wait_for_user for the user to select local files. target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. Failures return element diagnostics and optional alternatives. | Perform a high-level action on a page element: click clicks a button/link/control (uses touch in mobile mode); fill enters text into an input/textarea/contenteditable ("" clears; multiline contenteditable text inserts paragraphs and returns p/br/block structure); press sends a key (e.g. Enter); select chooses a native <select> option by exact option.value first, or by visible label (surrounding whitespace ignored) if no value matches (exact value takes priority); check/uncheck toggles a native checkbox/radio (radio cannot be unchecked); scroll scrolls the element into view. Obtain target from get_ax_tree or find_elements first. Use wait_for_user for local file selection. target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `target` | `zh-CN` | 元素机器引用，格式：target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>。element_uid 必须来自 get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images 返回的 uid；css_selector 必须能被当前页面 querySelector/querySelectorAll 匹配。示例：uid:t5493.000001.12；uid:t5493.000001.i3；css:button[aria-label="下一页"]；css:a[href*="page=2"]；css:input[name="q"]；css:textarea[placeholder*="评论"]；css:[data-testid="submit"]。不要传按钮文案、页面标题、URL 或自然语言。 | 元素引用（target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>）。element_uid 取自 get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images 的 uid；css_selector 须能被 querySelector/querySelectorAll 匹配（例：uid:t5493.000001.12；uid:t5493.000001.i3；css:button[aria-label="下一页"]；css:a[href*="page=2"]；css:input[name="q"]；css:textarea[placeholder*="评论"]；css:[data-testid="submit"]）。禁止传按钮文案、标题、URL 或自然语言。 |
| param `target` | `en` | Machine-readable element reference: target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>. element_uid must be a uid returned by get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match the current page via querySelector/querySelectorAll. Examples: uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]. Do not pass button text, page titles, URLs, or natural-language descriptions. | Element reference (target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>). element_uid comes from get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match via querySelector/querySelectorAll (e.g. uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]). Never pass button text, titles, URLs, or natural language. |
| param `index` | `zh-CN` | 匹配多个时的 index，默认 0 | css:... 匹配多个时的 0-based 序号，默认 0。 |
| param `index` | `en` | Match index when multiple elements match. Default: 0. | 0-based match index when css:... matches multiple elements. Default: 0. |
| param `value` | `zh-CN` | fill 的文本，或原生 select 的 option.value / 可见 label（精确 value 优先）；press 也可用作 key。例如 fill 搜索词，press Enter | fill 文本（"" 清空），或 select 的 option.value / 可见 label（精确 value 优先）；press 缺省 key 时也作按键名。 |
| param `value` | `en` | Text for fill, or an option.value / visible label for a native select (exact value takes priority). Can also supply the key for press. For example, fill a search query, then press Enter. | Text for fill ("" clears), or option.value / visible label for select (exact value takes priority); also used as the key for press if key is omitted. |
| param `key` | `zh-CN` | press 的按键，如 Enter/Escape/Tab/ArrowDown。press 时 key 或 value 必填 | press 按键名，如 Enter/Escape/Tab/ArrowDown（press 时 key 或 value 必填）。 |
| param `key` | `en` | Key for press, such as Enter/Escape/Tab/ArrowDown. press requires either key or value. | Key for press, such as Enter/Escape/Tab/ArrowDown (press requires key or value). |
| param `submit` | `zh-CN` | fill 后是否发送 Enter，默认 false；页面可以拦截按键，因此不保证提交成功。也可在检查输入后单独 press Enter | fill 后是否紧接发送 Enter，默认 false。 |
| param `submit` | `en` | Send Enter after filling. Default: false. Page handlers may prevent submission. You can also inspect the field and then press Enter separately. | Press Enter after fill completes. Default: false. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

Examples: uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]. Do not pass button text, page titles, URLs, or natural-language descriptions. | Element target to scroll into view. Element reference (target = "uid:" <element_uid> \| "css:" <css_selector>). element_uid comes from get_ax_tree/find_elements/list_images; css_selector must match via querySelector/querySelectorAll (e.g. uid:t5493.000001.12; uid:t5493.000001.i3; css:button[aria-label="Next page"]; css:a[href*="page=2"]; css:input[name="q"]; css:textarea[placeholder*="Comment"]; css:[data-testid="submit"]). Never pass button text, titles, URLs, or natural language. |

### `run_skill_script`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 内部工具：执行已加载 skill/plugin 包内的脚本资源。不要直接暴露给模型；Skill 工具通过该工具复用 run_script 的 page/inner 执行桥接能力。page 必须使用当前任务已拥有的网页 tab；inner 运行在轻舟内部 Worker，不需要网页 tab。 | 内部工具：执行已加载 Skill 包内的脚本资源（模型不直接调用）。page 必须使用当前任务已拥有的网页 tab；inner 运行在轻舟内部 Worker，不需要网页 tab。 |
| **description** | `en` | Internal tool: execute a script resource from a loaded Skill package. Do not expose directly to the model. Skill tools use this tool to reuse run_script’s page/inner execution bridge. page requires a web tab already owned by the current task; inner runs in qz’s internal Worker and needs no web tab. | Internal tool: execute a script resource from a loaded Skill package (not called directly by the model). page requires a web tab owned by the current task; inner runs in qz's internal Worker without a web tab. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 必填。脚本硬超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 | 必填。脚本超时秒数；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Required script timeout in seconds. <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. | Required script timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. |
| param `target` | `zh-CN` | 执行目标。不填或 page=当前任务网页；inner=轻舟内部 Worker。 | 执行目标：省略或 page=当前任务网页；inner=内部 Worker。 |
| param `target` | `en` | Execution target. Omitted or page: the current task’s web page. inner: qz’s internal Worker. | Execution target: omitted or page for the current task's web page; inner for the internal Worker. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务 workingTabId。 | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务 workingTabId。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s workingTabId. | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s workingTabId. |
| param `skill` | `zh-CN` | Skill 名称。 | Skill 名称。 |
| param `skill` | `en` | Skill name. | Skill name. |
| param `script` | `zh-CN` | Skill 包中的脚本路径，例如 scripts/collect-feed.js。 | Skill 包内脚本相对路径，如 scripts/collect-feed.js。 |
| param `script` | `en` | Script path within the Skill package, such as scripts/collect-feed.js. | Relative script path in the Skill package, such as scripts/collect-feed.js. |
| param `args` | `zh-CN` | 传给脚本的参数对象，脚本中通过 input 或 args 读取。 | 传给脚本的参数对象（脚本内通过 input 或 args 读取）。 |
| param `args` | `en` | Arguments passed to the script, available as input or args. | Arguments object passed to the script (read via input or args). |
| param `mutatesPage` | `zh-CN` | 脚本是否会修改页面 DOM、表单或页面状态。默认 false；仅当确实修改页面时设为 true。 | 脚本是否会修改页面 DOM、表单或状态，默认 false。 |
| param `mutatesPage` | `en` | Whether the script modifies the page DOM, forms, or page state. Default: false. Set true only when it actually modifies the page. | Whether the script modifies page DOM, forms, or state. Default: false. |

### `run_script`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 执行临时 JavaScript 代码，支持 top-level await。只接收 code；skill/plugin 包内脚本必须通过对应 Skill 工具调用。脚本中可访问 input/args、tools/tool、trace/log；页面模式还可访问 tabId/document/window。tools 是当前运行配置下可用工具的动态代理，可直接调用 await tools.find_elements({...})、await tools.act_on_screen({...})、await tools.data_store({...}) 等；不要用 Object.keys(tools) 判断能力，诊断时使用 await tools.$list()。<br><br>运行目标协议：<br>- target 不填或 target:"page" 表示页面模式，使用显式的当前任务拥有 tabId，或当前任务 workingTabId。<br>- target:"inner" 表示轻舟内部 offscreen 运行环境，只用于与页面无关的纯 JS、data_store 批处理、save_file/document_*、ask_llm 等内部工具编排；不会读取目标网页 DOM，也不会自动使用浏览器当前激活标签页。<br>- open_tab 创建新的工作标签页；list_tabs 返回网页标签页状态；use_tab 设置工作标签页。<br>- 页面模式不能操作其他任务拥有的 tab；只读查看其他任务 tab 时用只读页面工具显式传 tabId，不要用 run_script。<br><br>可靠性协议：<br>- 每次调用必须填写 timeout，单位秒；timeout <= 0 表示无限等待，timeout > 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。轻量读取可选几十秒；明确长采集、翻页遍历、批量落库任务可选 0，由用户停止。<br>- 页面模式语义固定为网页 JavaScript 上下文；CDP/页面上下文不可用时返回 page_context_unavailable。inner 模式是 Worker 环境，没有 document/window/DOM。<br>- 不能用 getEventListeners、$0、copy、monitorEvents 等 DevTools Console helper；页面模式用标准 document/window/Web APIs 和轻舟 tools；inner 模式用标准 JS/Web Worker APIs 和轻舟 tools。<br>- 默认把 code 写成单个 async () => { ... } 函数表达式；run_script 会自动调用它。不要写 IIFE、main();、函数外 return。<br>- 脚本开始处用一行注释简明说明：这个脚本在什么页面做什么事情，达到什么目的。<br>- 复杂脚本必须维护 stage，并在 start、选择器命中数、分支、循环进度、catch 里 trace(...)；失败后根据 diagnostic + trace 修正脚本再重试。<br>- run_script 页面模式适合读取页面、批量解析、批量落库、局部 DOM 调试、内部 ask_llm 子分析和已验证的页面批量交互。视觉序列先由主 Agent 单步观察；仅当效果符合预期、后续坐标可预测且 viewportChange 表明浏览器视口稳定时，才串行 await tools.act_on_screen，中间动作传 returnScreenshot:false，每步检查 coordinateBasisReady。需要观察时调用 screenshot，或让对应 act_on_screen 传 returnScreenshot:true；run_script 会按执行顺序返回最后一张此类图片。条件不再成立就立即返回。调用 run_script 时设 mutatesPage:true。<br>- 动态页面/懒加载/虚拟列表要写有限 waitFor(fn,label,timeout)；不要直接链式读取可能为空的元素。<br>- 图片或视觉素材采集先用 await tools.list_images(...) 获得候选，再把选中候选的 target 或 URL 交给 await tools.collect_images(...) 建本地图集；run_script 可用于滚动和整理候选元信息。需要保存页面区域/视口截图时调用 screenshot 并传 destination=图集表名。<br>- 脚本内调用 tools.data_store / tools.collect_images / tools.save_file / tools.download_url / document_* 生成的表和文件会自动进入任务产物。<br>- 脚本最终只返回小摘要：{ok, stage, count, inserted, updated, totalRows, table, sample, errors}；sample 最多 1-3 条，大批量数据在脚本内分批 await tools.data_store(...)。run_script 会自动提升内部最后一次 screenshot 或 act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) 的图片。<br><br>推荐骨架：<br>async () => {<br>  // 在当前列表页采集条目文本，形成 rows 摘要用于验证页面解析并支持后续落库。<br>  let stage = "init";<br>  const wait = (ms) => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));<br>  async function waitFor(fn, label, timeout = 8000) {<br>    const start = Date.now();<br>    while (Date.now() - start < timeout) {<br>      const value = fn();<br>      if (value) return value;<br>      await wait(200);<br>    }<br>    throw new Error("timeout waiting " + label);<br>  }<br>  try {<br>    trace("start", { target: "page", url: location.href, title: document.title });<br>    stage = "select";<br>    const items = [...document.querySelectorAll(".item")];<br>    trace("items", items.length);<br>    stage = "parse";<br>    const rows = items.map((el, i) => ({ i, text: el.textContent.trim() }));<br>    return { ok: true, stage, count: rows.length, sample: rows.slice(0, 3) };<br>  } catch (e) {<br>    trace("fail", { stage, message: e.message, stack: e.stack });<br>    return { ok: false, stage, error: e.message };<br>  }<br>} | 执行临时 JavaScript 代码，支持 top-level await。只接收内联 code；Skill 包内脚本请直接调用对应 Skill 工具。脚本可访问 input/args、tools/tool、trace/log；页面模式还可访问 tabId/document/window。tools 是当前运行配置下可用工具的动态代理（如 await tools.find_elements({...})、await tools.data_store({...})）；不要用 Object.keys(tools) 判断能力，诊断时使用 await tools.$list()。<br><br>运行目标：<br>- target 不填或 target:"page" 表示页面模式，使用显式的当前任务拥有 tabId，或当前任务 workingTabId（只读查看其他任务 tab 请直接用只读页面工具传 tabId，不要用 run_script）。<br>- target:"inner" 表示轻舟内部 offscreen 运行环境，仅用于与页面无关的纯 JS、data_store 批处理、save_file/document_*、ask_llm 等编排，没有 document/window/DOM/tabId。<br><br>编写规范：<br>- 必填 timeout（秒）：<= 0 表示无限等待（适合长采集/批量落库），> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>- 不能用 getEventListeners、$0、copy、monitorEvents 等 DevTools Console helper，也不能用 Node.js/fs/path/process/require/import/shell。<br>- 默认把 code 写成单个 async () => { ... } 函数表达式（不要写 IIFE、main() 或函数外 return）；脚本开始处用一行注释简明说明：这个脚本在什么页面做什么事情，达到什么目的。<br>- 复杂脚本维护 stage 并在关键节点调用 trace(...)；动态内容使用有限超时的 waitFor(fn,label,timeout)。失败后根据 diagnostic + trace 修正重试。<br>- 视觉序列先由主 Agent 单步观察；仅当效果符合预期、后续坐标可预测且 viewportChange 表明浏览器视口稳定时，才串行 await tools.act_on_screen（中间步 returnScreenshot:false，每步检查 coordinateBasisReady；需要观察时调用 screenshot 或让末步传 returnScreenshot:true，按执行顺序返回最后一张此类图片）。调用 run_script 时设 mutatesPage:true。<br>- 大批数据在脚本内分批 await tools.data_store(...) 落库，最终只返回小摘要：{ok, stage, count, inserted, updated, totalRows, table, sample, errors}（sample 最多 1–3 条）；run_script 会自动提升内部最后一次 screenshot 或 act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) 的图片。<br><br>推荐骨架：<br>async () => {<br>  // 在当前列表页采集条目文本，形成 rows 摘要用于验证页面解析并支持后续落库。<br>  let stage = "init";<br>  const wait = (ms) => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));<br>  async function waitFor(fn, label, timeout = 8000) {<br>    const start = Date.now();<br>    while (Date.now() - start < timeout) {<br>      const value = fn();<br>      if (value) return value;<br>      await wait(200);<br>    }<br>    throw new Error("timeout waiting " + label);<br>  }<br>  try {<br>    trace("start", { target: "page", url: location.href, title: document.title });<br>    stage = "select";<br>    const items = [...document.querySelectorAll(".item")];<br>    trace("items", items.length);<br>    stage = "parse";<br>    const rows = items.map((el, i) => ({ i, text: el.textContent.trim() }));<br>    return { ok: true, stage, count: rows.length, sample: rows.slice(0, 3) };<br>  } catch (e) {<br>    trace("fail", { stage, message: e.message, stack: e.stack });<br>    return { ok: false, stage, error: e.message };<br>  }<br>} |
| **description** | `en` | Execute temporary JavaScript with top-level await support. Accepts inline code only; scripts packaged in a Skill must be invoked through that Skill’s tool. Scripts can access input/args, tools/tool, and trace/log; page mode also provides tabId/document/window. tools is a dynamic proxy for tools available in the current run configuration. Call await tools.find_elements({...}), await tools.act_on_screen({...}), await tools.data_store({...}), and so on directly. Do not use Object.keys(tools) to discover capabilities; use await tools.$list() for diagnostics.<br><br>Execution targets:<br>- Omitting target or using target:"page" runs in page mode, using an explicit tabId owned by the current task or its workingTabId.<br>- target:"inner" runs in qz’s internal offscreen environment. Use only for page-independent JavaScript, data_store batches, save_file/document_* operations, ask_llm, and other internal tool orchestration. It does not read the target page’s DOM or automatically use the browser’s active tab.<br>- open_tab creates a new working tab; list_tabs reports web tab state; use_tab sets the working tab.<br>- Page mode cannot interact with tabs owned by other tasks. For read-only access to another task’s tab, pass tabId explicitly to a read-only page tool, not run_script.<br><br>Reliability requirements:<br>- Every call must include timeout in seconds. timeout <= 0 means no time limit; timeout > 0 sets the maximum duration. Use a few dozen seconds for lightweight reads. For explicitly long collection, pagination, or bulk-storage tasks, 0 allows the user to stop execution.<br>- Page mode always runs in the web page’s JavaScript context. If CDP or the page context is unavailable, returns page_context_unavailable. inner mode runs in a Worker without document/window/DOM.<br>- getEventListeners, $0, copy, monitorEvents, and other DevTools Console helpers are unavailable. In page mode use standard document/window/Web APIs and qz tools; in inner mode use standard JavaScript/Web Worker APIs and qz tools.<br>- By default, write code as one async () => { ... } function expression; run_script invokes it automatically. Do not use an IIFE, main();, or a return outside a function.<br>- Begin the script with a one-line comment explaining which page it works on, what it does, and why.<br>- Complex scripts must track stage and call trace(...) at start, for selector match counts, at branches, during loop progress, and in catch. On failure, use diagnostic + trace to correct the script before retrying.<br>- Page mode is suitable for reading pages, batch parsing and storage, local DOM debugging, internal ask_llm analysis, and verified batches of page interactions. The main agent must first observe visual sequences one step at a time. Only when effects match expectations, subsequent coordinates are predictable, and viewportChange confirms a stable browser viewport may a script call await tools.act_on_screen sequentially. Use returnScreenshot:false for intermediate actions and check coordinateBasisReady after every step. To observe, call screenshot or pass returnScreenshot:true to the relevant act_on_screen call; run_script returns the last such image in execution order. Return immediately when these conditions no longer hold. Set mutatesPage:true when calling run_script.<br>- For dynamic pages, lazy loading, and virtual lists, use a bounded waitFor(fn,label,timeout). Do not chain property reads on elements that may be null.<br>- To collect images or visual assets, first use await tools.list_images(...) to discover candidates, then pass selected targets or URLs to await tools.collect_images(...) to create a local collection. Use run_script for scrolling and organizing candidate metadata. To save a page region or viewport capture, call screenshot with destination set to the image collection table name.<br>- Tables and files created through tools.data_store / tools.collect_images / tools.save_file / tools.download_url / document_* automatically become task artifacts.<br>- Return only a small summary: {ok, stage, count, inserted, updated, totalRows, table, sample, errors}. Limit sample to 1–3 entries; write large datasets in batches within the script using await tools.data_store(...). run_script automatically surfaces the image from the last nested screenshot or act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) call.<br><br>Suggested structure:<br>async () => {<br>  // Extract item text from the current list page to verify parsing and support later storage.<br>  let stage = "init";<br>  const wait = (ms) => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));<br>  async function waitFor(fn, label, timeout = 8000) {<br>    const start = Date.now();<br>    while (Date.now() - start < timeout) {<br>      const value = fn();<br>      if (value) return value;<br>      await wait(200);<br>    }<br>    throw new Error("timeout waiting " + label);<br>  }<br>  try {<br>    trace("start", { target: "page", url: location.href, title: document.title });<br>    stage = "select";<br>    const items = [...document.querySelectorAll(".item")];<br>    trace("items", items.length);<br>    stage = "parse";<br>    const rows = items.map((el, i) => ({ i, text: el.textContent.trim() }));<br>    return { ok: true, stage, count: rows.length, sample: rows.slice(0, 3) };<br>  } catch (e) {<br>    trace("fail", { stage, message: e.message, stack: e.stack });<br>    return { ok: false, stage, error: e.message };<br>  }<br>} | Execute temporary JavaScript with top-level await support. Accepts inline code only; invoke scripts packaged in a Skill through that Skill's tool. Scripts can access input/args, tools/tool, and trace/log; page mode also provides tabId/document/window. tools is a dynamic proxy for tools enabled in the current run configuration (e.g. await tools.find_elements({...}), await tools.data_store({...})); do not use Object.keys(tools) to inspect capabilities—call await tools.$list() instead.<br><br>Execution targets:<br>- Omitting target or setting target:"page" runs in page mode on an explicit task-owned tabId or the current task's workingTabId (for read-only tabs owned by another task, pass tabId directly to a read-only page tool rather than run_script).<br>- target:"inner" runs in qz's internal offscreen Worker without document/window/DOM/tabId; use it only for page-independent JS, data_store batch processing, save_file/document_*, and ask_llm orchestration.<br><br>Requirements:<br>- Required timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit (for long collection/batch storage); > 0 sets the maximum duration.<br>- DevTools Console helpers (getEventListeners, $0, copy, monitorEvents) and Node.js/fs/path/process/require/import/shell APIs are unavailable.<br>- Write code as a single async () => { ... } function expression (no IIFE, main(), or return outside a function), starting with a one-line comment stating which page it runs on, what it does, and why.<br>- Track stage and call trace(...) at key steps in complex scripts; use a bounded waitFor(fn,label,timeout) for dynamic DOM. On failure, inspect diagnostic + trace before retrying.<br>- Observe visual sequences one step at a time first; only when effects match expectations, subsequent coordinates are predictable, and viewportChange confirms a stable browser viewport may a script call await tools.act_on_screen serially (pass returnScreenshot:false on intermediate steps, check coordinateBasisReady after each step, and call screenshot or set returnScreenshot:true on the final step; the last such image in execution order is returned). Set mutatesPage:true when modifying the page.<br>- Write large datasets in batches inside the script via await tools.data_store(...), returning only a compact summary: {ok, stage, count, inserted, updated, totalRows, table, sample, errors} (1–3 sample items); run_script automatically promotes the image from its last screenshot or act_on_screen(returnScreenshot:true) call.<br><br>Suggested structure:<br>async () => {<br>  // Extract item text from the current list page to verify parsing and support later storage.<br>  let stage = "init";<br>  const wait = (ms) => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));<br>  async function waitFor(fn, label, timeout = 8000) {<br>    const start = Date.now();<br>    while (Date.now() - start < timeout) {<br>      const value = fn();<br>      if (value) return value;<br>      await wait(200);<br>    }<br>    throw new Error("timeout waiting " + label);<br>  }<br>  try {<br>    trace("start", { target: "page", url: location.href, title: document.title });<br>    stage = "select";<br>    const items = [...document.querySelectorAll(".item")];<br>    trace("items", items.length);<br>    stage = "parse";<br>    const rows = items.map((el, i) => ({ i, text: el.textContent.trim() }));<br>    return { ok: true, stage, count: rows.length, sample: rows.slice(0, 3) };<br>  } catch (e) {<br>    trace("fail", { stage, message: e.message, stack: e.stack });<br>    return { ok: false, stage, error: e.message };<br>  }<br>} |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 必填。脚本硬超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 | 必填。脚本超时秒数；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Required script timeout in seconds. <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. | Required script timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. |
| param `target` | `zh-CN` | 执行目标。不填或 page=当前任务网页上下文；inner=轻舟内部 offscreen Worker，只用于与页面无关的纯 JS/data_store/save_file/document/ask_llm 批处理。 | 执行目标：省略或 page=当前任务网页上下文；inner=内部 offscreen Worker（仅用于与页面无关的纯 JS/data_store/save_file/document/ask_llm 批处理）。 |
| param `target` | `en` | Execution target. Omitted or page: the current task’s web page context. inner: qz’s internal offscreen Worker, only for page-independent JavaScript/data_store/save_file/document/ask_llm batch operations. | Execution target: omitted or page for the current task's web page context; inner for the internal offscreen Worker (only for page-independent JS/data_store/save_file/document/ask_llm batches). |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务 workingTabId。 | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务 workingTabId。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s workingTabId. | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s workingTabId. |
| param `args` | `zh-CN` | 传给临时代码的参数对象，脚本中通过 input 或 args 读取。 | 传给代码的参数对象（脚本内通过 input 或 args 读取）。 |
| param `args` | `en` | Arguments passed to the temporary code, available as input or args. | Arguments object passed to the code (read via input or args). |
| param `mutatesPage` | `zh-CN` | 脚本是否会修改页面 DOM、表单或页面状态。默认 false；仅当确实修改页面时设为 true。 | 脚本是否会修改页面 DOM、表单或状态，默认 false。 |
| param `mutatesPage` | `en` | Whether the script modifies the page DOM, forms, or page state. Default: false. Set true only when it actually modifies the page. | Whether the script modifies page DOM, forms, or state. Default: false. |
| param `code` | `zh-CN` | 临时 JavaScript 代码；最后一个表达式会作为 result，函数体风格可显式 return。可调用 trace(...items) / log(...items) 输出调试轨迹到结果 trace。skill/plugin 包内脚本请调用对应 Skill 工具。该字段可能很长，填写 target/tabId/args/mutatesPage 后最后填写 code。 | 内联 JavaScript 代码（推荐单个 async () => { ... } 函数表达式，可用 return 返回结果，用 trace/log 记录诊断轨迹）。该字段可能较长，先填 timeout/target/tabId/args/mutatesPage，最后填写 code。 |
| param `code` | `en` | Temporary JavaScript code. The last expression becomes result; a function body may use an explicit return. Call trace(...items) / log(...items) to append diagnostic entries to the result’s trace. Invoke scripts packaged in a Skill through the corresponding Skill tool. This field may be long: fill target/tabId/args/mutatesPage first and code last. | Inline JavaScript code (prefer a single async () => { ... } function expression; use return for results and trace/log for diagnostics). This field may be long: supply timeout/target/tabId/args/mutatesPage first and code last. |

### `manage_sessions`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 管理轻舟 sessions 和定时任务。create 会创建当前 session 的子任务，并可通过 firstMessage 派发异步后台任务；send_message 向已有 session 追加任务（mode=follow_up 在当前 run 后执行；mode=steer 在完整 turn 边界介入，空闲时直接启动）；stop 终止目标当前 run，保留排队消息。list/last_messages/wait/delete 管理普通 session。last_messages 和 wait 回收任务结果时会自动附带每个任务的 artifacts 产物索引（图集、数据表、文件句柄），便于上层继续打开、引用或下载，不会返回大表/文件正文。常见固化流程：先在当前临时对话里跑通方法，再用 create_automation 把明确 instruction、schedule、missedPolicy、notifyPolicy 保存成本地定时任务；list_automations 查看定时任务规则；automation_runs 查看某条定时任务的运行记录；run_automation 立即运行一次；update_automation 修改配置；delete_automation 删除规则及运行记录。 | 管理轻舟子任务、会话与本地定时任务（仅 user-created root session 可用）。子任务：create 创建子 session 并可通过 firstMessage 启动后台运行；wait 等待子任务完成并附带 artifacts 产物索引（图集、Data 表、文件句柄）；last_messages 读取最近消息与 artifacts；send_message 向其他 session 发送消息（follow_up 在当前 run 后执行，steer 在当前 turn 后介入，空闲时立即启动）；stop 终止目标当前 run；list/delete 列出或删除其他 session。定时任务：create_automation 保存 title、instruction、schedule、missedPolicy、notifyPolicy；list_automations/automation_runs/run_automation/update_automation/delete_automation 查看、立即运行、修改或删除定时任务。 |
| **description** | `en` | Manage qz sessions and automations. create starts a child session and can dispatch an asynchronous background task through firstMessage. send_message adds work to an existing session: mode=follow_up runs after the current run; mode=steer is delivered at a complete turn boundary; an idle session starts immediately. stop ends the target's current run but preserves queued messages. Use list/last_messages/wait/delete for ordinary sessions. last_messages and wait include each task's artifacts index (image collections, Data tables, and file handles) for opening, referencing, or downloading results, without returning large tables or file contents. To automate a workflow, first verify it in the current temporary conversation, then save explicit instruction, schedule, missedPolicy, and notifyPolicy with create_automation. list_automations lists rules; automation_runs lists a rule's run history; run_automation runs it immediately; update_automation edits it; delete_automation removes the rule and run history. | Manage qz child tasks, sessions, and local automations (available in user-created root sessions only). Sessions: create starts a child session (pass firstMessage to run in the background); wait waits for child sessions and includes their artifacts index (galleries, Data tables, file handles); last_messages reads recent messages and artifacts; send_message sends a message to another session (follow_up after the current run, steer after the current turn, or immediately if idle); stop ends the target's current run; list/delete lists or deletes other sessions. Automations: create_automation saves title, instruction, schedule, missedPolicy, and notifyPolicy; list_automations/automation_runs/run_automation/update_automation/delete_automation lists, runs, edits, or deletes automations. |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | 操作类型。分支必填：delete/last_messages/stop 需 sessionId；send_message 需 sessionId 和 message；wait 需 sessionIds 或 sessionId；create_automation 需 title 和 instruction；update/delete/run/automation_runs 需 automationId。 | 操作类型。必填项：delete/last_messages/stop 需 sessionId；send_message 需 sessionId + message；wait 需 sessionIds 或 sessionId；create_automation 需 title + instruction；update_automation/delete_automation/run_automation/automation_runs 需 automationId。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Operation. delete/last_messages/stop requires sessionId; send_message requires sessionId and message; wait requires sessionIds or sessionId; create_automation requires title and instruction; update_automation/delete_automation/run_automation/automation_runs requires automationId. | Action. Required fields: delete/last_messages/stop needs sessionId; send_message needs sessionId + message; wait needs sessionIds or sessionId; create_automation needs title + instruction; update_automation/delete_automation/run_automation/automation_runs needs automationId. |
| param `sessionId` | `zh-CN` | delete/last_messages/stop/send_message 目标 session id | 目标 sessionId（用于 delete/last_messages/stop/send_message/wait）。 |
| param `sessionId` | `en` | Target session ID for delete/last_messages/stop/send_message. | Target sessionId for delete/last_messages/stop/send_message/wait. |
| param `message` | `zh-CN` | send_message 发送给已有任务的消息正文 | send_message 发送的非空消息正文。 |
| param `message` | `en` | Message to send to an existing task with send_message. | Non-empty message text for send_message. |
| param `mode` | `zh-CN` | send_message 投递方式，默认 follow_up；steer 在当前完整 turn 后接收；空闲任务直接启动。 | send_message 投递模式：follow_up（默认，当前 run 后）或 steer（当前 turn 后）；空闲时直接启动。 |
| param `mode` | `en` | Delivery mode for send_message. Default: follow_up. steer is received after the current complete turn. Idle tasks start immediately. | Delivery mode: follow_up (default, after current run) or steer (after current turn); starts immediately if idle. |
| param `automationId` | `zh-CN` | update_automation/delete_automation/run_automation/automation_runs 的目标 automation id | update_automation/delete_automation/run_automation/automation_runs 的目标 ID。 |
| param `automationId` | `en` | Target automation ID for update_automation/delete_automation/run_automation/automation_runs. | Target automation ID for update_automation/delete_automation/run_automation/automation_runs. |
| param `sessionIds` | `zh-CN` | wait 目标 session id 列表 | wait 的目标 sessionId 数组。 |
| param `sessionIds` | `en` | Session IDs to wait for. | Target sessionIds array for wait. |
| param `waitFor` | `zh-CN` | wait 时等待其中之一完成(any)或全部完成(all)，默认 all。用户要求先检查最先完成项、再汇总全部时，先调用 any，再对同一批 sessionIds 调用 all。 | wait 模式：all（默认）或 any（先看首项再汇总时，先对同一批 sessionIds 调 any 再调 all）。 |
| param `waitFor` | `en` | Wait for any or all sessions to finish. Default: all. If the user wants to inspect the first result before summarizing all results, call any first, then all with the same sessionIds. | Wait mode: all (default) or any (call any then all on the same sessionIds to inspect the first result before summarizing all). |
| param `title` | `zh-CN` | create 的子 session 标题，或 create_automation 的定时任务标题；短而具体 | create 的子任务标题，或 create_automation 必填的简短标题。 |
| param `title` | `en` | Short, specific child-session title for create, or automation title for create_automation. | Child-session title for create, or required short title for create_automation. |
| param `firstMessage` | `zh-CN` | create 后发送给新 session agent 的第一句话；传入后该 session 会在后台开始运行 | create 后发送给新 session 并在后台启动执行的首条消息。 |
| param `firstMessage` | `en` | Initial message for the new session's agent after create. Providing it starts the session in the background. | Initial message sent to start the new session in the background after create. |
| param `instruction` | `zh-CN` | create_automation/update_automation 的定时任务执行说明。应沉淀已跑通的方法、目标页面、输出格式、数据表、异常处理要求。 | create_automation/update_automation 的执行说明（目标、步骤、页面、Data 表及异常处理）。 |
| param `instruction` | `en` | Instructions for create_automation/update_automation. Capture the verified method, target pages, output format, Data tables, and failure handling. | Instructions for create_automation/update_automation (goal, steps, pages, Data tables, and error handling). |
| param `schedule` | `zh-CN` | 定时任务调度规则。本地版支持 once/hourly/daily/weekly。单次运行使用 type=once，并必须提供 runAt；推荐 ISO 8601 北京时间格式，避免空格丢失。 | 定时调度规则，支持 once/hourly/daily/weekly。 |
| param `schedule` | `en` | Local schedule supporting once/hourly/daily/weekly. For a one-time run, use type=once and supply runAt. Prefer ISO 8601 with the Beijing time offset to avoid losing spaces. | Schedule rule supporting once/hourly/daily/weekly. |
| param `schedule.type` | `zh-CN` | once 单次运行；hourly 每小时/每 N 分钟；daily 每天；weekly 每周 | once（单次，需 runAt）、hourly（按分钟间隔）、daily（每天）、weekly（每周）。 |
| param `schedule.type` | `en` | once: one-time run; hourly: every hour or N minutes; daily: every day; weekly: every week. | once (requires runAt), hourly (by minute interval), daily, or weekly. |
| param `schedule.runAt` | `zh-CN` | once 必填。推荐 ISO 8601 北京时间 YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss+08:00，如 2026-07-08T09:30:00+08:00；兼容旧格式 YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss。 | once 必填。推荐 ISO 8601 北京时间 YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss+08:00（如 2026-07-08T09:30:00+08:00），兼容 YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss。 |
| param `schedule.runAt` | `en` | Required for once. Prefer ISO 8601 Beijing time: YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss+08:00, for example 2026-07-08T09:30:00+08:00. The legacy YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss format is also accepted. | Required for once. Prefer ISO 8601 Beijing time YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss+08:00 (e.g. 2026-07-08T09:30:00+08:00); accepts YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss. |
| param `schedule.time` | `zh-CN` | daily/weekly 的北京时间 HH:mm，如 09:00 | daily/weekly 的北京时间 HH:mm（如 09:00）。 |
| param `schedule.time` | `en` | Beijing time in HH:mm format for daily/weekly, for example 09:00. | Beijing time HH:mm for daily/weekly (e.g. 09:00). |
| param `schedule.weekday` | `zh-CN` | weekly 使用，1=周一 ... 7=周日 | weekly 星期几（1=周一 ... 7=周日）。 |
| param `schedule.weekday` | `en` | Day of the week for weekly: 1=Monday through 7=Sunday. | Day of the week for weekly (1=Monday through 7=Sunday). |
| param `schedule.intervalMinutes` | `zh-CN` | hourly/interval 使用，支持 5、15、30、60；优先使用该字段 | hourly 间隔分钟数（支持 5、15、30、60）。 |
| param `schedule.intervalMinutes` | `en` | Interval for hourly/interval. Supports 5, 15, 30, or 60 minutes. Prefer this field. | Interval in minutes for hourly (supports 5, 15, 30, or 60). |
| param `schedule.intervalHours` | `zh-CN` | 兼容旧字段，hourly 使用；会转换为 intervalMinutes | 兼容旧字段（小时数，自动转为 intervalMinutes）。 |
| param `schedule.intervalHours` | `en` | Legacy hourly interval, converted to intervalMinutes. | Legacy interval in hours (converted to intervalMinutes). |
| param `missedPolicy` | `zh-CN` | 本机离线/睡眠漏跑后的处理：run_once 恢复后补跑一次；latest_only 只检查最新状态；skip 跳过漏跑。 | 离线/睡眠漏跑策略：run_once（补跑一次）、latest_only（只查最新）、skip（跳过）。 |
| param `missedPolicy` | `en` | Handle runs missed while the computer was offline or asleep: run_once catches up once; latest_only checks only the latest state; skip skips missed runs. | Missed-run policy: run_once (catch up once), latest_only (check latest), or skip. |
| param `notifyPolicy` | `zh-CN` | 通知策略：failure 仅异常；always 每次完成；silent 静默记录。 | 通知策略：failure（仅失败）、always（每次完成）、silent（静默）。 |
| param `notifyPolicy` | `en` | Notification policy: failure notifies only on failures; always notifies after each run; silent records runs without notifications. | Notification policy: failure, always, or silent. |
| param `enabled` | `zh-CN` | 定时任务是否启用。create_automation 默认 true；update_automation 可修改。 | 是否启用（create_automation 默认 true）。 |
| param `enabled` | `en` | Whether the automation is enabled. Defaults to true for create_automation; editable with update_automation. | Whether enabled (defaults to true for create_automation). |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | list 返回数量，默认 100 | list 返回条数，默认 100。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum results for list. Default: 100. | Maximum items for list. Default: 100. |
| param `runLimit` | `zh-CN` | list_automations 每条定时任务附带的最近运行记录数量，默认 3 | list_automations 附带的最近运行记录数，默认 3。 |
| param `runLimit` | `en` | Recent runs to include for each automation in list_automations. Default: 3. | Recent runs included per automation in list_automations. Default: 3. |
| param `num` | `zh-CN` | last_messages 返回最近消息数量，默认 5，最大 50 | last_messages/wait 返回的最近消息条数，默认 5，最大 50。 |
| param `num` | `en` | Recent messages returned by last_messages. Default: 5; maximum: 50. | Recent messages returned by last_messages/wait. Default: 5; maximum: 50. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | wait 等待秒数；不填或 <=0 表示无限等待，>0 表示最多等待对应秒数后返回当前子 session 快照；其他 action 可不填。 | wait 等待秒数；省略或 <=0 表示无限等待（上限 2 小时），>0 表示最多等待对应秒数后返回快照。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Seconds to wait. Omitted or <=0 means no time limit. A positive value returns the current child-session snapshots after at most that many seconds. Optional for other actions. | Wait seconds: omitted or <=0 means infinite wait (up to 2 hours); >0 returns snapshots after at most that many seconds. |

### `manage_goal`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 管理当前 session 的目标追踪状态。适合长流程采集、翻页、批处理、跨多个工具步骤的任务，避免执行过程中忘记目标和检查点。使用方式：长流程开始时 create；每次已经写入 data_store、图集或文件等 durable 进展后 checkpoint；遇到阻塞时 block 写明 progress/next；完成前先用相关工具验证 criteria，再 complete。goal 只记录短目标、完成标准、进展、下一步和检查点；大表、大数组、正文、DOM、图片和文件内容不要写入 goal。 | 管理当前 root session 的多轮长任务目标追踪：长流程开始时 create 记录 objective 与 criteria；每次已经写入 data_store、图集或文件等 durable 进展后 checkpoint 更新 progress/next/checkpoint；遇到阻塞时 block 写明 progress（原因）和 next（等待事项）；完成前先用工具验证完成标准再 complete。只保存简短文本，禁止存入大数组、全文、DOM、base64 或文件内容。 |
| **description** | `en` | Track the current session's goal for long collection, pagination, batch, or multi-tool workflows so objectives and checkpoints are not lost. Use create at the start; checkpoint after durable progress is saved to data_store, an image collection, or a file; block with progress/next when blocked; verify criteria with relevant tools before complete. Keep only a short objective, completion criteria, progress, next step, and checkpoint. Do not put large tables, arrays, document text, DOM, images, or file contents in goal. | Track the current root session's multi-turn task goal: call create at the start with objective and criteria; call checkpoint after durable progress is written to data_store, a gallery, or a file; call block with progress (cause) and next (needed action) when blocked; verify criteria with tools before complete. Store only short text fields, never large arrays, full text, DOM, base64, or file contents. |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | 目标动作。分支必填：create 需 objective 和 criteria；block 需 progress 和 next。checkpoint/update 用 progress/next/checkpoint 更新短状态；complete 前先验证完成标准。 | 操作类型。必填项：create 需 objective + criteria；block 需 progress + next；checkpoint/update 更新 progress/next/checkpoint；complete 前须先验证 criteria。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Goal operation. create requires objective and criteria; block requires progress and next. checkpoint/update changes short progress/next/checkpoint fields. Verify completion criteria before complete. | Goal action. Required fields: create needs objective + criteria; block needs progress + next; checkpoint/update updates progress/next/checkpoint; verify criteria before complete. |
| param `objective` | `zh-CN` | 任务目标，create 时建议填写一句话。例：采集 Hacker News 前 3 页帖子并写入 hn_posts。 | 一句话任务目标（create 必填，最多 240 字符）。例：采集 Hacker News 前 3 页帖子写入 hn_posts。 |
| param `objective` | `en` | Task objective, preferably one sentence for create. Example: collect posts from the first 3 Hacker News pages into hn_posts. | One-sentence task objective (required for create, up to 240 chars). Example: Collect posts from the first 3 Hacker News pages into hn_posts. |
| param `criteria` | `zh-CN` | 完成标准，create 时建议填写；complete 前必须用工具结果验证。例：3 页完成、写入 hn_posts、按 link 去重、验证行数。 | 可验证的完成标准（create 必填，最多 360 字符）。例：完成 3 页、按 url 去重写入 hn_posts 并核对行数。 |
| param `criteria` | `en` | Completion criteria for create. Verify them with tool results before complete. Example: finish 3 pages, write to hn_posts, deduplicate by link, and verify the row count. | Verifiable completion criteria (required for create, up to 360 chars). Example: Finish 3 pages, deduplicate by url in hn_posts, and verify row count. |
| param `progress` | `zh-CN` | 当前进展短句。例：第 2 页已写入，累计 60 条。 | 当前已完成进展或 block 阻塞原因短句（block 必填，最多 360 字符）。 |
| param `progress` | `en` | Brief progress update. Example: page 2 saved; 60 rows total. | Brief progress summary or blocker cause for block (required for block, up to 360 chars). |
| param `next` | `zh-CN` | 下一步动作短句。例：打开第 3 页继续采集。 | 下一步动作或等待事项短句（block 必填，最多 240 字符）。 |
| param `next` | `en` | Brief next step. Example: open page 3 and continue collecting. | Brief next step or awaited action for block (required for block, up to 240 chars). |
| param `checkpoint` | `zh-CN` | 可恢复执行的短检查点，可写 page/cursor/table/count/url 等少量信息。例：page=2; next=?p=3; table=hn_posts; rows=60。 | 可恢复执行的短检查点（最多 500 字符），如 page=2; next=?p=3; table=hn_posts; rows=60。 |
| param `checkpoint` | `en` | Short resumable checkpoint with a few fields such as page/cursor/table/count/url. Example: page=2; next=?p=3; table=hn_posts; rows=60. | Short resumable checkpoint (up to 500 chars), such as page=2; next=?p=3; table=hn_posts; rows=60. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值；需要长等待时显式设置。 | 超时秒数；省略时使用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard tool timeout in seconds. If omitted, uses the tool type's default. Set explicitly for long waits. | Timeout in seconds. Uses the default timeout if omitted. |

### `get_location`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 获取用户当前经纬度、精度（米）和定位时间。仅在用户任务需要本地信息时调用，无需参数。首次使用在侧边栏等待用户同意，后续调用沿用该选择。需要打开侧边栏并允许设备定位。定位不可用或用户拒绝时，询问城市或区域，继续处理任务。 | 获取用户当前经纬度、精度（米）和定位时间。仅在任务需要本地位置信息时调用（无参数）。需打开侧边栏并由用户授权定位；不可用或被拒绝时，向用户询问城市或区域并继续任务。 |
| **description** | `en` | Get the user's current latitude, longitude, accuracy in meters, and location timestamp. Call only when the user's task needs local information. Takes no arguments. First use waits for the user's permission in the side panel; later calls reuse that permission. Requires an open side panel and device location access. If unavailable or declined, ask for a city or area and continue without precise location. | Get the user's current latitude, longitude, accuracy in meters, and location timestamp. Call only when the task needs local information (no arguments). Requires an open side panel and user permission; if unavailable or declined, ask the user for a city or area and continue. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `send_alert`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 给当前用户发送一条本地浏览器弹窗提醒，用于把用户叫回浏览器处理阻塞或查看重要进展。它不能替代 ask_user/wait_for_user；遇到登录、验证码、授权、付款等需要用户亲自操作的场景，仍必须调用 wait_for_user。工具内置全局流控，所有 session、定时任务和脚本调用共享限频，重复提醒可能被 suppressed。 | 发送本地浏览器系统通知，提醒用户回到浏览器查看重要进展或处理阻塞。它不能替代 ask_user 或 wait_for_user（登录、验证码、授权、付款等仍须调用 wait_for_user）。内置全局流控与防抖，重复或过频通知会返回 suppressed:true。 |
| **description** | `en` | Send the current user a local browser notification to bring them back for a blocker or important progress. It does not replace ask_user/wait_for_user. For login, CAPTCHA, authorization, payment, or other actions requiring the user, still call wait_for_user. Notifications share a global rate limit across all sessions, automations, and scripts; repeated notifications may be suppressed. | Send a local browser notification to alert the user of important progress or a blocker. Does not replace ask_user or wait_for_user (still call wait_for_user for sign-in, CAPTCHA, authorization, or payment). Globally rate-limited and deduplicated; repeated alerts return suppressed:true. |
| param `title` | `zh-CN` | 通知标题，默认“轻舟提醒”。保持简短。 | 简短通知标题（最多 80 字符），默认“轻舟提醒”。 |
| param `title` | `en` | Short notification title. Defaults to “qz”. | Short notification title (up to 80 chars). Default: “qz”. |
| param `message` | `zh-CN` | 通知正文，说明用户需要注意或处理什么。 | 必填。通知正文（最多 600 字符），说明需要用户关注或处理的事项。 |
| param `message` | `en` | Notification body explaining what needs the user's attention or action. | Required notification body (up to 600 chars) explaining what needs attention. |
| param `level` | `zh-CN` | 提醒级别，默认 info；只影响通知优先级。 | 通知级别（info/success/warning/error），默认 info。 |
| param `level` | `en` | Notification level. Default: info. Affects priority only. | Notification priority level (info/success/warning/error). Default: info. |
| param `dedupeKey` | `zh-CN` | 可选去重键。相同去重键在短时间内只会提醒一次；不填时按 title+message 自动去重。 | 可选去重键（2 分钟内相同 key 不重复发送）；省略时按 title+message 去重。 |
| param `dedupeKey` | `en` | Optional deduplication key. Only one notification with the same key is sent within a short period. If omitted, deduplicates by title+message. | Optional deduplication key (suppresses duplicates within 2 minutes); defaults to title+message. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `manage_settings`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取和修改轻舟自身设置。只负责模型服务商、模型名、模型参数、高级运行设置和模型预设；访问安全策略和站点流控请使用 qingzhou_management__manage_guardrails。update_config 接收字段补丁：只传本次要改变的字段，未传字段沿用当前配置，并按设置页保存语义 upsert provider/model 预设。modelParameters 是直接传给模型服务商的 JSON 参数对象，支持项由服务商决定，模型、消息、工具和流式开关等请求骨架字段由轻舟管理。实际切换到不同 provider/model 时，省略 contextWindow、vision 即自动采用新模型的默认值；匹配与设置页共用 models.dev SDK 离线快照及本地能力声明，未命中时才查询在线目录。不要复制旧模型的能力字段；用户明确要求的覆盖值仍优先。无法准确匹配新模型的上下文长度时工具会返回 MODEL_CAPABILITIES_REQUIRED；此时向用户确认 contextWindow，再明确传入并重试，不猜测默认值。无法识别图片能力时按不支持图片处理。apply_preset 恢复预设保存的能力及覆盖值；apply_preset/delete_preset 复用设置页的预设函数，不手工拼字段。用户要求处理模型接口 429/Too Many Requests/rate limit 时，可只把 requestRateLimit 更新为 true，让后续模型请求进入 RPM/TPM 流控；余额、账单或总配额耗尽不适用。典型流程：先 get 查看当前 provider/model 和已有预设；已有匹配预设时 apply_preset；需要找模型时 list_models；缺少 AK 时打开服务商网站或说明步骤并 wait_for_user/ask_user 等用户亲自创建或提供 AK；拿到 AK 后再 update_config。 | 读取和修改轻舟模型与运行设置（访问规则与站点流控请用 qingzhou_management__manage_guardrails，Skill 自带配置请用 qingzhou_management__manage_skill_settings）。先 get 查看当前 provider/model 和 presets；已有匹配预设时优先 apply_preset；查询可用模型用 list_models；缺少 API Key 时先请用户提供或调用 wait_for_user/ask_user。update_config 接收增量补丁（未传字段保持不变）：切换到新 provider/model 时省略 contextWindow 和 vision 以自动匹配新模型默认值（不要复制旧模型值）；若返回 MODEL_CAPABILITIES_REQUIRED，向用户确认 contextWindow 后显式传入重试。处理模型接口 429/rate limit 时只传 requestRateLimit:true 开启 RPM/TPM 排队（余额或总配额耗尽不适用）。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read and update qz settings: model provider, model name, request parameters, advanced runtime settings, and model presets. Use qingzhou_management__manage_guardrails for access rules and site rate limits. update_config accepts a field patch: supply only fields to change; omitted fields keep their current values. Provider/model presets are upserted using the same semantics as the Settings page. modelParameters is a JSON object passed directly to the provider; supported options depend on that provider. qz owns request structure such as model, messages, tools, and streaming. When switching to a different provider/model, omit contextWindow and vision to use the new model’s defaults. Matching shares the Settings page’s models.dev SDK snapshot and local capability definitions, consulting the online catalog only for local misses. Do not copy capabilities from the previous model. Explicit user overrides take precedence. If the new context length cannot be identified, the tool returns MODEL_CAPABILITIES_REQUIRED. Ask the user for contextWindow, then provide it explicitly and retry; do not guess a default. Unrecognized image support is treated as unsupported. apply_preset restores the preset’s saved capabilities and overrides. apply_preset/delete_preset use the same preset functions as Settings; do not construct preset fields manually. When the user asks to address model API 429/Too Many Requests/rate limits, set requestRateLimit=true to queue subsequent requests by RPM/TPM. This does not resolve exhausted balances, billing limits, or total quotas. Typical workflow: get the current provider/model and presets; apply_preset for a matching preset; list_models to find a model. If an API key is missing, open the provider website or explain the steps, then wait_for_user/ask_user while the user creates or provides the key. Call update_config after obtaining it. | Read and update qz model and runtime settings (use qingzhou_management__manage_guardrails for access rules/rate limits, and qingzhou_management__manage_skill_settings for Skill-defined settings). Call get first to inspect the current provider/model and presets; prefer apply_preset when a matching preset exists; use list_models to list models; if an API key is needed, ask the user via wait_for_user/ask_user first. update_config applies a partial patch (omitted fields stay unchanged): when switching provider/model, omit contextWindow and vision to use the new model's detected defaults (never copy the old model's values); if MODEL_CAPABILITIES_REQUIRED is returned, ask the user for contextWindow and retry. To mitigate model API 429/rate limits (not exhausted quota/balance), pass requestRateLimit:true. |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | 设置动作。分支必填：apply_preset/delete_preset 需 presetId；update_config 至少传一个模型配置字段。 | 操作类型。必填项：apply_preset/delete_preset 需 presetId；update_config 至少传一个配置字段。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Settings operation. apply_preset/delete_preset requires presetId. update_config requires at least one model configuration field. | Action. Required fields: apply_preset/delete_preset needs presetId; update_config needs at least one configuration field. |
| param `provider` | `zh-CN` | 模型服务商 id。使用 get 返回的 providers 中的 id；列表包含推荐、自定义及 models.dev SDK 支持的服务商，并提供各自的模型请求 endpoint。 | 模型服务商 ID（取自 get 返回的 providers[].id）。 |
| param `provider` | `en` | Provider ID from the providers array returned by get. It includes recommended, custom, and supported models.dev SDK providers, with each provider’s model request endpoint. | Model provider ID (from providers[].id in the get result). |
| param `model` | `zh-CN` | 模型名称。切换模型时传入；修改当前模型的其他设置时可省略并沿用已有值。 | 模型名称（切换模型时传入；仅改当前模型其他参数时可省略）。 |
| param `model` | `en` | Model name. Provide when switching models; omit to keep the current model while changing other settings. | Model ID (pass when switching models; omit when only updating other settings on the current model). |
| param `modelParameters` | `zh-CN` | 可选模型请求参数 Key/Value 对象，例如 reasoning_effort、thinking、temperature。值保留 JSON 类型并直接透传；服务商不支持时会返回其原始错误。 | 透传给模型服务商的请求参数对象（如 reasoning_effort、thinking、temperature，保留原始 JSON 类型）。 |
| param `modelParameters` | `en` | Optional JSON key/value object for model requests, such as reasoning_effort, thinking, or temperature. Values keep their JSON types and pass through directly. Unsupported parameters return the provider's original error. | Provider-specific model request parameters object (e.g. reasoning_effort, thinking, temperature; preserves JSON types). |
| param `apiKey` | `zh-CN` | 模型接口的 AK/API Key。 | 模型服务商 API Key。 |
| param `apiKey` | `en` | API key for the model endpoint. | API key for the model provider. |
| param `presetId` | `zh-CN` | apply_preset/delete_preset 使用的模型预设 id；来自 get 返回的 config.presets[].id。 | 模型预设 ID（取自 get 返回的 config.presets[].id，用于 apply_preset/delete_preset）。 |
| param `presetId` | `en` | Preset ID for apply_preset/delete_preset, from config.presets[].id in the get result. | Model preset ID from config.presets[].id in the get result (for apply_preset/delete_preset). |
| param `codexReasoningEffort` | `zh-CN` | OpenAI Codex provider 的 reasoning effort，默认 medium。 | OpenAI Codex 服务商的推理强度（low/medium/high/xhigh/max），默认 medium。 |
| param `codexReasoningEffort` | `en` | Reasoning effort for the OpenAI Codex provider. Default: medium. | Reasoning effort for the OpenAI Codex provider (low/medium/high/xhigh/max). Default: medium. |
| param `customProtocol` | `zh-CN` | 自定义接口协议。 | 自定义接口协议（openai/anthropic，仅 provider=custom 使用）。 |
| param `customProtocol` | `en` | Protocol for the custom endpoint. | Custom endpoint protocol (openai/anthropic, used when provider=custom). |
| param `customEndpoint` | `zh-CN` | 自定义接口 endpoint。 | 自定义接口 HTTP(S) 地址（仅 provider=custom 使用）。 |
| param `customEndpoint` | `en` | Custom API endpoint. | Custom HTTP(S) API endpoint URL (used when provider=custom). |
| param `customPresetName` | `zh-CN` | 自定义接口预设名称。 | 自定义接口预设显示名称。 |
| param `customPresetName` | `en` | Preset name for the custom endpoint. | Display name for the custom endpoint preset. |
| param `endpoint` | `zh-CN` | customEndpoint 的兼容别名，仅 provider=custom 时使用。 | customEndpoint 的兼容别名（仅 provider=custom 使用）。 |
| param `endpoint` | `en` | Compatibility alias for customEndpoint. Only used when provider=custom. | Compatibility alias for customEndpoint (only when provider=custom). |
| param `contextWindow` | `zh-CN` | 上下文窗口 tokens。切换模型时省略以采用自动匹配的默认值；仅用户要求覆盖或工具返回 MODEL_CAPABILITIES_REQUIRED 时明确传入，不复制旧模型的值。 | 上下文窗口 tokens（切换模型时省略以采用自动匹配的默认值；仅用户要求覆盖或返回 MODEL_CAPABILITIES_REQUIRED 时明确传入）。 |
| param `contextWindow` | `en` | Context window in tokens. Omit when switching models to use the detected default. Supply only for a user-requested override or after MODEL_CAPABILITIES_REQUIRED; do not copy the previous model’s value. | Context window in tokens (omit when switching models to use the detected default; supply only on user override or after MODEL_CAPABILITIES_REQUIRED). |
| param `trimOldToolResults` | `zh-CN` | 高级设置：是否在上下文压力下裁剪旧工具结果。 | 是否在上下文压力下自动裁剪旧工具结果。 |
| param `trimOldToolResults` | `en` | Advanced setting: trim old tool results when context space is limited. | Trim older tool results automatically under context pressure. |
| param `vision` | `zh-CN` | 当前模型是否支持图片输入。切换模型时省略以采用自动匹配的默认值；仅用户要求覆盖时明确传入。无法识别时按 false 处理。 | 模型是否支持图片输入（切换模型时省略以采用自动匹配的默认值；未知时按 false 处理；仅用户要求覆盖时明确传入）。 |
| param `vision` | `en` | Whether the model accepts image inputs. Omit when switching models to use the detected default; supply only for a user-requested override. Unknown support defaults to false. | Whether the model supports image input (omit when switching models to use the detected default; defaults to false when unknown; supply only on user override). |
| param `requestRateLimit` | `zh-CN` | 高级设置：是否对模型接口请求统一限速，默认关闭。开启后按 RPM/TPM 统一排队，可用于降低模型接口 429/Too Many Requests/rate limit 再次发生的概率；不处理余额、账单或总配额耗尽。 | 是否开启模型接口 RPM/TPM 请求限速排队（用于缓解模型 429/rate limit，默认 false）。 |
| param `requestRateLimit` | `en` | Advanced setting: apply shared rate limiting to model API requests. Disabled by default. When enabled, requests queue by RPM/TPM to reduce recurring 429/Too Many Requests/rate-limit errors. Does not resolve exhausted balances, billing limits, or total quotas. | Enable shared RPM/TPM request queuing for model API calls to mitigate 429/rate limits. Default: false. |
| param `debugMode` | `zh-CN` | 高级设置：调试模式。 | 是否开启调试模式。 |
| param `debugMode` | `en` | Advanced setting: debug mode. | Enable debug mode. |
| param `includeProviders` | `zh-CN` | get/update_config 是否返回 provider 摘要列表。默认 get=true，update_config=false。 | 是否在结果中附带 providers 列表（get 默认 true，update_config 默认 false）。 |
| param `includeProviders` | `en` | Include provider summaries in get/update_config results. Defaults to true for get and false for update_config. | Include provider summaries in the result (defaults to true for get, false for update_config). |
| param `limit` | `zh-CN` | list_models 最多返回模型数量，默认 200，最大 1000。 | list_models 最大返回模型数，默认 200，范围 1–1000。 |
| param `limit` | `en` | Maximum models returned by list_models. Default: 200; maximum: 1000. | Maximum models returned by list_models. Default: 200; range: 1–1000. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `manage_guardrails`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取和修改轻舟安全策略。用于配置访问规则和站点打开/跳转流控；模型配置请使用 qingzhou_management__manage_settings。访问规则支持禁止访问/允许访问，targetType=domain 表示域名及子域名，targetType=pattern 表示 URL 通配规则。流控使用 minOpenIntervalSeconds，单位秒；工具层会在 open_tab/navigate/点击跳转等打开页面动作上全局排队，多个 session 共享。 | 读取和修改轻舟访问安全护栏与站点打开流控（修改前先 get 查看现有规则及顺序）。访问规则支持 block（禁止）和 allow（加白），按列表顺序第一条命中生效；流控使用 minOpenIntervalSeconds（秒）限制打开/跳转同一站点的最小间隔。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read and update qz access rules and navigation rate limits. Use qingzhou_management__manage_settings for model settings. Rules can block or allow access: targetType=domain matches the domain and its subdomains; targetType=pattern matches URL wildcards. Rate limits use minOpenIntervalSeconds in seconds. The tool runtime queues page-opening actions such as open_tab, navigate, and click navigation globally across sessions. | Read and update qz access guardrails and page-opening rate limits (call get first to inspect existing rules and order). Access rules support block and allow, with the first matching rule in order taking effect; rate limits use minOpenIntervalSeconds in seconds between page openings/navigations. |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | 动作。add_rule 需 targetType/target；remove_rule 需 id 或 target；add_rate_limit 需 targetType/target/minOpenIntervalSeconds；remove_rate_limit 需 id 或 target；reorder 需 order。 | 操作类型。必填项：add_rule 需 targetType + target；remove_rule 需 id 或 target；add_rate_limit 需 targetType + target + minOpenIntervalSeconds；remove_rate_limit 需 id 或 target；reorder 需 order。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Operation. add_rule requires targetType/target; remove_rule requires id or target; add_rate_limit requires targetType/target/minOpenIntervalSeconds; remove_rate_limit requires id or target; reorder requires order. | Action. Required fields: add_rule needs targetType + target; remove_rule needs id or target; add_rate_limit needs targetType + target + minOpenIntervalSeconds; remove_rate_limit needs id or target; reorder needs order. |
| param `id` | `zh-CN` | 规则 id，用于 remove_rule/remove_rate_limit/reorder。 | 规则 ID（用于 remove_rule/remove_rate_limit）。 |
| param `id` | `en` | Rule ID for remove_rule/remove_rate_limit/reorder. | Rule ID for remove_rule or remove_rate_limit. |
| param `ruleAction` | `zh-CN` | 访问规则动作。block=禁止访问，allow=允许访问；add_rule 默认 block。 | 访问规则动作：block（禁止访问，add_rule 默认）或 allow（允许访问）。 |
| param `ruleAction` | `en` | Access rule action: block denies access; allow permits it. add_rule defaults to block. | Access rule action: block (default for add_rule) or allow. |
| param `targetType` | `zh-CN` | 匹配类型。domain 匹配域名及子域名；pattern 匹配 URL 通配规则。 | 匹配类型：domain（域名及所有子域名）或 pattern（URL 通配符规则）。 |
| param `targetType` | `en` | Match type: domain matches the domain and subdomains; pattern matches URL wildcards. | Match type: domain (domain and all subdomains) or pattern (URL wildcard pattern). |
| param `target` | `zh-CN` | 匹配目标。domain 例：example.com；pattern 例：*://example.com/private/*。 | 匹配目标（domain 如 example.com；pattern 如 *://example.com/private/*）。 |
| param `target` | `en` | Match target. Domain example: example.com. Pattern example: *://example.com/private/*. | Match target (e.g. example.com for domain, or *://example.com/private/* for pattern). |
| param `minOpenIntervalSeconds` | `zh-CN` | 站点打开/跳转最小间隔，单位秒；仅 add_rate_limit 使用。 | 打开/跳转该站点的最小间隔秒数（add_rate_limit 必填）。 |
| param `minOpenIntervalSeconds` | `en` | Minimum interval between page openings or navigations, in seconds. Used only by add_rate_limit. | Minimum interval in seconds between opening/navigating to the target (required for add_rate_limit). |
| param `enabled` | `zh-CN` | 流控规则是否启用，默认 true；仅 add_rate_limit 使用。 | 流控规则是否启用，默认 true（仅 add_rate_limit 使用）。 |
| param `enabled` | `en` | Whether the rate-limit rule is enabled. Default: true. Used only by add_rate_limit. | Whether the rate-limit rule is enabled. Default: true (for add_rate_limit). |
| param `order` | `zh-CN` | 安全策略顺序，填已有访问规则和流控规则的 id。访问规则先按顺序决定 allow/block；未禁止时，流控再按顺序命中。 | 按优先级排列的规则 ID 数组（用于 reorder）。 |
| param `order` | `en` | Ordered IDs of existing access and rate-limit rules. Access rules determine allow/block in order. If access is not blocked, rate-limit rules are then matched in order. | Ordered array of rule IDs for reorder. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `manage_skill_settings`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取和修改已安装 Skill 自带的配置。读/改 Skill 配置统一使用本工具，不要操作设置页 DOM，也不要直接读写 storage。典型流程：先 list 找到稳定的 settingId，再 get 查看字段定义和当前值，最后 update 传 settingId 与需要改变的 values；update 是局部更新，未传字段保持不变。密钥字段读取时只返回 hasValue，不返回已保存的明文；写入新的密钥值会替换旧值。模型与 Provider 配置请使用 qingzhou_management__manage_settings。 | 读取和修改已安装 Skill 声明的配置项（禁止直接操作设置页 DOM 或 storage）。先 list 获取 settingId，再 get 查看字段定义与当前值，最后 update 传入 settingId 和要修改的 values 补丁（未传字段保持不变；secret 字段读取仅返回 hasValue，传入新值即覆盖）。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read and update settings declared by installed Skills. Always use this tool for Skill settings; do not manipulate the Settings page DOM or read/write storage directly. First list to find a stable settingId, then get the field definitions and current values, then update with settingId and the values to change. update is a partial patch; omitted fields remain unchanged. Secret reads return only hasValue, never stored plaintext. Writing a new secret replaces the old value. Use qingzhou_management__manage_settings for model/provider configuration. | Read and update settings declared by installed Skills (never manipulate the Settings DOM or storage directly). Call list to find settingId, get to inspect field definitions and current values, and update with settingId and a partial values object (omitted fields stay unchanged; secret fields return hasValue only and are overwritten when a new value is supplied). |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | 动作。list 列出带配置的 Skill；get 需 settingId；update 需 settingId 和非空 values。 | 操作类型：list 列出带配置的 Skill；get 需 settingId；update 需 settingId + 非空 values。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Operation. list finds Skills with settings; get requires settingId; update requires settingId and nonempty values. | Action: list lists Skills with settings; get requires settingId; update requires settingId + non-empty values. |
| param `settingId` | `zh-CN` | Skill 配置的稳定 id，来自 list 返回的 settings[].id。get/update 必填。 | Skill 配置 ID（取自 list 返回的 settings[].id，get/update 必填）。 |
| param `settingId` | `en` | Stable Skill settings ID from settings[].id in the list result. Required for get/update. | Skill settings ID from settings[].id in the list result (required for get/update). |
| param `values` | `zh-CN` | update 使用的字段补丁，key 必须来自 get 返回的 setting.fields[].key；未传字段保持不变。 | update 的字段键值补丁对象（key 取自 get 返回的 setting.fields[].key）。 |
| param `values` | `en` | Field patch for update. Keys must come from setting.fields[].key in the get result. Omitted fields remain unchanged. | Partial field key/value patch for update (keys must match setting.fields[].key from get). |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `ask_llm`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 调用 LLM 进行独立分析。用于：数据分析、内容总结、翻译、分类、代码生成、对比等需要深度思考的子任务。<br>通常由 run_script 内部通过 const text = await tools.ask_llm(...) 调用，并由脚本处理返回的字符串、继续落库或决策。 | 在 run_script 内部通过 await tools.ask_llm({prompt, system, data}) 调用 LLM 完成分析、总结、翻译、分类或对比，返回纯文本字符串（data 最长 300,000 字符）。 |
| **description** | `en` | Call an LLM for independent analysis, such as data analysis, summarization, translation, classification, code generation, or comparison. Usually called inside run_script as const text = await tools.ask_llm(...); the script processes the returned string and continues saving data or making decisions. | Call the LLM from inside run_script via await tools.ask_llm({prompt, system, data}) for analysis, summarization, translation, classification, or comparison, returning a plain-text string (data up to 300,000 chars). |
| param `prompt` | `zh-CN` | 提示词 / 问题 | 必填。分析提示词或问题。 |
| param `prompt` | `en` | Prompt or question. | Required prompt or question. |
| param `system` | `zh-CN` | 可选的 system prompt，用于设定角色或输出格式 | 可选 system prompt（设定角色或输出格式）。 |
| param `system` | `en` | Optional system prompt defining the role or output format. | Optional system prompt (role or output format). |
| param `data` | `zh-CN` | 要分析的数据/文本（会追加到 prompt 后） | 可选待分析数据文本（追加在 prompt 之后，最长 300,000 字符）。 |
| param `data` | `en` | Data or text to analyze, appended to the prompt. | Optional data text to analyze (appended after prompt, up to 300,000 chars). |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `load_skill`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 加载一个 skill 的完整指令和真实资源清单。当任务匹配可用 skill 时，必须先调用此工具获取详细步骤再执行；正文在 content 字段中返回，resources 只包含 plugin.json、scripts、references、模板等资源。需要分块读取 references、脚本或模板等单个资源文件时，优先调用 load_skill_resource(name,path)；includeResources:true 仅作整包诊断兜底。返回的 runtime 字段说明 skill 的真实运行环境。 | 加载指定 Skill 的完整 Markdown 指令（content）、资源索引（resources，含 plugin.json、scripts、references 等）及运行环境说明，并在当前会话激活该 Skill 声明的扩展工具。需要读取单个资源文件内容时，调用 load_skill_resource(name, path)。 |
| **description** | `en` | Load a Skill's full instructions and actual resource index. When an available Skill matches the task, load it before following its detailed workflow. The body is returned in content; resources contains only files such as plugin.json, scripts, references, and templates. Prefer load_skill_resource(name,path) to read individual reference, script, or template files in chunks. Use includeResources:true only as a whole-package diagnostic fallback. runtime describes the Skill's actual execution environment. | Load a Skill's full Markdown instructions (content), resource index (resources, including plugin.json, scripts, and references), and runtime guidance, and activate its declared Skill tools for the current session. Call load_skill_resource(name, path) to read an individual resource file. |
| param `name` | `zh-CN` | skill 名称 | 必填。Skill 名称。 |
| param `name` | `en` | Skill name. | Required Skill name. |
| param `includeResources` | `zh-CN` | 是否返回资源源码/content。默认 false，避免把大脚本放进上下文。 | 是否一并返回全部文本资源的源码 content，默认 false（优先用 load_skill_resource 按需读取单文件）。 |
| param `includeResources` | `en` | Return resource source/content. Default: false, to keep large scripts out of context. | Include full content of all text resources. Default: false (prefer load_skill_resource for individual files). |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `load_skill_resource`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 加载 skill 包内单个真实资源文件。文本资源按字符分块返回；DOCX、XLSX、PDF 会自动使用轻舟 document pipeline 解析，返回首块预览与 docModelId，长文档可继续用 qingzhou-documents 的 document_read_chunk 读取。skill 正文请直接使用 load_skill 返回的 content 字段。 | 读取 Skill 包内单个资源文件（path）：文本资源按 start/maxChars 分段返回；DOCX、XLSX、PDF 自动解析并返回首块预览与 docModelId（续读长文档请先 load_skill("qingzhou-documents") 再调 document_read_chunk）。读取 Skill 正文请用 load_skill。 |
| **description** | `en` | Load one actual resource file from a Skill package. Text resources are returned in character-based chunks. DOCX, XLSX, and PDF files are parsed automatically by qz's document pipeline, returning an initial preview and docModelId. Continue long documents with qingzhou-documents document_read_chunk. For the Skill body, use content returned by load_skill. | Read a single resource file (path) from a Skill package: text resources return character slices via start/maxChars; DOCX, XLSX, and PDF resources are parsed automatically and return an initial preview plus docModelId (load qingzhou-documents and call document_read_chunk to read further). Use load_skill for the Skill Markdown body. |
| param `name` | `zh-CN` | skill 名称 | 必填。Skill 名称。 |
| param `name` | `en` | Skill name. | Required Skill name. |
| param `path` | `zh-CN` | skill 包内真实资源相对路径，如 references/checklist.md、references/report.xlsx、references/brief.docx、references/manual.pdf、scripts/collect.js 或 plugin.json | 必填。Skill 包内资源相对路径，如 plugin.json、scripts/collect.js、references/checklist.md、references/report.xlsx。 |
| param `path` | `en` | Actual relative resource path in the Skill package, such as references/checklist.md, references/report.xlsx, references/brief.docx, references/manual.pdf, scripts/collect.js, or plugin.json. | Required relative resource path inside the Skill package, such as plugin.json, scripts/collect.js, references/checklist.md, or references/report.xlsx. |
| param `start` | `zh-CN` | 可选：仅用于文本资源，从第几个字符开始读取，默认 0。 | 文本资源起始字符偏移，默认 0。 |
| param `start` | `en` | Optional character offset for text resources only. Default: 0. | Starting character offset for text resources. Default: 0. |
| param `maxChars` | `zh-CN` | 可选：仅用于文本资源，最多返回多少字符，默认 300000，最大 1000000。 | 文本资源单次最大返回字符数，默认 300000，范围 1–1000000。 |
| param `maxChars` | `en` | Optional maximum characters for text resources only. Default: 300000; maximum: 1000000. | Maximum characters to return for text resources. Default: 300000; range: 1–1000000. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `manage_skill`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 内部工具：创建、更新、删除或列出 skill。仅供技能工作台 Skill 脚本调用；模型不直接使用。 | 内部工具：创建、更新、删除或列出 Skill（仅供技能工作台脚本调用，模型不直接使用）。 |
| **description** | `en` | Internal tool to create, update, delete, or list Skills. Called only by Skill workbench scripts, not directly by the model. | Internal tool to create, update, delete, or list Skills (called by Skill workbench scripts only). |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | skill 管理动作。分支必填：create 需 name、description、content；update/delete 需 name；list_skills 可只传 scope。 | 操作类型：create 需 name + description + content；update/delete 需 name；list_skills 可传 scope。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Skill operation. create requires name, description, and content; update/delete requires name; list_skills may supply only scope. | Action: create needs name + description + content; update/delete needs name; list_skills accepts scope. |
| param `name` | `zh-CN` | 要修改的 skill 名称 | 必填。要更新的 Skill 名称。 |
| param `name` | `en` | Name of the Skill to update. | Required name of the Skill to update. |
| param `renameTo` | `zh-CN` | 可选：重命名 skill | 可选新 Skill 英文 ID。 |
| param `renameTo` | `en` | Optional new Skill name. | Optional new lowercase English Skill ID. |
| param `displayName` | `zh-CN` | 可选：更新中文显示名。标准 name 保持英文 id，中文名写这里。 | 可选：更新中文显示名（保持 name 为英文 id）。 |
| param `displayName` | `en` | Optional localized display name. Keep name as an English ID; put the user-facing name here. | Optional new localized display name (keep name as an English ID). |
| param `description` | `zh-CN` | 可选：更新用途描述 | 可选新用途描述。 |
| param `description` | `en` | Optional updated purpose description. | Optional updated purpose description. |
| param `scope` | `zh-CN` | action=list_skills 时的技能范围 | action=list_skills 的技能范围（all/global/site/resident）。 |
| param `scope` | `en` | Skill scope for action=list_skills. | Skill scope for action=list_skills (all/global/site/resident). |
| param `status` | `zh-CN` | 可选：active/disabled 等状态 | 可选状态（active/disabled）。 |
| param `status` | `en` | Optional status, such as active/disabled. | Optional status (active/disabled). |
| param `domains` | `zh-CN` | 可选：替换适用域名 | 可选：替换适用域名数组。 |
| param `domains` | `en` | Optional replacement domains. | Optional replacement domains array. |
| param `urlPatterns` | `zh-CN` | 可选：替换 URL/path 匹配 | 可选：替换 URL/path 匹配数组。 |
| param `urlPatterns` | `en` | Optional replacement URL/path patterns. | Optional replacement URL/path patterns array. |
| param `excludePatterns` | `zh-CN` | 可选：替换排除规则 | 可选：替换排除规则数组。 |
| param `excludePatterns` | `en` | Optional replacement exclusion patterns. | Optional replacement exclusion patterns array. |
| param `oldText` | `zh-CN` | 可选：Skill Markdown 正文单次精确替换的旧文本；只改正文，不改资源。必须唯一匹配。复杂修改请拆成多次 update_skill。 | 可选：Skill Markdown 正文单次精确替换的唯一旧文本（只改正文，不改资源）。 |
| param `oldText` | `en` | Optional old text for one exact replacement in Skill Markdown, not resources. Must match once. Split complex edits into multiple update_skill calls. | Optional unique old text for one exact replacement in Skill Markdown (modifies Markdown body only, not resources). |
| param `newText` | `zh-CN` | 可选：Skill Markdown 正文单次精确替换的新文本；只改正文，不改资源文件。 | 可选：Skill Markdown 正文单次精确替换的新文本。 |
| param `newText` | `en` | Optional replacement text for Skill Markdown only, not resource files. | Optional replacement text for Skill Markdown. |
| param `prependContent` | `zh-CN` | 可选：追加到正文开头 | 可选：添加到 Markdown 正文开头的文本。 |
| param `prependContent` | `en` | Optional text to prepend to the Markdown content. | Optional text to prepend to the Markdown body. |
| param `appendContent` | `zh-CN` | 可选：追加到正文末尾 | 可选：追加到 Markdown 正文末尾的文本。 |
| param `appendContent` | `en` | Optional text to append to the Markdown content. | Optional text to append to the Markdown body. |
| param `content` | `zh-CN` | 完整 Skill Markdown 正文。写过程知识、前置导航、页面状态判断、失败回退；如有 Skill 工具，再写清何时调用。该字段可能很长，必须在 name/displayName/description/scope/domains/urlPatterns/excludePatterns 之后最后填写。 | 必填。完整 Skill Markdown 正文（写清流程、前置导航、状态校验、工具调用时机与失败回退）。该字段可能很长，必须在元信息字段之后最后填写。 |
| param `content` | `en` | Complete Skill Markdown: procedures, prerequisite navigation, page-state checks, recovery steps, and when to call any Skill tools. This may be long; supply it last, after name/displayName/description/scope/domains/urlPatterns/excludePatterns. | Required complete Skill Markdown body (workflow, prerequisite navigation, state checks, when to call tools, and fallbacks). This field may be long; supply it last after metadata fields. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |
