### `list_tabs`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 列出所有打开的网页标签页及其页面角色和可用性；pageMode 为当前实际 desktop/mobile 模式。active=true 表示用户可见标签页，isWorkingTab=true 表示当前任务工作标签页，ownedByCurrentTask=true 表示当前任务拥有该标签页；claimRequired=true 表示 use_tab 会将该无人认领标签页归属当前任务，readOnlyUsable=true 表示只读页面工具可显式传 tabId 查看。忽略 chrome://、chrome-extension://、devtools://、新标签页等非网页 tab。命中安全策略的标签页仍返回标题、URL、tabId 等元信息，并标记 contentAccess=blocked。 | 列出所有打开的普通网页标签页及角色与可用性（忽略 chrome://、扩展页、新标签页等非网页 tab）：pageMode 为当前 desktop/mobile 模式；active=true 表示用户可见标签页，isWorkingTab=true 表示当前任务工作标签页，ownedByCurrentTask=true 表示当前任务拥有该标签页；claimRequired=true 表示可用 use_tab 认领为当前任务工作标签页；readOnlyUsable=true 表示可用只读页面工具显式传 tabId 查看。命中安全护栏的标签页返回标题/URL/tabId 且标记 contentAccess=blocked。 |
| **description** | `en` | List all open web tabs with their roles and availability; pageMode reports the current desktop/mobile mode. active=true marks the tab visible to the user; isWorkingTab=true marks the current task’s working tab; ownedByCurrentTask=true means the current task owns the tab. claimRequired=true means use_tab will assign the unclaimed tab to the current task. readOnlyUsable=true means read-only page tools may inspect it by passing tabId explicitly. Excludes non-web tabs such as chrome://, chrome-extension://, devtools://, and new-tab pages. Tabs blocked by a security policy still return metadata such as title, URL, and tabId, with contentAccess=blocked. | List all open web tabs with their roles and availability (excluding non-web tabs such as chrome://, extension pages, and new-tab pages): pageMode reports desktop/mobile mode; active=true marks the user-visible tab; isWorkingTab=true marks the current task's working tab; ownedByCurrentTask=true means the current task owns the tab; claimRequired=true means use_tab can claim the unclaimed tab; readOnlyUsable=true means read-only page tools can inspect it with an explicit tabId. Tabs blocked by security guardrails return title/URL/tabId with contentAccess=blocked. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `get_working_tab`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取当前任务已存在的工作标签页。没有工作标签页时返回 hasWorkingTab=false；存在时返回 hasWorkingTab=true、pageMode（当前实际 desktop/mobile 模式）及页面元信息。若命中安全策略，仍返回标题、URL、tabId，并标记 contentAccess=blocked；后续不要读取页面内容。 | 读取当前任务的工作标签页。没有工作标签页时返回 hasWorkingTab=false；存在时返回 hasWorkingTab=true、pageMode 及页面元信息。若命中安全护栏，标记 contentAccess=blocked，后续不要读取其页面内容。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read the current task’s existing working tab. Returns hasWorkingTab=false if none exists, or hasWorkingTab=true with page metadata and the current desktop/mobile pageMode if it does. A tab blocked by a security policy still returns title, URL, and tabId with contentAccess=blocked; do not subsequently read its page content. | Read the current task's working tab. Returns hasWorkingTab=false if none exists, or hasWorkingTab=true with pageMode and tab metadata. If blocked by security guardrails, returns contentAccess=blocked; do not read its page content. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `open_tab`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 打开新标签页，等待目标文档的导航提交后返回，可接着调用页面读取工具；不等待所有资源或动态内容加载完成。若需要捕获新页面首屏、重定向或加载期接口，传 watchNetwork:true；工具会先创建空白页并在导航前开启 Network 监听，再跳到目标 URL，之后可直接用 network_recent 查看首屏请求。监听已打开页面中点击、搜索、提交等后续交互的请求时，使用 network_watch。 | 打开新网页标签页并设为当前任务工作标签页，等待文档导航提交后返回（可紧接调用页面读取工具）。需捕获首屏、重定向或加载期接口时传 watchNetwork:true（导航前开启 Network 监听，加载后用 network_recent / network_body 查看）；监听已打开页面中点击、搜索等后续交互请求用 network_watch。 |
| **description** | `en` | Open a new tab and wait for the destination document's navigation to commit before returning. Page-reading tools can follow immediately; resources and dynamic content may still be loading. To capture requests made during the initial page load or redirects, including API calls, pass watchNetwork:true. The tool creates a blank tab, starts network monitoring before navigation, then opens the target URL. Use network_recent afterward to inspect initial requests. Use network_watch to capture requests from later interactions on an open page, such as clicking, searching, or submitting. | Open a new web tab as the current task's working tab and return once navigation commits (page-reading tools can follow immediately). Pass watchNetwork:true to start network monitoring before navigation and capture initial load and redirect requests (inspect with network_recent / network_body); use network_watch for subsequent interaction requests on an open page. |
| param `url` | `zh-CN` | 要打开的 HTTP/HTTPS 或已获文件网址访问权限的 file:// URL；需要移动网页时同时设置 mode:mobile，首次请求即使用移动模式。 | 要打开的 HTTP/HTTPS URL（或已开启文件网址访问权限的 file:// URL）；需要移动版页面时同时传 mode:"mobile"。 |
| param `url` | `en` | HTTP/HTTPS URL or a file:// URL permitted by Chrome file access. For a mobile page, also set mode:mobile so the very first request uses mobile mode. | HTTP/HTTPS URL (or a file:// URL when file URL access is enabled). Set mode:"mobile" to load in mobile mode from the first request. |
| param `mode` | `zh-CN` | desktop（桌面端，默认）或 mobile（Android Chrome，412×915，支持触摸）。 | desktop（桌面端，默认）或 mobile（Android Chrome，412×915，支持触摸）。 |
| param `mode` | `en` | desktop (default) or mobile (Android Chrome, 412×915, touch enabled). | desktop (default) or mobile (Android Chrome, 412×915, touch enabled). |
| param `watchNetwork` | `zh-CN` | 打开新标签页时是否在真正导航前开启 Network 监听；用于抓首屏、重定向、页面加载期请求。页面已打开后的交互监听请用 network_watch。 | 是否在导航前开启 Network 监听以捕获首屏和重定向请求，默认 false；页面已打开后的交互监听请用 network_watch。 |
| param `watchNetwork` | `en` | Whether to start network monitoring before navigating the new tab, to capture requests made during the initial page load or redirects. Use network_watch for interactions on an already open page. | Start network monitoring before navigation to capture initial load and redirect requests. Default: false. Use network_watch for interactions on an already open page. |
| param `resetNetwork` | `zh-CN` | watchNetwork=true 时是否清空该 tab 的已有记录，默认 true | watchNetwork=true 时是否清空该 tab 已有网络记录，默认 true。 |
| param `resetNetwork` | `en` | Whether to clear existing records for this tab when watchNetwork=true. Default: true. | Clear existing network records for this tab when watchNetwork=true. Default: true. |
| param `networkMaxRecords` | `zh-CN` | watchNetwork=true 时最多保留请求数，默认 200，范围 20-1000 | watchNetwork=true 时最多保留的请求数，默认 200，范围 20–1000。 |
| param `networkMaxRecords` | `en` | Maximum number of requests to retain when watchNetwork=true. Default: 200; range: 20–1000. | Maximum network requests to retain when watchNetwork=true. Default: 200; range: 20–1000. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `set_page_mode`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 切换当前任务拥有的页面的桌面/移动模式。实际变化会自动刷新页面，相同模式不刷新。刷新后重新观察页面；旧元素引用和截图坐标不可继续使用。不要在未保存表单时切换。移动模式在本轮任务完成或停止后保持，直到切回桌面、关闭标签页或删除任务。导航导致调试目标丢失时自动尝试无刷新恢复；若恢复失败或用户主动断开，下一次页面操作会恢复移动模式并刷新，请先重新观察。 | 切换当前任务拥有页面的 desktop / mobile 模式。模式变化会自动刷新页面（相同模式不刷新）；切换后必须重新观察页面，禁止复用旧元素 target 或截图坐标。有未保存表单时不要切换。 |
| **description** | `en` | Switch a task-owned page between desktop and mobile mode. A change automatically reloads the page; the same mode does not reload. Observe the page again afterward; old element references and screenshot coordinates are stale. Avoid switching with unsaved form input. Mobile mode persists after a run completes or stops, until switching to desktop, closing the tab, or deleting the task. Navigation-related target loss triggers an automatic restore without reloading. If it fails or the user disconnects the debugger, the next page tool restores mobile mode and reloads; observe again first. | Switch a task-owned page between desktop and mobile mode. Changing the mode reloads the page (setting the same mode does not reload); re-observe the page afterward and never reuse old element targets or screenshot coordinates. Do not switch modes with unsaved form input. |
| param `mode` | `zh-CN` | desktop（桌面端，默认）或 mobile（Android Chrome，412×915，支持触摸）。 | desktop（桌面端，默认）或 mobile（Android Chrome，412×915，支持触摸）。 |
| param `mode` | `en` | desktop (default) or mobile (Android Chrome, 412×915, touch enabled). | desktop (default) or mobile (Android Chrome, 412×915, touch enabled). |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `use_tab`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 将指定网页标签页设为当前任务工作标签页，用户可见标签页保持不变。适用于当前任务已拥有或无人认领的网页标签页；归属其他任务的标签页可由只读页面工具显式传 tabId 查看。 | 将当前任务已拥有或未归属的网页标签页设为当前任务工作标签页，用户可见标签页保持不变。归属其他任务的只读标签页无需调用本工具，直接用只读页面工具显式传 tabId 查看。 |
| **description** | `en` | Set the specified web tab as the current task’s working tab, without changing the tab visible to the user. Accepts tabs already owned by the current task or unclaimed tabs. To inspect a tab owned by another task, pass tabId explicitly to a read-only page tool. | Set a task-owned or unclaimed web tab as the current task's working tab; the user-visible tab remains unchanged. For a read-only tab owned by another task, pass tabId directly to a read-only page tool instead. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 标签页 ID | 目标标签页 ID。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Tab ID. | Target tab ID. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `navigate`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 在当前任务的工作标签页或当前任务拥有的指定标签页中导航到新 URL，等待本次导航提交后返回，避免后续读取旧页面；不等待所有资源或动态内容加载完成。若需要捕获这次跳转的首屏、重定向或加载期接口，传 watchNetwork:true；工具会先在原 tab 上开启 Network 监听，再执行导航，之后可直接用 network_recent 查看本次导航请求。监听当前页面中点击、搜索、提交等后续交互的请求时，使用 network_watch。 | 在当前任务拥有的标签页中导航到新 URL，等待导航提交后返回。需捕获本次跳转的首屏、重定向或加载期接口时传 watchNetwork:true，随后用 network_recent / network_body 查看本次导航请求；监听页面加载后后续交互的请求用 network_watch。 |
| **description** | `en` | Navigate to a new URL in the current task’s working tab or a specified tab it owns. Waits for this navigation to commit so subsequent tools do not read the old page; resources and dynamic content may still be loading. To capture requests made during the initial page load or redirects, including API calls, pass watchNetwork:true. The tool starts network monitoring on the existing tab before navigating; use network_recent afterward to inspect this navigation’s requests. Use network_watch to capture requests from later interactions on the current page, such as clicking, searching, or submitting. | Navigate a task-owned tab to a new URL and return once navigation commits. Pass watchNetwork:true to capture initial load and redirect requests for this navigation, then inspect them with network_recent / network_body; use network_watch for subsequent interaction requests after loading. |
| param `url` | `zh-CN` | 目标 HTTP/HTTPS 或已获文件网址访问权限的 file:// URL | 目标 HTTP/HTTPS URL（或已开启文件网址访问权限的 file:// URL）。 |
| param `url` | `en` | Destination HTTP/HTTPS URL or a file:// URL permitted by Chrome file access. | Destination HTTP/HTTPS URL (or a file:// URL when file URL access is enabled). |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 本次调用的目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Target tab ID for this call. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `watchNetwork` | `zh-CN` | 本次导航前是否开启 Network 监听；用于抓跳转首屏、重定向、加载期请求。页面已加载后的交互监听请用 network_watch。 | 是否在本次导航前开启 Network 监听以捕获首屏和重定向请求，默认 false；页面已加载后的交互监听请用 network_watch。 |
| param `watchNetwork` | `en` | Whether to start network monitoring before this navigation, to capture requests made during the initial page load or redirects. Use network_watch for interactions after the page has loaded. | Start network monitoring before this navigation to capture initial load and redirect requests. Default: false. Use network_watch for interactions after the page has loaded. |
| param `resetNetwork` | `zh-CN` | watchNetwork=true 时是否清空该 tab 的已有记录，默认 true | watchNetwork=true 时是否清空该 tab 已有网络记录，默认 true。 |
| param `resetNetwork` | `en` | Whether to clear existing records for this tab when watchNetwork=true. Default: true. | Clear existing network records for this tab when watchNetwork=true. Default: true. |
| param `networkMaxRecords` | `zh-CN` | watchNetwork=true 时最多保留请求数，默认 200，范围 20-1000 | watchNetwork=true 时最多保留的请求数，默认 200，范围 20–1000。 |
| param `networkMaxRecords` | `en` | Maximum number of requests to retain when watchNetwork=true. Default: 200; range: 20–1000. | Maximum network requests to retain when watchNetwork=true. Default: 200; range: 20–1000. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `wait_for_user`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 暂停并等待用户完成登录、验证码、授权、本地文件选择等必须亲自操作的步骤。侧边栏会显示提示，用户完成操作后点击[继续]，Agent 自动恢复。 | 暂停并等待用户完成登录、验证码、授权、付款、本地文件选择等必须亲自操作的步骤。侧边栏显示提示，用户点击[继续]后自动恢复。 |
| **description** | `en` | Pause for the user to complete a step requiring direct interaction, such as signing in, solving a CAPTCHA, granting permission, or selecting a local file. The side panel displays the instructions; the user clicks [Continue] when finished, and the agent resumes automatically. | Pause for the user to complete a step requiring direct page interaction, such as signing in, solving a CAPTCHA, authorizing access, paying, or selecting a local file. Resumes when the user clicks [Continue]. |
| param `message` | `zh-CN` | 展示给用户的提示信息，说明需要用户做什么 | 展示给用户的提示，说明需要用户在页面完成什么操作。 |
| param `message` | `en` | Instructions shown to the user explaining what they need to do. | Instructions shown to the user explaining what to do on the page. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 等待完成后要观测的标签页 ID；不填则用当前 session 的工作标签页 | 恢复后观测的标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | ID of the tab to observe after the wait. Defaults to the current session’s working tab. | Tab ID to observe after resuming. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |

### `ask_user`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 向用户展示一个简洁的结构化表单，帮助澄清需求后再继续。适合在需求不明确、需要用户在多个选项中选择、或需要补充少量字段时使用。提交后返回用户填写的 answers。 | 向用户展示结构化表单并返回提交的 answers。用于澄清需求、提供选项、收集字段，或在向外部系统执行发送/提交/发布/创建/修改/删除动作前请求确认。 |
| **description** | `en` | Show a concise structured form to clarify the user’s request before continuing. Use when requirements are unclear, the user needs to choose between options, or a few details are missing. Returns the user’s answers after submission. | Show a structured form to the user and return submitted answers. Use to clarify requirements, offer choices, collect fields, or confirm before sending, submitting, publishing, creating, modifying, or deleting external data. |
| param `title` | `zh-CN` | 表单标题，默认“帮我补充信息” | 表单标题，默认“帮我补充信息”。 |
| param `title` | `en` | Form title. Default: “Provide more information”. | Form title. Default: “Provide more information”. |
| param `message` | `zh-CN` | 给用户的简短说明，说明为什么需要这些信息 | 简短说明为什么需要这些信息或确认。 |
| param `message` | `en` | Brief explanation of why this information is needed. | Brief explanation of why this information or confirmation is needed. |
| param `submitLabel` | `zh-CN` | 提交按钮文案，默认“提交” | 提交按钮文案，默认“提交”。 |
| param `submitLabel` | `en` | Submit button label. Default: “Submit”. | Submit button label. Default: “Submit”. |
| param `fields` | `zh-CN` | 表单字段，最多 8 个。支持 text、textarea、single_choice、multi_choice。 | 表单字段数组（最多 8 个），支持 text、textarea、single_choice、multi_choice。 |
| param `fields` | `en` | Up to 8 form fields. Supports text, textarea, single_choice, and multi_choice. | Up to 8 form fields; supports text, textarea, single_choice, and multi_choice. |
| param `fields[].id` | `zh-CN` | 字段 ID，用于返回 answers 的 key | 字段 ID（作为返回 answers 的 key）。 |
| param `fields[].id` | `en` | Field ID, used as the key in the returned answers. | Field ID (used as the key in returned answers). |
| param `fields[].label` | `zh-CN` | 字段标题 | 字段标题。 |
| param `fields[].label` | `en` | Field label. | Field label. |
| param `fields[].type` | `zh-CN` | 字段类型 | 字段类型，默认 text。 |
| param `fields[].type` | `en` | Field type. | Field type. Default: text. |
| param `fields[].presentation` | `zh-CN` | 选择题可设为 cards，以竖排卡片展示候选结果，最多 50 项。 | 选择题可设为 cards 以卡片展示（最多 50 项）。 |
| param `fields[].presentation` | `en` | Set to cards for a vertical result-selection list, with up to 50 choices. | Set to cards on choice fields for card selection (up to 50 options). |
| param `fields[].placeholder` | `zh-CN` | 输入提示 | 输入占位提示。 |
| param `fields[].placeholder` | `en` | Input placeholder. | Input placeholder. |
| param `fields[].help` | `zh-CN` | 补充说明，小字显示 | 字段补充说明。 |
| param `fields[].help` | `en` | Additional help text, displayed in smaller text. | Additional help text. |
| param `fields[].defaultValue` | `zh-CN` | 建议值/默认值。text/textarea/single_choice 传字符串。用户可直接提交或修改。 | text/textarea/single_choice 的默认字符串值。 |
| param `fields[].defaultValue` | `en` | Suggested/default value. Use a string for text/textarea/single_choice. The user can accept or edit it. | Default string value for text/textarea/single_choice. |
| param `fields[].defaultValues` | `zh-CN` | multi_choice 的默认勾选值数组。用户可直接提交或修改。 | multi_choice 的默认勾选值数组。 |
| param `fields[].defaultValues` | `en` | Array of initially selected values for multi_choice. The user can accept or edit it. | Default selected values array for multi_choice. |
| param `fields[].required` | `zh-CN` | 是否必填，默认 true | 是否必填，默认 true。 |
| param `fields[].required` | `en` | Whether the field is required. Default: true. | Whether required. Default: true. |
| param `fields[].options` | `zh-CN` | 选择题选项。每项为 {label,value,description}；value 不填时可与 label 相同。 | 选择题选项数组 {label, value, description}（value 省略时同 label）。 |
| param `fields[].options` | `en` | Choice options. Each entry is {label,value,description}; if omitted, value may be the same as label. | Choice options array {label, value, description} (value defaults to label). |
| param `fields[].options[].meta` | `zh-CN` | 卡片标签，最多 6 个，每个最多 80 字符。 | 卡片短标签（最多 6 个，每个最多 80 字符）。 |
| param `fields[].options[].meta` | `en` | Card metadata: up to 6 labels of 80 characters each. | Card metadata tags (up to 6 tags, 80 chars each). |
| param `fields[].options[].imageUrl` | `zh-CN` | 卡片缩略图的 HTTP(S) 地址。 | 卡片缩略图 HTTP(S) URL。 |
| param `fields[].options[].imageUrl` | `en` | HTTP(S) URL for the card thumbnail. | Card thumbnail HTTP(S) URL. |
| param `fields[].options[].url` | `zh-CN` | 卡片来源链接的 HTTP(S) 地址。 | 卡片来源 HTTP(S) URL。 |
| param `fields[].options[].url` | `en` | HTTP(S) source link for the card. | Card source HTTP(S) URL. |

### `wait_for_navigation`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 等待标签页加载完成。在 click/navigate 后调用。默认只返回加载状态、URL、标题；already_complete 只表示调用时页面已完成加载，不证明上一动作成功或失败。只有需要视觉确认、弹窗/验证码判断时才传 screenshot:true。 | 等待标签页加载完成，返回 status、url、title。already_complete 只表示调用时页面已完成加载，不证明上一动作成功或失败。仅需视觉确认弹窗或验证码时传 screenshot:true。 |
| **description** | `en` | Wait for a tab to finish loading. Use after click/navigate. By default returns only loading status, URL, and title. already_complete means the page was already loaded when called; it does not prove the preceding action succeeded or failed. Pass screenshot:true only when visual confirmation is needed, such as checking a dialog or CAPTCHA. | Wait for a tab to finish loading, returning status, url, and title. already_complete means the page was already loaded when called; it does not prove the preceding action succeeded or failed. Pass screenshot:true only for visual checks such as dialogs or CAPTCHAs. |
| **noImages.description** | `zh-CN` | 等待标签页加载完成。在 click/navigate 后调用。默认只返回加载状态、URL、标题；already_complete 只表示调用时页面已完成加载，不证明上一动作成功或失败。当前模型不支持图片，加载后请用 get_ax_tree/read_article/find_elements 确认状态。 | 等待标签页加载完成，返回 status、url、title。already_complete 只表示调用时页面已完成加载，不证明上一动作成功或失败。当前模型不支持图片，加载后用 get_ax_tree/read_article/find_elements 确认状态。 |
| **noImages.description** | `en` | Wait for a tab to finish loading. Use after click/navigate. By default returns only loading status, URL, and title. already_complete means the page was already loaded when called; it does not prove the preceding action succeeded or failed. The current model does not support images. Use get_ax_tree/read_article/find_elements to verify the state after loading. | Wait for a tab to finish loading, returning status, url, and title. already_complete means the page was already loaded when called; it does not prove the preceding action succeeded or failed. The current model does not support images; verify state with get_ax_tree/read_article/find_elements. |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 标签页 ID；不填时使用当前任务工作标签页 | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Tab ID. Defaults to the current task’s working tab. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 超时秒数，默认 10 | 超时秒数，默认 10。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Timeout in seconds. Default: 10. | Timeout in seconds. Default: 10. |
| param `screenshot` | `zh-CN` | 是否在加载完成后截图，默认 false。需要视觉确认时设为 true。 | 加载完成后是否截图，默认 false。 |
| param `screenshot` | `en` | Whether to take a screenshot after loading. Default: false. Enable for visual confirmation. | Capture a screenshot after loading. Default: false. |
| param `fromUrl` | `zh-CN` | 可选：上一动作前的 URL，用于判断当前 URL 是否已变化 | 可选：动作前 URL，用于计算 urlChanged。 |
| param `fromUrl` | `en` | Optional URL from before the preceding action, used to check whether the URL has changed. | Optional pre-action URL to compute urlChanged. |
| param `fromTitle` | `zh-CN` | 可选：上一动作前的标题，用于判断当前标题是否已变化 | 可选：动作前标题，用于计算 titleChanged。 |
| param `fromTitle` | `en` | Optional title from before the preceding action, used to check whether the title has changed. | Optional pre-action title to compute titleChanged. |

### `wait_for_element`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 等待页面上出现指定元素。适合等待动态加载内容、弹窗消失、搜索结果渲染等场景。 | 等待页面出现指定元素（或 absent:true 等待其消失）。 |
| **description** | `en` | Wait for a specified element to appear on the page. Useful for dynamic content, dismissing dialogs, and search-result rendering. | Wait for a matching element to appear on the page (or disappear when absent:true). |
| param `tabId` | `zh-CN` | 标签页 ID | 目标标签页 ID；省略时使用当前任务工作标签页。 |
| param `tabId` | `en` | Tab ID. | Target tab ID. Defaults to the current task's working tab. |
| param `selector` | `zh-CN` | 等待出现的元素 CSS 选择器；可传原始 CSS 或 css:...。不支持 uid:...，因为 uid 是快照引用，不适合等待未来 DOM 状态。 | 目标 CSS 选择器（支持原始 CSS 或 css:...，不支持 uid:... 快照引用）。 |
| param `selector` | `en` | CSS selector for the element to wait for. Accepts raw CSS or css:.... Does not support uid:... because a uid is a snapshot reference, unsuitable for waiting on future DOM state. | Target CSS selector (accepts raw CSS or css:...; uid:... is not supported). |
| param `absent` | `zh-CN` | 反转：等待元素消失，默认 false | 是否等待元素消失，默认 false。 |
| param `absent` | `en` | Invert the condition: wait for the element to disappear. Default: false. | Wait for the element to disappear. Default: false. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 超时秒数，默认 10 | 超时秒数，默认 10。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Timeout in seconds. Default: 10. | Timeout in seconds. Default: 10. |
| param `intervalSeconds` | `zh-CN` | 轮询间隔秒数，默认 0.5 | 轮询间隔秒数，默认 0.5。 |
| param `intervalSeconds` | `en` | Polling interval in seconds. Default: 0.5. | Polling interval in seconds. Default: 0.5. |

### `wait`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 等待指定秒数。用于页面动画、延迟加载等场景。 | 等待指定秒数（等待网络或元素时优先用 wait_for_network_idle / wait_for_element）。 |
| **description** | `en` | Wait for the specified number of seconds. Useful for page animations, delayed loading, and similar situations. | Wait for the specified seconds (prefer wait_for_network_idle or wait_for_element for network or DOM state). |
| param `seconds` | `zh-CN` | 等待秒数 | 等待秒数。 |
| param `seconds` | `en` | Number of seconds to wait. | Seconds to wait. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 工具硬超时，单位秒。不填时系统按工具类型给出默认值。 | 超时秒数；省略时用默认超时。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Hard timeout in seconds. If omitted, the runtime uses the default for this tool type. | Timeout in seconds; defaults to the tool default. |

### `wait_until`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 等到指定的日期和时间再继续。适合让定时任务提前启动并先做准备，然后等到正式执行时间。最长等待 2 小时；更久请创建或调整定时任务。指定时间已过时立即继续。until 推荐使用带时区的 ISO 8601；也兼容 YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss（按北京时间解释）。 | 等到指定日期时间再继续（已过时立即返回 pastDue:true）。适合定时任务提前启动准备后等待正式执行时间；最长等待 2 小时，更久请用定时任务。 |
| **description** | `en` | Wait until a specified date and time before continuing. Useful when an automation starts early to prepare, then waits for its execution time. Maximum wait: 2 hours; for longer delays, create or adjust an automation. Continues immediately if the time has already passed. Prefer ISO 8601 with a time zone for until. Also accepts YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss, interpreted as Beijing time. | Wait until a specified date and time before continuing (returns immediately with pastDue:true if past). Useful when a scheduled task starts early to prepare; maximum wait: 2 hours (use automations for longer delays). |
| param `until` | `zh-CN` | 要等到的日期和时间。推荐带时区的 ISO 8601，例如 2026-07-17T12:00:00+08:00；也可传 YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss，此格式固定按北京时间解释。最长等待 2 小时。 | 目标日期时间（最长 2 小时）。推荐带时区的 ISO 8601（如 2026-07-17T12:00:00+08:00），也兼容按北京时间解释的 YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss。 |
| param `until` | `en` | Date and time to wait until. Prefer ISO 8601 with a time zone, such as 2026-07-17T12:00:00+08:00. Also accepts YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss, always interpreted as Beijing time. Maximum wait: 2 hours. | Target date and time (max 2 hours). Prefer ISO 8601 with time zone (e.g. 2026-07-17T12:00:00+08:00); also accepts YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss in Beijing time. |

### `qingzhou_skill_author__load_available_renderers`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 读取轻舟当前支持的 UI renderer 类型、用途和返回 schema。创建带 UI 的 Skill 前先调用它。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需要前置导航、多步流程或页面上下文，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整过程知识；Skill 脚本只封装稳定可执行步骤，不替代 SKILL.md。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）让模型主动判断本次 Skill 脚本执行上限；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=table: 展示结构化行列数据。<br>- 默认 return { rows: [...] }；如果 renderer 配置了 itemsPath/valuePath，则该路径必须是数组。<br>- rows 每项至少包含字段：type, title, returnShape, description。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 | 读取轻舟支持的 UI renderer 类型、用途与返回 JSON schema（设计带 UI 的 Skill 工具前先调用）。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需前置导航或多步流程，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整 SOP。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）：<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=table: 展示结构化行列数据。<br>- 默认 return { rows: [...] }；如果 renderer 配置了 itemsPath/valuePath，则该路径必须是数组。<br>- rows 每项至少包含字段：type, title, returnShape, description。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 |
| **description** | `en` | Read supported UI renderer types, purposes, and return schemas. Call this before creating a Skill with a custom result view.<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task needs prior navigation, multiple steps, or page context, first call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow. Skill scripts provide stable executable steps; they do not replace SKILL.md.<br>You must supply timeout in seconds after deciding how long this Skill script may run. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=table: Show structured rows and columns.<br>- Return { rows: [...] } by default. If itemsPath/valuePath is configured, that path must contain an array.<br>- Minimum fields for each rows entry: type, title, returnShape, description.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. | List supported UI renderer types, purposes, and return JSON schemas (call before designing a Skill tool with a result UI).<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task requires prior navigation or multiple steps, call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow first.<br>Required timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=table: Show structured rows and columns.<br>- Return { rows: [...] } by default. If itemsPath/valuePath is configured, that path must contain an array.<br>- Minimum fields for each rows entry: type, title, returnShape, description.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 必填。Skill 脚本硬超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 | 必填。Skill 脚本超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Required. Hard timeout for the Skill script, in seconds. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds. | Required. Skill script timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. |

### `qingzhou_skill_author__import_page_zip`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |

| **description** | `zh-CN` | 接收 Agent 已发现的 Zip URL，使用当前任务网页标签页的认证上下文请求 Skill Zip，完成解压、整包校验并直接创建 Skill。本工具不探测或猜测 URL；默认拒绝覆盖同名 Skill。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需要前置导航、多步流程或页面上下文，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整过程知识；Skill 脚本只封装稳定可执行步骤，不替代 SKILL.md。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）让模型主动判断本次 Skill 脚本执行上限；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=summary: 展示一个对象的主标题、副标题和少量指标字段。<br>- return 一个对象，顶层或对应路径包含字段：names.0, url, count, overwrittenCount。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 | 使用当前任务工作标签页的认证上下文下载并导入已确认的 Skill Zip URL（不探测或猜测 URL，默认不覆盖同名 Skill）。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需前置导航或多步流程，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整 SOP。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）：<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=summary: 展示一个对象的主标题、副标题和少量指标字段。<br>- return 一个对象，顶层或对应路径包含字段：names.0, url, count, overwrittenCount。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 |
| **description** | `en` | Import a Skill ZIP from a URL already discovered by the agent, using the current task tab's authenticated context. Extracts and validates the whole package before creating Skills. Does not discover or guess URLs; refuses to overwrite existing names by default.<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task needs prior navigation, multiple steps, or page context, first call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow. Skill scripts provide stable executable steps; they do not replace SKILL.md.<br>You must supply timeout in seconds after deciding how long this Skill script may run. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=summary: Show an object's main title, subtitle, and a few metric fields.<br>- Return an object with these fields at the top level or configured paths: names.0, url, count, overwrittenCount.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. | Download and import a confirmed Skill ZIP URL using the current working tab's authenticated context (does not scan or guess URLs; refuses to overwrite by default).<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task requires prior navigation or multiple steps, call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow first.<br>Required timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=summary: Show an object's main title, subtitle, and a few metric fields.<br>- Return an object with these fields at the top level or configured paths: names.0, url, count, overwrittenCount.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 必填。Skill 脚本硬超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 | 必填。Skill 脚本超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Required. Hard timeout for the Skill script, in seconds. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds. | Required. Skill script timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. |
| param `url` | `zh-CN` | 当前页面中的 Skill Zip 完整下载地址，仅支持 http(s)。下载会复用该页面登录态。 | 必填。当前工作标签页上已确认的 Skill Zip 完整 http(s) 下载地址（复用该页认证态）。 |
| param `url` | `en` | Complete http(s) Skill ZIP download URL from the current page. The download reuses that page's authenticated context. | Required confirmed http(s) Skill ZIP download URL from the current working tab (reuses that tab's authenticated context). |
| param `overwrite` | `zh-CN` | 是否覆盖本地同名 Skill，默认 false；只有用户明确确认覆盖后才传 true。 | 是否覆盖本地同名 Skill，默认 false（仅用户明确确认后传 true）。 |
| param `overwrite` | `en` | Whether to overwrite local Skills with the same name. Defaults to false. Pass true only after explicit user confirmation. | Overwrite local Skills with the same name. Default: false (pass true only after explicit user confirmation). |

### `qingzhou_skill_author__install_skill_to_git`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 把一个已保存的本地 Skill 安装到发布适配器支持的仓库根页或 tree 目录，创建或更新 SKILL.md 与资源并提交一次 commit。仅在用户明确要求写入目标仓库时调用。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需要前置导航、多步流程或页面上下文，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整过程知识；Skill 脚本只封装稳定可执行步骤，不替代 SKILL.md。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）让模型主动判断本次 Skill 脚本执行上限；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=summary: 展示一个对象的主标题、副标题和少量指标字段。<br>- return 一个对象，顶层或对应路径包含字段：skillName, targetPath, fileCount, createdCount, updatedCount, shortId。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 | 将已保存的本地 Skill 写入受支持 Git 仓库根页或 tree 目录并提交一次 commit（仅在用户明确要求提交到仓库时调用）。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需前置导航或多步流程，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整 SOP。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）：<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=summary: 展示一个对象的主标题、副标题和少量指标字段。<br>- return 一个对象，顶层或对应路径包含字段：skillName, targetPath, fileCount, createdCount, updatedCount, shortId。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 |
| **description** | `en` | Install a saved local Skill into a repository root or tree directory supported by the publishing adapter. Creates or updates SKILL.md and resources in one commit. Call only when the user explicitly requests a write to the target repository.<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task needs prior navigation, multiple steps, or page context, first call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow. Skill scripts provide stable executable steps; they do not replace SKILL.md.<br>You must supply timeout in seconds after deciding how long this Skill script may run. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=summary: Show an object's main title, subtitle, and a few metric fields.<br>- Return an object with these fields at the top level or configured paths: skillName, targetPath, fileCount, createdCount, updatedCount, shortId.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. | Write a saved local Skill to a supported Git repository root or tree directory in a single commit (call only when explicitly requested by the user).<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task requires prior navigation or multiple steps, call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow first.<br>Required timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=summary: Show an object's main title, subtitle, and a few metric fields.<br>- Return an object with these fields at the top level or configured paths: skillName, targetPath, fileCount, createdCount, updatedCount, shortId.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 必填。Skill 脚本硬超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 | 必填。Skill 脚本超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Required. Hard timeout for the Skill script, in seconds. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds. | Required. Skill script timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. |
| param `name` | `zh-CN` | 要安装到 Git 仓库的本地 Skill 名称。 | 必填。要安装到 Git 仓库的本地 Skill 名称。 |
| param `name` | `en` | Name of the saved local Skill to install in Git. | Required local Skill name to install into Git. |
| param `repositoryUrl` | `zh-CN` | 发布适配器支持的仓库根页或 tree 目录页地址；tree 目录决定 Skill 的安装父目录。 | 必填。发布适配器支持的 Git 仓库根页或 tree 目录页 URL。 |
| param `repositoryUrl` | `en` | Repository-root or tree-directory URL supported by the publishing adapter. The tree directory is the Skill's installation parent directory. | Required repository root or tree directory URL supported by the publishing adapter. |
| param `commitMessage` | `zh-CN` | 可选 Git commit message；不填时使用稳定的默认提交说明。 | 可选 Git commit message；省略时使用默认提交说明。 |
| param `commitMessage` | `en` | Optional Git commit message. If omitted, uses a stable default message. | Optional Git commit message; uses a default message if omitted. |

### `qingzhou_skill_author__create_skill`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |

| **description** | `zh-CN` | 创建一个轻舟 skill，只写 Skill Markdown 正文和顶层元信息。普通 skill 不需要资源；带工具的 Skill 创建后，再用“编辑技能资源”逐个写入 plugin.json、scripts/*.js 或 references/*.md。不要在本工具里传资源数组。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需要前置导航、多步流程或页面上下文，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整过程知识；Skill 脚本只封装稳定可执行步骤，不替代 SKILL.md。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）让模型主动判断本次 Skill 脚本执行上限；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=skill: 展示创建、资源编辑、更新、删除 skill 的专用状态。<br>- return manage_skill 风格结果对象，包含 action/name/saved 或 updated/deleted 等状态字段。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 | 创建新 Skill 的 Markdown 正文（content）和顶层元信息。带工具的 Skill 先用本工具创建正文，再用 edit_skill_resource 逐个写入 plugin.json、scripts/*.js 或 references/*.md。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需前置导航或多步流程，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整 SOP。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）：<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=skill: 展示创建、资源编辑、更新、删除 skill 的专用状态。<br>- return manage_skill 风格结果对象，包含 action/name/saved 或 updated/deleted 等状态字段。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 |
| **description** | `en` | Create a qz Skill with Markdown content and top-level metadata only. Ordinary skills need no resources. For tools, create the Skill first, then use the resource-editing tool to write plugin.json, scripts/*.js, or references/*.md one file at a time. Do not pass a resource array here.<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task needs prior navigation, multiple steps, or page context, first call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow. Skill scripts provide stable executable steps; they do not replace SKILL.md.<br>You must supply timeout in seconds after deciding how long this Skill script may run. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=skill: Show the status of Skill creation, resource edits, updates, or deletion.<br>- Return a manage_skill-style result with status fields such as action/name/saved or updated/deleted.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. | Create a new Skill's Markdown body (content) and top-level metadata. For a Skill with tools, create the body here first, then write plugin.json, scripts/*.js, or references/*.md one at a time with edit_skill_resource.<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task requires prior navigation or multiple steps, call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow first.<br>Required timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=skill: Show the status of Skill creation, resource edits, updates, or deletion.<br>- Return a manage_skill-style result with status fields such as action/name/saved or updated/deleted.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 必填。Skill 脚本硬超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 | 必填。Skill 脚本超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Required. Hard timeout for the Skill script, in seconds. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds. | Required. Skill script timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. |
| param `name` | `zh-CN` | skill 标准名，稳定英文小写 id，只用 a-z、0-9 和连字符，例如 bilibili-video-search | 必填。Skill 标准英文小写 id（仅含 a-z、0-9 和连字符，如 bilibili-video-search）。 |
| param `name` | `en` | Standard Skill name: a stable lowercase English ID containing only a-z, 0-9, and hyphens, such as bilibili-video-search. | Required lowercase English Skill ID (a-z, 0-9, and hyphens only, e.g. bilibili-video-search). |
| param `displayName` | `zh-CN` | 中文显示名，例如 B站视频搜索。创建中文技能时必须填写；不要只把中文放在 SKILL.md 标题里。 | 中文显示名（创建中文技能时必须填写，不要只写在正文标题中）。 |
| param `displayName` | `en` | Localized display name, such as Bilibili video search. For Chinese skills, supply the Chinese displayName explicitly rather than putting it only in the SKILL.md heading. | Localized display name (supply explicitly for Chinese skills rather than only in the Markdown heading). |
| param `description` | `zh-CN` | 一句话描述 skill 的用途 | 必填。一句话描述 Skill 的用途与触发场景。 |
| param `description` | `en` | One-sentence description of the Skill's purpose. | Required one-sentence description of the Skill's purpose and trigger scenario. |
| param `scope` | `zh-CN` | resident=常驻技能，用于系统指令调整、长久记忆信息、个人偏好或交付标准；global=全局按需加载；site=站点技能 | 作用域：resident（常驻注入系统上下文）、global（按需加载）或 site（按域名/URL 激活）。 |
| param `scope` | `en` | resident: always-loaded guidance for system instructions, persistent knowledge, preferences, or delivery standards; global: loaded on demand; site: site-specific skill. | Scope: resident (always injected), global (loaded on demand), or site (activated by domain/URL). |
| param `domains` | `zh-CN` | site skill 适用域名 | scope=site 的适用域名数组。 |
| param `domains` | `en` | Domains where the site skill applies. | Domains array for scope=site. |
| param `urlPatterns` | `zh-CN` | site skill URL/path 匹配 | scope=site 的 URL/path 匹配数组。 |
| param `urlPatterns` | `en` | URL/path patterns matched by the site skill. | URL/path patterns array for scope=site. |
| param `excludePatterns` | `zh-CN` | site skill URL/path 排除 | scope=site 的 URL/path 排除数组。 |
| param `excludePatterns` | `en` | URL/path patterns excluded from the site skill. | Excluded URL/path patterns array for scope=site. |
| param `content` | `zh-CN` | 完整 Skill Markdown 正文。写过程知识、前置导航、页面状态判断、失败回退；如有 Skill 工具，再写清何时调用。该字段可能很长，必须在 name/displayName/description/scope/domains/urlPatterns/excludePatterns 之后最后填写。 | 必填。完整 Skill Markdown 正文（写清流程、前置导航、状态校验、工具调用时机与失败回退）。该字段可能很长，必须在元信息字段之后最后填写。 |
| param `content` | `en` | Complete Skill Markdown: procedures, prerequisite navigation, page-state checks, recovery steps, and when to call any Skill tools. This may be long; supply it last, after name/displayName/description/scope/domains/urlPatterns/excludePatterns. | Required complete Skill Markdown body (workflow, prerequisite navigation, state checks, when to call tools, and fallbacks). This field may be long; supply it last after metadata fields. |

### `qingzhou_skill_author__edit_skill_resource`

| 字段 | 语言 | 修改前 (Old) | 修改后 (New) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **description** | `zh-CN` | 新增、整体替换或编辑某个 skill 的单个资源文件。一次只改一个资源、一次只做一个 action，所有参数都是顶层字段；用于 plugin.json、scripts/*.js、references/*.md 等资源，避免把多个资源或多个 edits 塞进 nested 参数。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需要前置导航、多步流程或页面上下文，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整过程知识；Skill 脚本只封装稳定可执行步骤，不替代 SKILL.md。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）让模型主动判断本次 Skill 脚本执行上限；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=skill: 展示创建、资源编辑、更新、删除 skill 的专用状态。<br>- return manage_skill 风格结果对象，包含 action/name/saved 或 updated/deleted 等状态字段。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 | 新增、整体覆盖（set）、局部替换（replace）、首尾追加（prepend/append）、重命名（rename）或删除（delete）Skill 的单个资源文件（plugin.json、scripts/*.js、references/*.md）。每次调用只编辑一个资源、执行一个 action。<br>来源扩展: 技能工作台<br>分类: 系统<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>若任务需前置导航或多步流程，先调用 load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) 读取完整 SOP。<br>必须传入 timeout（秒）：<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。<br>返回格式契约：该工具配置了 UI renderer，脚本最终 return 必须匹配以下 JSON 形状，不要把主要数据包在 result 字段里。<br>- renderer=skill: 展示创建、资源编辑、更新、删除 skill 的专用状态。<br>- return manage_skill 风格结果对象，包含 action/name/saved 或 updated/deleted 等状态字段。<br>- 不要 return 大段 raw JSON 字符串；需要调试信息放 trace 或少量 debug 字段。 |
| **description** | `en` | Add, replace, or edit one Skill resource file. Use one action per call, with all arguments at the top level. Applies to plugin.json, scripts/*.js, references/*.md, and similar resources. Do not pack multiple resources or edits into nested arguments.<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task needs prior navigation, multiple steps, or page context, first call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow. Skill scripts provide stable executable steps; they do not replace SKILL.md.<br>You must supply timeout in seconds after deciding how long this Skill script may run. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=skill: Show the status of Skill creation, resource edits, updates, or deletion.<br>- Return a manage_skill-style result with status fields such as action/name/saved or updated/deleted.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. | Create, overwrite (set), replace text in (replace), prepend/append, rename, or delete a single Skill resource file (plugin.json, scripts/*.js, references/*.md). Edit one resource with one action per call.<br>Source Skill: Skill workbench<br>Category: System<br>Skill: skill-workbench<br>If the task requires prior navigation or multiple steps, call load_skill({name:"skill-workbench"}) for the full workflow first.<br>Required timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration.<br>Return contract: this tool has a UI renderer. The script's final return value must match the following JSON shape. Do not wrap the main data in a result field.<br>- renderer=skill: Show the status of Skill creation, resource edits, updates, or deletion.<br>- Return a manage_skill-style result with status fields such as action/name/saved or updated/deleted.<br>- Do not return large raw JSON strings. Put diagnostics in trace or a few small debug fields. |
| param `timeout` | `zh-CN` | 必填。Skill 脚本硬超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 | 必填。Skill 脚本超时，单位秒；<= 0 表示无限等待，> 0 表示最多等待对应秒数。 |
| param `timeout` | `en` | Required. Hard timeout for the Skill script, in seconds. A value <=0 means no time limit; a positive value limits execution to that many seconds. | Required. Skill script timeout in seconds: <= 0 means no time limit; > 0 sets the maximum duration. |
| param `name` | `zh-CN` | 要编辑资源的 skill 名称 | 必填。目标 Skill 名称。 |
| param `name` | `en` | Name of the Skill whose resource will be edited. | Required target Skill name. |
| param `path` | `zh-CN` | 资源路径，如 plugin.json、scripts/collect.js 或 references/checklist.md。必须先填写，便于用户先看到要写哪个文件。 | 必填。资源相对路径（如 plugin.json、scripts/collect.js、references/checklist.md；必须先填写）。 |
| param `path` | `en` | Resource path, such as plugin.json, scripts/collect.js, or references/checklist.md. Supply this first so the user can see which file will be written. | Required relative resource path (e.g. plugin.json, scripts/collect.js, references/checklist.md; supply before content). |
| param `action` | `zh-CN` | 资源编辑动作。set=新增或整体替换；replace=一次精确文本替换；append/prepend=追加/前置文本；rename=重命名；delete=删除。 | 必填。编辑动作：set（新增或全量覆盖）、replace（单次精确替换）、append/prepend（首尾追加）、rename（重命名）、delete（删除）。 |
| param `action` | `en` | Resource edit action. set: add or fully replace; replace: one exact text replacement; append/prepend: add text at the end/start; rename: rename; delete: remove. | Required edit action: set (create or overwrite), replace (single exact replacement), append/prepend (add at end/start), rename, or delete. |
| param `newPath` | `zh-CN` | rename 时的新资源路径 | rename 时的新资源路径。 |
| param `newPath` | `en` | New resource path for rename. | New resource path for rename. |
| param `type` | `zh-CN` | set/rename 时可更新资源类型，如 json/script/markdown/asset | set/rename 可选资源类型（json/script/markdown/asset）。 |
| param `type` | `en` | Optional resource type for set/rename, such as json/script/markdown/asset. | Optional resource type for set/rename (json/script/markdown/asset). |
| param `title` | `zh-CN` | set/rename 时可更新显示标题 | set/rename 可选显示标题。 |
| param `title` | `en` | Optional display title for set/rename. | Optional display title for set/rename. |
| param `description` | `zh-CN` | set/rename 时可更新用途说明 | set/rename 可选用途说明。 |
| param `description` | `en` | Optional purpose description for set/rename. | Optional purpose description for set/rename. |
| param `oldText` | `zh-CN` | replace 时的旧文本；从刚读取的资源 content 中原样复制最小但唯一的连续片段 | replace 必填的旧文本片段（从刚读取的资源 content 中原样复制最小唯一连续片段）。 |
| param `oldText` | `en` | Old text for replace. Copy the smallest unique contiguous excerpt verbatim from the resource just read. | Required old text for replace (copy the smallest unique contiguous excerpt verbatim from the freshly read resource). |
| param `newText` | `zh-CN` | replace 时的新文本 | replace 必填的新文本片段。 |
| param `newText` | `en` | Replacement text for replace. | Required replacement text for replace. |
| param `appendText` | `zh-CN` | append 时追加到资源末尾的文本 | append 追加到末尾的文本。 |
| param `appendText` | `en` | Text to append to the resource for append. | Text to append for append. |
| param `prependText` | `zh-CN` | prepend 时添加到资源开头的文本 | prepend 添加到开头的文本。 |
| param `prependText` | `en` | Text to prepend to the resource for prepend. | Text to prepend for prepend. |
| param `content` | `zh-CN` | set 时的完整资源内容，文本直接保存，JSON 对象或数组序列化保存。path=plugin.json 时，设置 UI 的 settings.fields[].type 只支持 text\|secret\|url\|boolean\|number\|select，width 只支持 full\|half，分组使用 settings.layout.sections；通用属性为 label/description/placeholder/required/default，number 使用 min/max/step，select 使用 options，text 使用 pattern，secret 使用 reenterWhen。完整约束见已加载 skill-workbench 的“Skill 设置 DSL 与 inner 网络”。script 资源必须是轻舟 page/inner 浏览器 JS，推荐 async () => { ... } 源码形态。该字段可能很长，必须放在 path/type/title/description 之后最后填写；一次调用只改一个资源。 | set 时的完整资源内容（文本或可序列化 JSON）。path=plugin.json 时，settings.fields[].type 只支持 text\|secret\|url\|boolean\|number\|select，width 只支持 full\|half，分组用 settings.layout.sections（完整约束见已加载 skill-workbench）；script 资源须为浏览器 JS（推荐单个 async () => { ... } 表达式）。一次调用只改一个资源，先填 path/type/title/description，最后填写本字段。 |
| param `content` | `en` | Full resource content for set. Text is saved directly; JSON objects/arrays are serialized. For path=plugin.json: settings.fields[].type supports text\|secret\|url\|boolean\|number\|select; width supports full\|half; group fields with settings.layout.sections. Common attributes are label/description/placeholder/required/default; number uses min/max/step, select uses options, text uses pattern, and secret uses reenterWhen. See “Settings DSL and inner-runtime networking” in the loaded skill-workbench for the full contract. Scripts must be browser JavaScript for qz's page/inner runtime, preferably one async () => { ... } expression. Supply this long field last, after path/type/title/description. Edit one resource per call. | Full resource content for set (string or serializable JSON). For path=plugin.json, settings.fields[].type supports text\|secret\|url\|boolean\|number\|select, width supports full\|half, and sections use settings.layout.sections (see the loaded skill-workbench for the full contract); scripts must be browser JS (prefer a single async () => { ... } expression). Edit one resource per call, supplying path/type/title/description first and this field last. |

## 6. 待拍板清单 (Open Decisions for Owner Review)

以下事项在审阅代码与测试时发现，需要 Owner 拍板确认：

1. **多语言包 (`src/capabilities/i18n/locales/<lang>/media-tools.json`) 中的 6 个参数翻译覆写**：
   - 现象：`ja`、`ko`、`zh-TW`、`de`、`es`、`fr`、`pt-BR`、`ru`、`ar`、`hi` 语言包中的 `media-tools.json` 包含了 `screenshotMaxWidth`、`screenshotMaxHeight`、`collectImagesMaxWidth`、`collectImagesMaxHeight`、`collectImagesMaxBytes`、`readImageMaxBytes` 共 6 个参数的本地化翻译。当用户在非中英界面下运行扩展时，`translateMessage` 会优先返回语言包中的当地语言文案而非英文 fallback。
   - 本次处理：保持这 6 个键的中英文及 10 个语言包文案不变，确保所有语言包校验与测试 100% 绿灯。
   - 待拍板：是否需要在后续将 `media-tools.json` 从 UI 语言包中移除，使所有非 `zh-CN` 界面的模型工具 schema 统一使用英文？

2. **内置按需技能正文 (`builtinContent`) 是否做进一步压缩**：
   - 现象：`qingzhou-documents`、`qingzhou-management`、`qingzhou-mcp`、`skill-workbench`、`site-skill-author` 的 `SKILL.md` 正文仅在模型调用 `load_skill` 时按需进入上下文，不在默认 System Prompt 或常驻工具列表中。同时现有单测（如 `test-builtin-plugin-creator-skill.mjs`、`test-manage-settings-tool.mjs`）对这些技能正文的章节标题与关键句有大量精确正则断言。
   - 本次处理：本次聚焦于全面打磨每轮必带的 `SYSTEM_PROMPT`（双语）与全部 64+9 个工具的 `description` / `parameters`，仅对 `load_skill` 返回的 `runtimeGuidance` 做了精简，未改动 5 个内置技能的 `SKILL.md` 正文。
   - 待拍板：是否需要对 `site-skill-author`（约 550 行）和 `skill-workbench`（约 275 行）的 `SKILL.md` 正文也做一轮大幅瘦身并同步更新对应单测？

3. **`run_script` description 中的完整推荐代码骨架（18 行）去留**：
   - 现象：`run_script` 的工具描述末尾附带了一段 18 行的 `async () => { ... }` 推荐骨架代码（占约 600 字符），且 `tests/unit/test-run-script-source.mjs` 对该代码骨架有精确匹配断言。
   - 本次处理：保留了该推荐代码骨架，将 `run_script` 描述前半部分的散文说明压缩了约 32%。
   - 待拍板：对于 DeepSeek flash / v4-pro 级别模型，是否仍需在 `run_script` 的 tool description 中常驻保留这 18 行代码模板，还是缩短为 3–4 行最简骨架？

4. **`scroll_page` 的 `pixels` 参数与 `direction: "top" | "bottom"` 行为说明**：
   - 现象：核查 `src/content/content-script.ts` 发现，当 `direction` 为 `"top"` 或 `"bottom"` 时，代码直接使用 `±document.documentElement.scrollHeight`，`pixels` 参数仅在 `"up"` / `"down"` 时生效；且传 `target` 时会忽略 `direction` 和 `pixels`，直接将该元素 `scrollIntoView({ block: "center" })`。
   - 本次处理：已在 `scroll_page` 的 description 与 `pixels` 参数说明中明确写出「传 target 时将该元素滚入视口中央；不传 target 时按 direction 和 pixels（仅 up/down 生效）滚动窗口」。请确认该表述是否符合预期。
